### [1] [Selective Prediction and Uncertainty-Aware Referral for Pap Smear Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17545)
*Nisreen Albzour,Sarah S. Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在Herlev宫颈细胞学数据集上研究选择性预测与不确定性驱动的转诊，比较两个轻量级Transformer（Swin-Tiny、TinyViT-5M）与二者的软投票集成。集成虽未提升准确率或macro-F1，但将AURC减半（0.0022 vs. 0.0045，降低51.8%），并把零错误覆盖率从18.3%提升到72.8%；然而集成的绝对校准更差（ECE 0.0339 vs. 0.0247）、假阴性更多（14 vs. 10），从而区分了“对预测可信度排序的能力”与“置信度数值本身的准确性”这两个常被混淆的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈细胞学深度学习模型几乎总被评估为每个预测都必须被处理，但部署在细胞病理学家旁的筛查系统并非必须对每张玻片做分类——它可以转诊最不确定的病例。因此评估这类系统不仅要问它多常正确，还要问其置信度是否能把错误排序到底部。本文旨在研究选择性预测与不确定性感知转诊，并厘清风险-覆盖排序能力与置信度数值校准之间的区别。

Method: 在Herlev巴氏涂片数据集上采用二分类“正常-异常”任务。两个轻量级Transformer骨干网络（Swin-Tiny、TinyViT-5M）从ImageNet预训练权重微调，使用加权随机采样；通过在后留校准子集上拟合的post-hoc温度缩放进行校准；并与两模型的软投票集成对比。报告判别性能（准确率、macro-F1）以及期望校准误差（ECE），主要终点为风险-覆盖曲线下面积（AURC）。统计显著性检验用于比较两配置。

Result: 两种配置在准确率与macro-F1上无统计学显著差异。集成将AURC减半（0.0022 vs. 0.0045，降低51.8%，五个折中均更低），并把池化测试预测实现零错误的覆盖率从18.3%扩展到72.8%。但集成在绝对意义上校准更差（ECE 0.0339 vs. 0.0247），且产生更多假阴性（14 vs. 10）。

Conclusion: 这些结果分离了两个常被混淆的性质：对预测按可信度排序的能力，与置信度数值本身的准确性。集成改善了前者而恶化了后者；前者直接决定观测到的风险-覆盖权衡，后者则决定所报告置信度数值的可解释性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for cervical cytology are almost always evaluated as if every prediction must be acted upon, yet a screening system deployed alongside a cytopathologist need not classify every slide: it can defer the cases it is least certain about. Evaluating such a system requires asking not only how often it is correct, but whether its confidence ranks its errors to the bottom. This paper studies selective prediction and uncertainty-aware referral on the Herlev Pap smear dataset under a binary Normal-versus-Abnormal formulation. Two lightweight transformer backbones (Swin-Tiny, TinyViT-5M) are fine-tuned on Herlev from ImageNet-pretrained weights with weighted random sampling, calibrated by post-hoc temperature scaling fit on a held-out calibration subset, and compared against a soft-voting ensemble of both models. Discrimination is reported alongside expected calibration error (ECE) and, as the primary endpoint, the area under the risk-coverage curve (AURC). No statistically significant difference was detected between the two configurations in accuracy or macro-F1, yet the ensemble halves AURC (0.0022 vs. 0.0045, a 51.8% reduction, lower in all five folds) and extends the coverage at which zero errors are made from 18.3% to 72.8% of the pooled test predictions. The same ensemble is nonetheless worse calibrated in absolute terms (ECE 0.0339 vs. 0.0247) and produces more false negatives (14 vs. 10). These results separate two properties that are frequently conflated: the ability to rank predictions by trustworthiness, and the accuracy of the confidence values themselves. Ensembling improves the former while degrading the latter, and the former directly governs the observed risk-coverage tradeoff, whereas the latter governs the interpretation of the reported confidence values.

</details>


### [2] [A Heisenberg Lift Descriptor for Order Sensitive Online Handwriting Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17565)
*Hassan Ugail,Newton Howard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Heisenberg群的、对笔画书写顺序敏感的轻量特征增强方法，以补足传统欧氏形状描述子"顺序盲"的缺陷；只需附加一个参数自由的"末端带符号面积"标量即可稳定提升在线手写识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线手写识别系统常用固定长度的欧氏形状描述子刻画笔迹的空间轮廓，这类表示对笔画产生的先后顺序不敏感：同一平面区域被相反方向书写的两个笔画对它不可区分，但笔顺方向（如环的取向、笔画顺序）可能携带决定性类别信息。

Method: 引入Heisenberg提升框架，构建紧凑、可解释、对顺序敏感的在线笔迹特征增强。最简实例为"末端带符号面积"：一个无参数标量，以极小计算代价附加到现有欧氏描述子上。进一步提出由非交换Heisenberg群细分格式导出的15维扩展，并设置维度匹配的统计对照以排除特征数增加带来的虚假增益。

Result: 在两个标准在线手写基准上，仅增加一个标量即可一致提升分类器相对欧氏基线的准确率。在最难区分的字符对"o"与"y"上，仅带符号面积即实现完美分离，而欧氏基线无法区分。在叠加坐标噪声（笔迹数据中实用的退化情形）下增益更大；15维扩展在噪声与环结构场景下提供额外提升，统计对照证实所有增益源于几何信息而非特征数量膨胀。

Conclusion: 所提描述子轻量、闭式且可直接解释，为在线手写及文档轨迹分类流程中笔顺方向具有判别信息的情形提供了一种实用的增强方案。

Abstract: Online handwriting recognition systems typically represent pen trajectories through fixed-length Euclidean shape descriptors that capture the spatial outline of each stroke, but are insensitive to the order in which that outline is produced. Two strokes that trace the same region of the plane in opposite directions are indistinguishable to any such order-blind representation, yet their traversal directions may carry decisive class information in characters where loop orientation and stroke sequencing matter. This paper introduces a Heisenberg-lift framework that addresses this gap through a compact, interpretable, order-sensitive augmentation for online pen-trajectory features. The simplest instance is the terminal signed area, a single parameter-free scalar appended to an existing Euclidean descriptor at negligible computational cost. Evaluated on two standard online handwriting benchmarks, this one-scalar addition, consistently raises classifier accuracy over the Euclidean baseline. On the hardest character pair in our study, the letters o and y, the signed area alone achieves perfect separation while the Euclidean baseline falls short. The advantage grows further under additive coordinate noise, a practically relevant degradation in pen-trajectory data. A richer fifteen-dimensional extension, derived from a noncommutative Heisenberg-group subdivision scheme, provides additional gains in noisy and loop-structured conditions. Dimension-matched statistical controls confirm that all improvements reflect geometric information rather than feature-count inflation. The resulting descriptor is lightweight, closed-form, and directly interpretable, making it a practical augmentation for online handwriting and related document-trajectory classification pipelines in which the direction of stroke execution carries discriminative information.

</details>


### [3] [Adaptive Interpolatory Curve Subdivision with Learned Local Angles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17566)
*Hassan Ugail,Newton Howard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应局部角度插值细分方法，通过为每条边学习一个插入角度（边级预测器），在保持插值结构不变的前提下，在欧氏平面、二维球面和庞加莱盘三种几何上显著优于固定张力基线和向心Catmull-Rom。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典四点/六点插值细分方案虽能保证曲线通过设计控制点，但其行为由单一全局张力参数控制，难以同时适应平坦区域、急转弯和变化多样的局部几何。作者希望在保持插值结构这一结构性特征（而非依赖训练权重）的同时，赋予每个新顶点局部自适应能力。

Method: 提出自适应局部角度公式：保持插值结构不变，学习如何插入每个新顶点。设计一个紧凑的边级预测器，为每条边分配一个插入角度；原始顶点在每次细分层级中完全原样复制，因此插值是算子的结构属性，不依赖于训练权重。同一预测器配合几何特定的测地原语，分别应用于欧氏平面、二维球面和庞加莱盘三种几何空间。

Result: 在匹配密度的评估协议下，该方法将最近邻误差比最佳验证调参的固定张力基线降低5至17倍，在欧氏情形下比向心Catmull-Rom降低约1.8倍；同时显著降低弯曲能量和切线粗糙度，并与分别针对每种几何训练的模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该自适应局部角度插值细分方法在多个几何空间上均实现了明显优于传统固定张力方案与向心Catmull-Rom的性能，且插值性质作为结构属性被保证，展现出良好的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Curve subdivision is pivotal in computer graphics for generating smooth geometric objects from control polygons. Interpolatory subdivision is especially attractive because the refined curve is guaranteed to pass through the designer's control points. Classical four-point and six-point schemes preserve this property, but their behaviour is governed by a single global tension parameter, limiting their ability to adapt across flat regions, sharp turns and varying local geometries. We introduce an adaptive local-angle formulation that keeps the interpolatory structure intact while learning how each new vertex should be inserted. A compact edge-wise predictor assigns one insertion angle per edge, while the original vertices are copied exactly at every refinement level. Interpolation is therefore a structural property of the operator and does not depend on the trained weights. The same predictor is used with geometry-specific geodesic primitives on the Euclidean plane, the two-sphere and the Poincaré disk. Under a matched-density evaluation protocol, the method reduces nearest-neighbour error by factors of five to seventeen over the best validation-tuned fixed-tension baseline, and by about 1.8 over centripetal Catmull-Rom in the Euclidean case. It also substantially reduces bending energy and tangent roughness, while remaining competitive with separately trained per-geometry models.

</details>


### [4] [DualCount: Structurally Consistent Density and Point Modeling for Zero-Shot Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17613)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实例感知的双解码器框架，将密度估计建模为对潜在实例集合的结构化质量分配问题，并通过逐实例质量守恒与质心对齐两个几何约束，缓解零样本物体计数中的空间模糊与背景泄漏，在FSC-147、PUCPR+、CARPK上取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本物体计数方法依赖密度回归或检测式实例预测，其中密度类模型因质量分配缺乏约束，易出现空间模糊与背景泄漏，导致表示碎片化或偏向局部，在复杂场景下计数误差增大。作者希望从结构上耦合密度表示与点表示，引入实例级几何一致性来改进质量分配。

Method: 提出实例感知的双解码器框架，将密度估计视为对潜在实例集合的结构化质量分配问题：预测的实例中心对密度图进行软性的逐实例分解，并施加两个几何约束——(1)逐实例质量守恒（每个物体约贡献一个单位的密度质量）；(2)质心对齐（每个密度分量聚集于其对应预测中心周围）。以此引入实例级几何一致性，提升质量分配精度。

Result: 在FSC-147、PUCPR+和CARPK三个基准上的大量实验表明，该方法持续降低计数误差，并在零样本物体计数任务上取得新的最优（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过将密度估计重构为结构化质量分配并施加实例级几何约束，能够有效缓解密度类计数模型的碎片化与背景泄漏问题，从而在零样本物体计数上实现更低的计数误差与领先性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot object counting aims to estimate the number of objects specified by a text query without category-specific training. Recent approaches primarily rely on density regression or detection-style instance prediction. While effective, density-based models often suffer from spatial ambiguity and background leakage due to weakly regulated mass allocation, leading to fragmented or part-biased representations that increase counting error in complex scenes. In this work, we propose an instance-aware dual-decoder framework that structurally couples density and point representations for zero-shot object counting. Instead of treating density estimation as independent pixel-wise regression, we interpret it as a structured mass allocation problem over a latent set of object instances. Predicted instance centers induce a soft instance-wise decomposition of the density map, upon which we enforce two geometric constraints: (1) per-instance mass conservation, ensuring each object contributes approximately one unit of density mass, and (2) center-of-mass alignment, encouraging each density component to concentrate around its corresponding predicted center. These constraints introduce instance-level geometric consistency and lead to more accurate mass allocation, thereby reducing counting error. Extensive experiments on FSC-147, PUCPR+, and CARPK show that our approach consistently reduces counting error and establishes new state-of-the-art performance in zero-shot object counting.

</details>


### [5] [Geometry-Driven Shadow Harmonisation for Composited Faces: A Multiplicative, Albedo-Preserving Relighting Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17740)
*Vijesh KP*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保守的、由几何驱动的形态阴影注入方法，用于修复换脸/合成人脸中"几何对齐但光度不一致"的问题。该方法仅通过单通道均匀地对线性RGB乘以一个范围在[g_min,1]的增益场来压暗面部，从而在不改变色度、肤色与纹理的前提下，利用3D人脸代理生成形态阴影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换脸与合成流程常产生几何对齐但光度不合理的人脸：目标脸带有平坦的近正面影棚光照，而宿主身体与背景带有方向性场景光。现有方案（颜色迁移、神经重光照、逆渲染）可能改变身份、肤色和纹理，因此需要一种不重新绘制人脸、仅通过压暗来注入阴影的保守替代方案。

Method: 从不重绘人脸，而是从栅格化的3D人脸代理估计逐像素增益场 g∈[g_min,1]，并以通道均匀方式乘到线性RGB上，使算子只能压暗、不能偏移色度。具体流程：将密集地标网格栅格化为深度缓冲，由此推导表面法线、空腔项（cavity term）和屏幕空间投射阴影；主光方向由宿主侧线索（身体、背景、发丝高光）估计，面部线索被降权以避免恢复成目标脸自身光照；阴影强度不匹配宿主，而由三参数传递函数 (τ, σ, g_min) 设定。阴影场先除以皮肤区域第75百分位归一化，再进行门控、缩放、裁剪、平滑，并在带羽化的皮肤门控人脸掩码内重新裁剪。

Result: 在解析人脸高度场上，默认参数 (τ, σ, g_min)=(0.90, 0.45, 0.82) 修改了56.5%的人脸像素，平均增益0.938（被修改像素上为0.890），并将3.4%的像素压到下限。色相不变性是该算子的必然推论。论文对传递函数进行了闭式分析并消融其参数。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种只压暗、不改色度的保守阴影注入方式，能保持身份、肤色与纹理不变。同时论文讨论了单调仅压暗公式的失效模式，包括对非平坦目标脸的双重阴影问题。

Abstract: Face swapping and face compositing pipelines routinely produce a face that is geometrically well aligned but photometrically implausible: the donor face carries flat, near-frontal studio illumination while the host body and background carry directional scene light. Most existing remedies re-synthesise the face through colour transfer, neural relighting, or inverse rendering, and therefore risk altering identity, skin tone, and texture. We present a conservative alternative: geometry-driven form-shadow injection. The pipeline never repaints the face. It estimates a per-pixel gain field $g\in[g_{\min},1]$ from a rasterised 3D face proxy and multiplies it channel-uniformly onto linear RGB, so the operator can only darken and cannot shift chromaticity. A dense landmark mesh is rasterised into a depth buffer, from which we derive surface normals, a cavity term, and screen-space cast shadows. Key-light direction is estimated from host-side cues (body, background, hair halo); on-face cues are downweighted because they recover the donor's lighting. Shadow magnitude is not matched to the host: it is set by a three-parameter transfer $(τ,σ,g_{\min})$. The shading field is divided by its 75th percentile over skin, then gated, scaled, clamped, smoothed, and re-clipped inside a feathered, skin-gated face mask. On an analytic face heightfield, the default $(τ,σ,g_{\min})=(0.90,0.45,0.82)$ modifies 56.5% of face pixels with mean gain 0.938 (0.890 on modified pixels) and drives 3.4% of pixels to the floor. Hue invariance is a corollary of the operator. We analyse the transfer in closed form, ablate its parameters, and discuss failure modes of a monotone, darkening-only formulation, including double-shadowing of non-flat donors.

</details>


### [6] [How to make effective use of domain experts for image classification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17749)
*Dieu-Donné Fangnon,Diane Lingrand,Aurélie Liard,Marco Corneli,Antoine Pasqualini,Frédéric Precioso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于专家属性知识（支持数值、区间、二元、类别等多种编码格式）的图像分类方法：先微调特征提取器预测属性，再学习分类头；并在三个数据集上验证分类提升，同时提出对可能误分类样本的自动筛选以引入专家修正属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来研究者期望将领域专家知识融入图像分类模型。现有方法如概念瓶颈模型（CBM）采用概念的二元编码，其后继变体概念嵌入模型（CEM）仅用两个向量嵌入每个概念，过于简化。而真实场景中专家知识通常以概念形式组织，且每个概念由多种属性刻画，属性又可能以数值、数值区间、二元或类别等不同格式编码，现有方法难以同时兼容这些多样的编码方式。

Method: 首先微调一个图像特征提取器，用于预测代表下游目标类别的属性（这些属性由专家以数值、区间、二元、类别等多种格式指定）；随后基于这些多格式属性学习一个分类头，用于对目标对象进行分类。此外，提出对可能误分类样本的自动筛选步骤，针对这些样本请专家修改预测属性，以进一步提升分类性能。

Result: 实验在三个数据集上显示该方法能够提升分类性能：Kaggle 鱼类数据集、AWA2，以及一个更具挑战性的新木材木炭（wood charcoal）数据集。同时，通过自动筛选潜在误分类样本并由专家修正属性的第二步流程，进一步改进分类效果。

Conclusion: 通过让模型处理专家提供的多种编码格式的属性，并辅以针对误分类样本的专家修正机制，能够比二元概念瓶颈模型及其嵌入变体更贴合真实领域知识，从而有效提升图像分类性能。

Abstract: A lot of expectations have been put for years on integrating domain expert knowledge in image classification models. Several approaches have been explored, Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) opened up a new avenue of research leading to many variants, and more recently to Concept-based Embedding Models (CEMs). CBM consider binary encoding of each concept, while CEM expands this idea by embedding each concept through two vectors. However in real-life scenarii, domain experts' knowledge is usually organized in concepts determined by various attributes, each attribute encoded either with numerical values, or range of values, or binary values, or categorical values. In this work, we first finetune an image feature extractor for classifying attributes representing the downstream object classes, where the class attributes have been specified by experts under various encoding formats. A classification head is then learnt from these various attributes to categorize target objects. We experimentally show that it improves the classification for three datasets: Kaggle fish dataset, AWA2 and a more challenging new wood charcoal dataset. We then propose an automatic selection of potential missclassified data. In this second step, experts are asked for those data to eventually modify the predicted attributes in order to improve the classification.

</details>


### [7] [Beyond Performance Metrics: Uncertainty Mapping of Label Ambiguity in Fazekas Score Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17753)
*Susanne Schmid,Johanna Ospel,Richard Frayne,Roberto Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"不确定性映射"框架，用于分析脑室周围Fazekas评分预测模型的行为。该方法将模型预测的认知不确定性与其在特征表示中的空间位置相关联，从而识别类别边界过渡区、潜在的标签分歧（尤其是不确定性低但被误分类的病例），并揭示损失函数选择对类分离和不确定性分布的影响。尽管最佳模型的MCC达0.70，但不同数据划分和损失函数下性能波动，传统指标难以充分解释模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类模型的参考标签并不总是确定的，这种不确定性（如Fazekas评分受评分者间差异性影响）会干扰性能指标的解读。传统指标（如MCC）无法充分揭示模型能力及标签歧义对评估的影响，亟需一种超越常规指标的分析方法，以理解模型在高歧义区域的预测行为并发现标签分歧。

Method: 作者提出"不确定性映射"框架，将模型的认知不确定性视为可映射到学习到的特征表示空间中的量，而非孤立的标量值。具体做法是：将每个病例的预测不确定性与其特征表示位置关联，划分类别边界过渡区以识别歧义病例和易误分类区域；同时结合专家复核，对低不确定性却误分类的病例进行标签一致性审查。此外，对比不同损失函数下的类分离程度与不确定性分布，以评估损失函数选择的影响。

Result: 最佳Fazekas评分预测模型的MCC为0.70，但性能随数据划分和损失函数变化而波动。不确定性映射揭示了类别边界过渡区的歧义病例和更易发生的误分类，并识别出与原始参考评分不一致的低不确定性误分类病例（经专家复核确认）。不同损失函数导致不同的不确定性剖面：部分模型表现出更清晰的类分离，且在歧义区域的不确定性更局部化。

Conclusion: 不确定性映射能够支持Fazekas评分预测的模型解释与针对性数据集审查，尤其在参考标签受歧义或评分者间差异性影响时具有价值。该方法将模型行为与类分离及潜在的模型-标签分歧关联起来，为超越传统指标的模型评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Reference labels used to train medical image classification models are not always as certain as they may appear, and this uncertainty has implications on performance metrics. In this study, we propose a framework to analyze model performance for periventricular Fazekas score prediction that goes beyond conventional metrics. The Fazekas score is an ordinal visual rating scale used to assess the severity of white matter hyperintensities and is known to be affected by inter-rater variability. While the best Fazekas score prediction model achieved a Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) of 0.70, performance varied across data splits and loss functions, making interpretation of model capabilities difficult.
  Rather than interpreting epistemic uncertainty of a model's prediction as an isolated scalar value, our approach of uncertainty mapping relates uncertainty to its position within the learned feature representation. This highlights regions of class-boundary transitions where cases appear more ambiguous and misclassifications are more likely. It also identifies potential label disagreement, including low-uncertainty misclassified cases that expert review found to be inconsistent with the original reference Fazekas score. Therefore, uncertainty mapping allows model behaviour to be examined in relation to class separation and potential model-label disagreement.
  Loss function choice also influenced the uncertainty profile, with some models showing clearer class separation and more localized uncertainty in ambiguous regions than others. These findings suggest that uncertainty mapping for Fazekas score predictions can support model interpretation and targeted dataset review when reference labels are affected by ambiguity/ inter-rater variability.

</details>


### [8] [Not All Patches Are Equally Forgettable: Spatially Localized Domain Unlearning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17790)
*Akanksha Singh,Vinod K. Kurmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段、基于patch选择的视觉语言模型选择性域遗忘框架，通过估计patch级域敏感度并选择性衰减那些对遗忘域预测贡献大于语义效用的patch，从而在保留域性能提升最高3.8%的同时改善遗忘-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型即使不额外训练也具有较强跨域识别能力，但这种鲁棒性会保留不想要的域特定行为。问题根源在于域相关信息与语义信息在表示空间中纠缠，导致选择性域遗忘困难。现有方法多采用潜空间解耦及prompt/特征级干预，但未直接归因和衰减单个patch-token的贡献。作者认为与其统一抑制全表示，不如利用视觉Transformer的空间结构，定位并抑制对遗忘域预测贡献过大的patch区域。

Method: 提出两阶段patch选择性框架：第一阶段估计patch级域敏感度（识别哪些patch对遗忘域预测贡献大）；第二阶段选择性衰减那些"对遗忘域预测贡献强于其语义效用"的patch。整体思路是使遗忘过程依据不同视觉区域的域贡献来进行引导，而非统一抑制全部表示。

Result: 在Office-Home、Mini DomainNet和DomainNet上评估，相比先前方法取得更好的遗忘-保留权衡，保留域识别性能最高提升3.8%。在视觉重叠和未见域设置下的额外评估进一步表明其在分布偏移下具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 利用视觉Transformer的空间结构进行patch级选择性衰减，比统一抑制全表示更有效地实现选择性域遗忘，在改善遗忘效果的同时保护甚至提升语义识别能力，并在分布偏移场景下保持更强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong cross-domain recognition performance even without additional training. However, this robustness can also preserve undesirable domain-specific behavior, as domain-related and semantic information often remain entangled within the learned representation space, making selective domain unlearning challenging. Existing approaches typically address this problem through latent-space disentanglement and prompt- or feature-level interventions, without directly attributing and attenuating individual patch-token contributions. However, here we suggest that rather than uniformly suppressing the full representation, it may be more effective to exploit the spatial structure of vision transformers to localize and suppress patch regions that contribute disproportionately to forget-domain prediction. Patches that strongly influence forget-domain prediction may not be equally important for semantic recognition, suggesting that forgetting should be guided according to the domain contribution of different visual regions. Specifically, we propose a two-stage patch-selective framework that first estimates patch-level domain sensitivity and then selectively attenuates patches whose contribution to forget-domain prediction is stronger than their semantic utility. We evaluate our framework on Office-Home, Mini DomainNet, and DomainNet. Experimental results demonstrate improved forgetting-retention tradeoffs compared to prior methods while improving retained-domain recognition by up to 3.8\%. Additional evaluations under visually overlapping and unseen-domain settings further demonstrate improved robustness under distribution shift.

</details>


### [9] [AgenTeeth: A Model-Agnostic Framework for Suppressing Hallucination in Frozen Vision-Language Models on Dental X-Rays via Tool Evidence Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17800)
*Ahmed Rafid,Fariya Ahmed,Rumman Adib,Mehedi Ahamed,Ajwad Abrar,Tareque Mohmud Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgenTeeth，一个模型无关、工具增强的框架，通过七个专用牙科视觉专家模型为冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）提供结构化证据，大幅提升其在全景牙科 X 光片上的牙齿定位与空间推理可靠性，无需微调底层 VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在全景牙科 X 光片上不可靠，倾向于依赖学到的解剖先验而非图像中的实际证据，尤其在牙齿定位和空间推理方面问题突出；且微调后的牙科 VLM 仍保留同样的空间偏差。因此需要一个无需微调、能强制模型基于图像证据回答的方案。

Method: 提出 AgenTeeth 框架：一个问题感知的编排器（orchestrator）根据问题选择七个专用牙科视觉专家模型中的相关工具；工具检测结果被映射为 FDI 牙齿编号或解剖区域，并以结构化发现和标注图像叠加层返回；随后进行一次新的综合（synthesis）调用，利用这些证据回答问题，整个过程不对底层 VLM 进行微调。

Result: 在 MMOral-OPG-Bench 上，AgenTeeth 使四个骨干 VLM 较基线提升 12.9–23.0 个百分点；最强配置在开放式 VQA 上达到 65.66%，而 OralGPT-Plus 为 45.35%；在同等规模下，冻结的 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 配合 AgenTeeth 达到 48.11%，超过基于同一骨干经监督微调和强化学习工具使用的 OralGPT-Plus。

Conclusion: AgenTeeth 证明通过工具增强并将专业视觉专家模型的结构化证据注入冻结 VLM，能够有效克服空间偏差、显著提升牙科 VQA 性能，且无需昂贵的微调。作者同时开源了框架、全部七个专家模型，以及一个用于全景片牙槽骨吸收检测的牙医标注数据集。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) remain largely unreliable on panoramic dental radiographs and can rely on learned anatomical priors rather than evidence in the image. This is particularly problematic for tooth localization and spatial reasoning, and fine-tuned dental VLMs can retain the same spatial biases. We present AgenTeeth, a model-agnostic, tool-augmented framework that grounds frozen VLMs using seven specialized dental vision experts. A question-aware orchestrator selects the relevant tools, whose detections are mapped to FDI tooth numbers or anatomical regions and returned as structured findings together with annotated image overlays. A fresh synthesis call then answers the question using this evidence, without fine-tuning the underlying VLM. On MMOral-OPG-Bench, AgenTeeth improves four backbone VLMs by 12.9-23.0 percentage points over their baselines. Our strongest configuration reaches 65.66% on open-ended VQA, compared with 45.35% for OralGPT-Plus. The advantage also holds at matched scale: a frozen Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct with AgenTeeth reaches 48.11%, exceeding OralGPT-Plus built on the same backbone after supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning for tool use. We release the framework, all seven expert models, and a dentist-annotated dataset for alveolar bone-loss detection in panoramic radiographs.

</details>


### [10] [Wind on Trees: Testing Physical Grounding in Dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17810)
*Weiying Chen,Edmond Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用物理参数化的形变先验（每个刚性部件一个受风驱动的阻尼谐振子，经可微 RK4 积分）替代 4D 高斯泼溅中的直接学习形变场，并在合成风动树木测试台上验证该先验的物理合理性，发现其在分布外（时间外推、未见风速）泛化更好，但物理参数恢复能力远弱于预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风动植被的单目重建严重欠约束：沿视线方向的运动不可观测、动态冠层缺乏可靠对应、场景几乎全动态缺乏静态参考，导致直接学习的形变场只是拟合光度一致性而非恢复真实运动。作者希望检验用物理先验替代学习形变场是否真正具有物理基础，而非仅仅拟合得好。

Method: 用物理参数化形变先验替代 4D 高斯泼溅中的直接形变场：每个刚性部件建模为受观测风驱动的阻尼谐振子，由可微 RK4 积分，仅以光度一致性监督训练。构建受控合成测试台：三棵程序化生成的树，骨架复杂度跨一个数量级，各部件固有频率由其自身几何决定、阻尼比为固定常数，二者均在训练中不可见。评估指标包括留出视角、时间外推、零样本迁移到未见风速，以及物理参数本身的恢复程度。

Result: 该先验在训练分布内视角上牺牲了外观保真度，但在训练窗口之外和训练风速之外外推显著更好。参数恢复远比表面看起来弱：频率恢复仅在三棵树中最稀疏的一棵上通过未训练的零对照，阻尼完全未被恢复。

Conclusion: 物理先验改善了分布外泛化（时间外推与未见风速），但代价是分布内保真度下降；同时物理参数恢复极其有限，提示当前物理先验的"物理合理性"需要谨慎审视，不能仅凭拟合表现推断其实际恢复了真实物理量。

Abstract: Monocular reconstruction of wind-driven vegetation is severely underconstrained: motion along the viewing direction is largely unobservable, a moving canopy offers few reliable correspondences, and nearly the entire scene is dynamic, providing little static reference. Directly-learned deformation fields in 4D Gaussian Splatting therefore optimize photometric consistency rather than recover the motion that produced it. We replace that field with a physically parameterized deformation prior: one damped harmonic oscillator per rigid part, driven by the observed wind and integrated by differentiable RK4, supervised photometrically alone. To test whether such a prior is physically grounded rather than merely well fit, we build a controlled synthetic testbed of three procedurally generated trees spanning an order of magnitude in skeleton complexity, whose per-part natural frequency follows from its own geometry and whose damping ratio is a fixed constant, both held out of training. On it, we measure held-out views, temporal extrapolation, zero-shot transfer to unseen wind speeds, and recovery of the physical parameters themselves. The prior costs appearance fidelity on in-distribution views and extrapolates markedly better outside the training window and the training wind, while parameter recovery is far weaker than it first appears: frequency recovery survives an untrained null control on only the sparsest of the three trees, and damping is not recovered at all.

</details>


### [11] [GazeDiT: Gaze-Accurate Diffusion Image Generation for Eye Tracking via Spatial Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17814)
*Dongze Wu,David Colmenares,Fengting Yang,Jogendra Nath Kundu,Yao Xie,Ali Behrooz,Conny Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GazeDiT 提出一种通过内部构造的空间条件（瞳孔/虹膜局部几何）来精确控制 4D 双目注视标签的图像扩散模型，生成数据显著降低注视标签误差并改善下游眼动追踪器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型越来越多地用于生成合成训练数据，但当条件信号为低维、粗粒度的标签（如 4D 注视标签）时，难以实现精确的标签控制。文本条件图像只要求宽泛的提示一致性，而有监督训练要求每张图像与其数值标签精确对应。在眼动追踪中，这种困难尤为突出：4D 双目注视通过细微、空间局部化的瞳孔与虹膜几何来体现，全局低维标签难以直接约束这种局部几何。

Method: GazeDiT 的核心思路是构造内部空间条件，将全局注视标签锚定到局部几何上。训练阶段，使用冻结的 SegFormer 从多样真实图像中提取瞳孔/虹膜几何，使模型学习以该几何为条件的逼真外观；推理阶段，采用物理眼睛渲染器通过改变解剖结构和相机状态来采样与注视一致的几何，从而在无需源图像的情况下实现多样化合成。模型以该空间几何为条件进行扩散生成。

Result: 与其他扩散基线相比，GazeDiT 的尾部注视标签误差显著更低，并接近同一冻结注视估计器在真实图像上的误差。其生成的数据还改善了下游眼动追踪器：在最小队列的困难样本上，注视误差从 3.05° 降至 2.80°。

Conclusion: 通过在扩散模型中引入内部构造的空间几何条件，GazeDiT 有效解决了低维标签条件下的精确注视控制难题，生成的合成数据不仅标签精度接近真实图像水平，还能切实提升下游眼动追踪模型在困难场景下的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used to generate synthetic training data, but precise label control remains difficult when the conditioning signal is low-dimensional and coarse. Text-conditioned images are judged by broad prompt consistency, whereas supervised training requires precise correspondence between each image and its numerical label. This is challenging in eye tracking, where a 4D binocular gaze is expressed through subtle, spatially localized pupil and iris geometry. We introduce GazeDiT, a diffusion model that generates images for a requested 4D gaze through an internally constructed spatial condition that grounds the global gaze label in this local geometry. During training, a frozen SegFormer extracts pupil/iris geometry from diverse real images, allowing the model to learn realistic appearance conditioned on that geometry. At inference, a physical eye renderer samples gaze-consistent geometries by varying anatomy and camera state, enabling diverse synthesis without a source image. GazeDiT achieves substantially lower tail gaze-label error than other diffusion baselines, approaching the error of the same frozen gaze estimator on real images. Its generated data also improves the downstream eye tracker, reducing gaze error on difficult cases from 3.05° to 2.80° in the smallest cohort.

</details>


### [12] [CALIPER: Metric-Grounded Model-Free Recognition of Visually Similar Industrial Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17820)
*Alankrit Gupta,Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CALIPER 是一个无模型的 RGB-D 框架，结合基于支持集的外观匹配与度量尺寸证据，用于视觉相似工业零件的细粒度识别，支持无需重训的新类别注册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉相似的工业零件主要在物理尺寸上存在差异，而将检测到的目标裁剪归一化到固定尺寸会抑制绝对尺度信息；同时工业库存不断变化，可能无法获得 CAD 模型和大规模类别专属数据集，传统方法难以适应。

Method: CALIPER 采用无模型 RGB-D 框架。训练时，每个类别通过单段转台 RGB-D 视频和一到两张标注真实图像完成注册：3D 重建提供新视角外观支持，对齐后的深度图生成类别专属的度量尺寸分布。推理时，粗粒度 YOLOv8n-seg 模型定位零件，冻结的 DINOv2 骨干网络配合情景式（episodic）训练的嵌入头进行细粒度支持集匹配。Margin-conditioned 度量融合仅在出现外观模糊决策时激活概率尺寸证据。新类别由小型 RGB-D 支持集注册，无需更新网络参数。

Result: 在 18 个视觉相似的工业零件上评估：16 类用于训练，2 个螺丝作为免训练注册类别。CALIPER 达到 88.2% 的闭集准确率和 99.8% 的定位召回率；对两个未见过螺丝进行 10-shot 注册后总体准确率为 85.7%。度量融合将未见类准确率最高提升 37.4 个百分点，且未对原有库存类别造成统计显著下降。机械臂部署中识别出 18 个零件中的 17 个，无需针对部署重新训练。

Conclusion: CALIPER 证明结合外观支持匹配与度量尺寸证据，可以在缺少 CAD 模型和大规模数据的情况下实现可扩展的细粒度工业零件识别，并能将度量信息有效用于新类别注册和实际部署。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition of visually similar industrial parts is challenging when classes differ primarily in physical dimensions. Normalizing detected object crops to a fixed input size suppresses absolute scale, while CAD models and large class-specific datasets may be unavailable in evolving industrial inventories. We present CALIPER, a model-free RGB-D framework that couples support-based appearance matching with metric size evidence. Each training class is onboarded from a single turntable RGB-D video and one to two labeled real images; 3D reconstruction provides novel-view appearance support, while aligned depth yields a class-specific metric size profile. At inference, a coarse YOLOv8n-seg model localizes parts, and a frozen DINOv2 backbone with an episodically trained embedding head performs fine-grained support matching. Margin-conditioned metric fusion activates probabilistic size evidence only for appearance-ambiguous decisions. New classes are enrolled from a small RGB-D support set without updating network parameters. We evaluate CALIPER on 18 visually similar industrial parts: 16 classes are used for training, while two screws are reserved for training-free enrollment. CALIPER achieves 88.2% closed-set accuracy with 99.8% localization recall and 85.7% overall accuracy after 10-shot enrollment of the two unseen screws. Metric fusion improves unseen-class accuracy by up to 37.4 percentage points without statistically significant degradation of the original inventory. Robot-arm deployment identifies 17/18 parts without deployment-specific retraining.

</details>


### [13] [RoboVAD: A Large Cross-Domain Evaluation Benchmark for Anomaly Detection in Robotic Arm Manipulation Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17843)
*Alexandru-Bogdan Dura,Sebastian Balmus,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboVAD，一个面向机械臂操作视频的大规模视频异常检测基准，包含跨域评估场景（训练中未见过的动作与异常类型），并验证现有及一种新提出的 VAD 方法，结果显示最具挑战的设置下所有方法微平均帧级 AUC 均低于 70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测在公共监控、道路安全等场景应用广泛，对机械臂交互也具有重要意义（如学习更好的交互与操作能力、异常发生时触发恢复流程）。然而，由于可用资源匮乏，机械臂操作视频中的异常检测研究十分有限，因此需要大规模基准来推动该方向的发展。

Method: 构建 RoboVAD 大规模基准，设计跨域评估场景，使部分动作（机械臂执行的任务）与异常类型（执行任务时出现的错误）在训练阶段不可见；在基准上训练并评估多种现有最先进 VAD 方法，并提出一种专门适配机械臂操作的新方法。

Result: 所提方法优于多数最先进方法，但在最具挑战性的评估设置下，所有方法（含所提方法）的微平均帧级 AUC 均低于 70%，表明该基准具有较高难度。

Conclusion: RoboVAD 填补了机械臂操作视频异常检测领域缺乏基准的空白，为在真实场景（未预见任务与新异常类型）下评测 VAD 方法提供了平台；实验表明现有及改进方法仍有显著提升空间。数据集与代码已公开发布。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is an actively studied task, having wide applications in typical scenarios such as public surveillance and road traffic safety. The task is also relevant for robotic arm interactions, where it has several downstream applications, including learning better interaction and manipulation abilities, triggering recovery procedures when anomalies occur, etc. Despite its relevance, the exploration of anomaly detection in robotic arm manipulation videos is limited by the low number of available resources. To this end, we introduce RoboVAD, a large-scale benchmark for video anomaly detection that comprises challenging cross-domain evaluation scenarios, where certain actions (tasks executed by a robotic arm) and anomaly types (mistakes that occur while performing certain tasks) remain unseen during training. RoboVAD is designed to benchmark VAD methods in realistic scenarios, where robotic arms can perform unforeseen tasks, and thereby encounter new anomaly types. We train and evaluate several state-of-the-art VAD methods, including a novel method specifically adapted for robotic arm manipulation. While the proposed method outperforms many state-of-the-art competitors, all methods remain below a micro-averaged frame-level AUC threshold of 70% in the most challenging evaluation setup, confirming the difficulty of the proposed benchmark. We publicly release our dataset and code at https://zenodo.org/records/22754659.

</details>


### [14] [Investigating Adversarial Robustness of Heterogeneous Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17856)
*Chenyi Wang,Yutong Liu,Qingzhao Zhang,Ming F. Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 异构协同感知中，先前认为异构性天然防御攻击的假设不成立；本文提出单次前向、免标签的生成式攻击 HetPoison，能跨异构设计迁移并达到或超越优化型攻击效果，并据此提出轻量信任层 HetShield，恢复 83–95% 被攻击损失的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知的数据交换引入关键攻击面，单个恶意智能体可发送伪造特征擦除邻居融合场景中的真实物体。此前普遍假设异构性天然防御此类攻击（攻击者不了解受害方检测器，且翻译模块扰乱对抗梯度），但这一保护假设缺乏验证，且现有优化型攻击依赖真实标签与迭代反向传播，无法构成实时的实际现场威胁。

Method: 1) 用 matched-objective harness（统一扰动预算、目标与前向路径）评估调优后的迭代攻击，检验异构性防御假设；2) 提出 HetPoison：一个学习得到的生成器，在单次、免标签前向传播中生成物体擦除扰动，无需访问受害方检测器即可跨异构设计迁移；3) 提出 HetShield：轻量信任层，校验特征间的时空一致性来抵御攻击。

Result: 调优后的迭代攻击可缩小甚至逆转此前由异构性带来的鲁棒性差距，证明异构性并非有效防御；HetPoison 跨主要异构设计迁移，效果匹配或超过代价高昂的优化型攻击；HetShield 恢复 83–95% 被攻击损失的精度，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 异构性本身不能作为协同感知的安全防线，攻击者即使不了解受害方检测器也能实现实用的实时攻击。因此需要如 HetShield 这样的显式防御机制来保障异构协同感知的鲁棒性与安全性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous cooperative perception (CP) enables connected vehicles with diverse sensor setups to share spatial awareness via compact feature maps, where receivers reconcile these maps using learned translation modules for fusion and inference. Prior attacks against CP in a homogeneous setting reveal that the data exchange introduces a critical attack surface: a single malicious agent can transmit crafted features that erase real objects from a neighbor's fused scene. Yet, it is widely hypothesized that heterogeneity naturally defends against these attacks, as the attacker lacks knowledge of the victim's detector and the translation module scrambles adversarial gradients. We demonstrate that this protection is largely an illusion. Using a matched-objective harness to standardize the perturbation budget, objective, and forward path, we show that properly tuned iterative attacks close or reverse the apparent robustness gap. However, these optimization-based attacks require ground-truth labels and iterative backpropagation, meaning they do not represent a practical field threat running in real-time. To bridge this gap, we introduce HetPoison, a learned generator that crafts a removal perturbation in a single, label-free forward pass. HetPoison transfers across major heterogeneous designs without requiring access to the victim's detector, matching or exceeding the effectiveness of expensive optimizer-based attacks. Since heterogeneity itself is not a defense, we propose HetShield, a lightweight trust layer that validates the spatiotemporal consistency across features, recovering 83--95% of the accuracy degraded by attacks, outperforming prior art.

</details>


### [15] [Lumen: Parameter-Efficient Alignment of Pretrained Vision and Language Encoders for Zero-Shot Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17868)
*Kiarash Tajbakhsh,Abdelrahman Faqieh,Michael Jopiti,Javier Garcia-Baroja,Philipp Zens,Branislav Zagrapan,Yuri Tolkach,Martin D. Berger,Aurel Perren,Bastian Dislich,Inti Zlobec,Amjad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lumen 通过参数高效的适配器与投影头对齐冻结的 Virchow2 和 BioMedBERT 骨干，仅训练 0.40% 参数即可在零样本斑块基准和淋巴结转移检测上达到或超越现有病理视觉语言模型，证明对齐冻结单模态基础模型是强且可迁移的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理视觉语言模型通常依赖对大规模编码器进行预训练或全量微调，计算开销大且会改动原有表征。作者提出疑问：能否在不改动冻结的单模态基础模型表征的前提下，仅通过参数高效对齐来组装病理视觉语言模型，从而以极低训练代价获得强性能与可迁移性。

Method: 提出 Lumen，使用秩-4 适配器（rank-4 adapters）和投影头（projection heads）对齐冻结的 Virchow2（视觉）与 BioMedBERT（文本）骨干，仅在公开的 QUILT-1M 语料上训练总参数的 0.40%。评估涵盖九个公开零样本斑块基准、淋巴结转移检测（4,214 张内部留出切片及 2,368 张跨九个外部队列、六个器官的切片）、内部校准阈值下的平衡准确率，以及跨模态检索对比；并与全量微调双编码器进行消融比较。

Result: 在九个公开零样本斑块基准上，Lumen 的平均机会校正平衡准确率最高，为 0.546，最强基线为 0.461（配对差异 0.086，95% CI 0.042–0.136）。淋巴结转移检测上，AUROC 内部为 0.964（95% CI 0.956–0.971），外部为 0.955（95% CI 0.942–0.966）；在内部校准阈值下，平衡准确率内部 0.909（95% CI 0.896–0.923）、外部 0.915（95% CI 0.902–0.929），超越所有视觉语言基线。跨模态检索方面排名第三，落后于 CONCH 和 PathGen-L/14。全量微调双编码器相比低秩适配并未带来一致收益，仅提升了检索性能。

Conclusion: 对齐冻结的单模态基础模型（仅训练极少量参数）即可在斑块级和切片级任务上取得强大且可迁移的性能，为病理视觉语言模型的构建提供了一种计算高效、无需改动预训练表征的可行替代方案；唯一明显短板是跨模态检索，仍落后于专门的对比预训练模型。

Abstract: Pathology vision-language models are commonly built by pretraining or fine-tuning large encoders on paired image-caption data. We asked whether a pathology vision-language model can instead be assembled by parameter-efficient alignment of frozen unimodal foundation models, leaving their pretrained representations untouched. Here we present Lumen, which aligns frozen Virchow2 and BioMedBERT backbones using rank-4 adapters and projection heads, training only 0.40% of the total parameters on the public QUILT-1M corpus. Across nine public zero-shot patch benchmarks, Lumen achieved the highest mean chance-corrected balanced accuracy, 0.546 versus 0.461 for the strongest baseline (paired difference 0.086, 95% CI 0.042-0.136). On lymph-node metastasis detection, Lumen reached an AUROC of 0.964 (95% CI 0.956-0.971) on 4,214 held-out internal slides and 0.955 (95% CI 0.942-0.966) on 2,368 slides across nine external cohorts and six organs. At the internally calibrated threshold, it outperformed all vision-language baselines, with a balanced accuracy of 0.909 (95% CI 0.896-0.923) internally and 0.915 (95% CI 0.902-0.929) externally. Lumen performed competitively across the evaluations, with the exception of cross-modal retrieval, where it ranked third behind CONCH and PathGen-L/14. Fully fine-tuning both encoders gave Lumen no consistent benefit over low-rank adaptation, although it improved retrieval. Aligning frozen unimodal foundation models therefore yields strong and transferable performance at patch and slide level while training only a small fraction of the parameters.

</details>


### [16] [Can VLMs Reliably Assess Sidewalk Accessibility Attributes from Pedestrian-Level Imagery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17882)
*Seung Jae Lieu,Diego Morra,Chiara Cadoni,Wonseop Song,Martina Mazzarello,Carlo Ratti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于采样的保形预测（CP）应用于视觉语言模型（VLM）的无障碍评估，在首尔514张人行道图像上检验有效宽度、纵坡、横坡和路面状况的可测性，发现校准后虽达到90%名义覆盖率，但各属性信息量差异显著，且响应自一致性不等于准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市无障碍（尤其对轮椅使用者和行动不便者）的关键环节是人行道是否满足可量化的合规要求。以往基于行人视角图像用VLM评估时，常将采样离散度当作不确定性，但缺乏真实地面真值的校准，导致其可靠性存疑。作者旨在检验这些属性能否被可靠评估，并引入CP提供有校准保证的不确定性量化。

Method: 在首尔采集514张人行道图像及现场实测的四类属性（有效宽度、纵坡、横坡、路面状况A-E级）作为地面真值；用四个VLM生成基于采样的响应，并首次采用采样式保形预测进行校准，比较校准前后区间覆盖率、区间半宽及信息量，并分析非对称校准、自一致性子集的表现。

Result: 保形校准在全部模型和属性上达到名义90%覆盖率；有效宽度信息量最高（最佳模型平均区间半宽约1.0 m），且所有模型都高估宽度，非对称校准可在覆盖不变下将区间缩短最多33%；纵坡仅勉强有信息，横坡区间过宽无法解析法规阈值，路面状况除一个模型外退化为A-E全五个等级；未校准的原始采样区间在名义90%水平下仅覆盖17-47%的实测值，在自一致性最强的图像中漏检率高达96%，说明自一致性不等于准确、采样离散度未经校准不能视为不确定性。

Conclusion: 所有定量属性均未达到通用合规评估所需的精度，但保形预测能仅凭校准数据识别出哪些属性可用于筛查远离阈值的路段。作者公开了带标注的行人视角图像及对应现场实测属性值数据集。

Abstract: An important component of urban accessibility, particularly for wheelchair users and people with reduced mobility, is sidewalk compliance with measurable requirements. We test whether effective width, longitudinal slope, cross slope, and pavement condition can be assessed reliably from pedestrian-level imagery using vision-language models (VLMs). We present the first application of sampling-based conformal prediction (CP) for VLM-based accessibility assessment. We evaluate four VLMs on 514 sidewalk images from Seoul, South Korea, with field-measured ground truth. Conformal calibration attains the nominal 90% coverage for all models and attributes, but the calibrated regions differ in informativeness. Effective width yields the most informative estimates, with a mean interval half-width of about 1.0 m for the best model. Since every model overestimates width, asymmetric calibration shortens the intervals by up to 33% at unchanged coverage. Longitudinal slope is marginally informative, cross-slope intervals are too wide to resolve regulatory thresholds, and pavement-condition sets degenerate to all five grades (A-E) for three of the four models. Uncalibrated intervals from raw sampling dispersion cover only 17-47% of field-measured values at a nominal 90% level. Among the images with the most self-consistent responses, these intervals miss the field-measured value in up to 96% of cases. Response self-consistency is therefore not evidence of accuracy, and sampling dispersion cannot be interpreted as uncertainty until it has been calibrated against field-measured ground truth. No quantitative attribute reaches the precision required for general compliance assessment, but CP identifies from calibration data alone which attributes can support screening of segments far from the thresholds. We release the annotated pedestrian-level images and their corresponding field-measured attribute values.

</details>


### [17] [Zing-0.5: Toward Playable Worlds with Real-Time Joint Action and Text Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17909)
*Mingyang Chen,Shengdong Chen,Xiaoxiao Fu,Bosheng Gong,Haoyuan Guo,Bowen Li,Jiawen Li,Kejun Li,Tianpeng Li,Yin Liu,Haoze Sun,Zeyang Tian,Meng Wang,Xinmiao Wu,Jiangqiao Yan,Zining Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zing-0.5 是一个 5B 参数的自回归世界模型，支持用户通过键盘与在线文本联合操控实时探索并影响生成的世界，在低延迟、低成本下实现可玩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型往往缺乏"可玩性"：用户难以在生成过程中实时影响事件走向并接收反馈。作者旨在构建一个既能探索生成世界、又能通过联合键盘与文本控制影响事件演变的世界模型，使生成内容真正具备交互式体验。

Method: 提出三项技术：1) 统一动作与文本条件化，将幅度感知键盘输入与时间对齐的文本指令、联合标注视频结合，在同一序列中学习导航与事件控制；2) 事件尺度监督的增量生成，用连接多提示视频训练的段级教师模型，通过分布匹配蒸馏监督块级因果学生模型；3) 低成本实时交互，结合四步生成与上下文保持流式传输，支持 832×480 分辨率、24 FPS 推理，单流分钟成本约 0.009 美元。

Result: 在 158 个 WBench Navigation 测试用例上取得总分 81.0、一致性得分 88.5；联合控制演示展示了在持续导航过程中由文本指令驱动的事件变化，且无需重新开始生成。模型权重、推理代码及 Zing-SGLang 服务实现均已开源。

Conclusion: Zing-0.5 成功将动作与文本条件融合进自回归世界模型，以高一致性和低成本实现实时可玩的生成世界，并通过开源生态推动可玩生成世界方向的研究。

Abstract: We introduce Zing-0.5, a 5B autoregressive world model designed for playability: users can explore generated worlds, influence unfolding events, and respond to the resulting feedback through joint keyboard and online text control. Our approach brings together three technical contributions: (1) Unified action and text conditioning, combining magnitude-aware keyboard inputs with temporally aligned text instructions and jointly annotated videos to learn navigation and event control within the same sequence; (2) Event-scale supervision for incremental generation, using a segment-level teacher trained on connected multi-prompt videos to supervise a block-level causal student through distribution-matching distillation; and (3) Low-cost real-time interaction, combining four-step generation with context-preserving streaming to support 832 x 480 inference at 24 FPS at an estimated server rental cost of approximately USD 0.009 per stream-minute. Zing-0.5 achieves an overall score of 81.0 and a consistency score of 88.5 across 158 WBench Navigation cases. A joint-control demonstration shows a text-directed event change during continued navigation without restarting generation. We release the model weights, inference code, and Zing-SGLang serving implementation to support further work on playable generated worlds.

</details>


### [18] [Face-voice Association across LAnguages and Gender (FLAG) 2027 Challenge Evaluation Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17913)
*Marta Moscati,Swapnil Khandoker,Muhammad Saad Saeed,Shah Nawaz,Fatima Noor,Rohan Kumar Das,Mubashir Noman,Junaid Mir,Muhammad Haroon Yousaf,Khalid Malik,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 FLAG 2027 挑战赛，将人脸-声音关联建模为跨模态验证任务，旨在考察模型在跨语言（heard/unheard）和同性别（gender-constrained）条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸-声音关联模型可能依赖语音中的语言或性别线索，而非说话人特有的声纹特征，导致在识别多语言说话人或区分同性别人群时性能下降。为系统研究这一问题，作者发起该挑战。

Method: 将人脸-声音关联形式化为跨模态验证任务：给定一段语音，从包含目标说话人人脸及若干负样本的“gallery”中识别正确人脸。采用未见于训练数据的身份（unseen）进行评估，并区分训练中听过/未听过的语言（heard/unheard）。设置两种评测条件：标准无约束条件与性别约束条件（gender-constrained，gallery 内全为同性别样本），以检验模型对性别线索的依赖。挑战提供基准数据集、预训练基线模型和评测框架。

Result: 基线模型在语言迁移（language shifts）和性别约束条件下的性能均出现下降，表明现有模型过度依赖语言与性别线索，缺乏对身份特异性特征的建模。

Conclusion: 需要推动开发能够捕捉超越语言和性别的身份特异性特征的模型，FLAG 2027 挑战通过数据、基线和评测框架为该目标提供支撑。

Abstract: Face--voice association models may rely on language or gender cues in the voice rather than on speaker-specific voice characteristics, which can lead to a performance deterioration when the model has to identify a multilingual speaker or distinguis same-gender speakers. To investigate these issues, we introduce the Face-voice Association across LAnguages and Gender (FLAG) 2027 Challenge. The challenge formulates face--voice association as a cross-modal verification task: given a voice, identify the speaker's face from a ``gallery'' of faces consisting of the speaker's face and a set of negative samples. Models are evaluated on identities not present in the training data (``unseen'') and both for languages present or absent from the training data (``heard'' and ``unheard''). Two evaluation settings are used to test models' reliance on gender: a standard, unconstrained and a gender-constrained one, where the latter uses a same-gender gallery. The performance of existing, baseline models in these settings reveals that models performance degrades under language shifts and in gender-constrained settings, highlighting the need to foster the development of models that capture identity-specific aspects beyond language and gender. The challenge provides a benchmark dataset, pretrained baseline models, and an evaluation framework to advance face--voice association.

</details>


### [19] [Audio for Sports Highlight Detection: A Comparative Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17923)
*Hao Xu,Meenakshi Sarkar,Vishnu Raj,David Gunawan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视音频在体育精彩片段检测中的作用，构建轻量级纯音频基线，发现仅用音频即可取得强劲性能，甚至超过部分现有音视频方法；音频与视觉互补，声源分离分析表明解说人声最具信息量，且精彩片段在响度与中频能量上更高但分布重叠明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体育精彩片段检测方法多侧重视觉或视觉-语言表示，忽视了体育视频中丰富的音频线索（解说、观众反应、哨声、击球声、裁判声等）。作者希望探究音频这一被低估的模态究竟能单独发挥多大作用。

Method: 使用预训练音频表示构建轻量级纯音频基线（如 GRU）；在 SV-Highlights 基准上，将纯音频、纯视觉与音视频方法在同一监督评估设置下对比；进一步构建简单音视频融合基线；并通过声源分离分析（vocal/解说 vs. 纯背景音频）以及可解释音频特征（RMS 响度、峰值响度、中频能量）统计来解析音频的贡献。

Result: 纯音频 GRU 基线性能强劲，在该监督评估设置下超过若干现有音视频方法；简单音视频融合取得全指标最佳，说明音视频互补；解说/人声音频比纯背景音频信息量更大，两者结合最优；精彩片段在 RMS 响度、峰值响度和中频能量上均高于非精彩片段，但分布重叠明显，表明仅靠响度不够。

Conclusion: 音频是体育精彩片段检测中被低估但信息量极高的模态，应作为主要信号而非辅助线索对待。

Abstract: Sports highlight detection aims to identify the most exciting and meaningful moments from long sports videos. While existing methods often emphasize visual or visual-language representations, sports videos contain rich audio cues, including commentator speech, crowd reactions, whistles, ball impacts, and referee calls. In this work, we revisit the role of audio in sports highlight detection and ask a simple question: how far can audio alone go? We construct lightweight audio-only baselines using pretrained audio representations and compare them with visual-only and audio-visual methods on the SV-Highlights benchmark. Surprisingly, our audio-only GRU baseline achieves strong performance and outperforms several existing audio-visual methods under our supervised evaluation setting. Furthermore, a simple audio-visual fusion baseline achieves the best performance across all metrics, indicating that audio and visual cues provide complementary information. To better understand the contribution of audio, we conduct source-separated analysis and show that vocal/commentary audio is more informative than background-only audio, while their combination performs best. We also analyze interpretable audio cues and find that highlight clips exhibit higher RMS loudness, peak loudness, and mid-frequency energy than non-highlight clips, although substantial distribution overlap indicates that loudness alone is insufficient. Our findings suggest that audio is an underexplored but highly informative modality for sports highlight detection and should be treated as a primary signal rather than merely an auxiliary cue.

</details>


### [20] [Rapid Loss of the Sierra Nevada's Largest Trees Driven by Fire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17925)
*Fabien H. Wagner,Dan J. Dixon,Christopher W. Woodall,Mayumi C. M. Hirye,Felipe Saad,Griffin Carter,Ricardo Dalagnol,Lorena Alves,Cynthia Creze,Stephen C. Hagen,Zhihua Liu,Christopher Mihiar,Adugna Mullissa,Le Bienfaiteur Sagang Takougoum,Bryan Shaddy,Yan Yang,Dafeng Zhang,Sassan Saatchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用深度学习模型（U-Net-ID）和亚米级航拍影像，在加利福尼亚州内华达山脉植物区系范围内测绘了所有冠幅≥100 m²的巨树（共6,515,705棵），并结合Sentinel-2时间序列与BFAST突变检测算法分析了2020–2025年巨树的健康动态，发现野火是巨树死亡的主导因素，在极端火季（2020–2021年）杀死了全区10%的巨树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨树对生物量储存、生境结构和生态系统功能贡献巨大，但其分布和健康动态在区域尺度上仍缺乏精确量化。为此，需要开展大范围、个体水平的巨树制图与动态监测。

Method: 使用基于2020年亚米级航拍影像生成的高程冠层模型，训练深度学习模型U-Net-ID（采用超过330万个合成树冠训练）来勾绘所有冠幅≥100 m²的巨树个体，并用20,273个独立树冠验证（中位IoU=0.602）；随后利用2020–2025年Sentinel-2时间序列提取每个树冠的光谱轨迹，结合BFAST突变点检测算法与扰动分类框架，识别单株树木的死亡、受扰和恢复轨迹，并用CAL FIRE火场边界估计野火影响。

Result: 共测绘出6,515,705棵巨树，覆盖内华达山脉植物区系约78.7%的区域；巨树空间分布与海拔、温度和降水相关；野火是巨树死亡的主导驱动因素，导致全区10%的巨树死亡，且死亡主要集中在2020–2021年的极端火季。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在区域尺度上实现了巨树的个体级制图与多年健康动态追踪，揭示了野火（尤其极端火季）对内华达山脉巨树种群构成的重大威胁，为巨树保护与生态系统管理提供了关键数据支撑。

Abstract: Large trees disproportionately contribute to biomass storage, habitat structure, and ecosystem functioning. However, their distribution and health dynamics remain poorly quantified at a regional scale. Here, a deep learning model (U-Net-ID) and canopy height models derived from sub-meter aerial imagery from 2020 were used to delineate all individual trees with crown area $\geq$ 100 m$^2$ across the Sierra Nevada Floristic Province. The model was trained using more than 3.3 million synthetic tree crowns and achieved a median Intersection over Union (IoU) of 0.602 when validated against an independent dataset of 20,273 crowns. A total of 6,515,705 large trees were mapped, occurring across approximately 78.7% of the Sierra Nevada Floristic Province. The spatial distribution of large trees showed associations with elevation, temperature, and precipitation. Using Sentinel-2 time series from 2020 to 2025, tree health dynamics were characterized by extracting spectral trajectories for each crown and applying BFAST breakpoint detection algorithm combined with a disturbance classification framework to identify mortality, disturbance, and recovery trajectories of individual trees. Wildfires, estimated from CAL FIRE fire perimeters, were identified as the dominant driver of large-tree mortality, killing 10% of all large trees in the Sierra Nevada, with mortality strongly concentrated during the extreme 2020-2021 fire seasons.

</details>


### [21] [EDCT-Bench: Uncovering Faithfulness Gaps in VLMs via Explanation-Driven Counterfactual Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17953)
*Sihao Ding,Santosh Vasa,Aditi Ramadwar,Thomas Monninger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EDCT 协议，通过干预式反事实测试评估 VLM 自然语言解释的忠实度，并构建跨三领域的 EDCT-Bench 基准，揭示模型解释与视觉证据之间的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 生成的 NLE 往往表面合理，却与其所引用的视觉证据不一致；现有评估缺乏系统的干预式手段来验证解释是否真正忠实于图像。

Method: 提出 Explanation-Driven Counterfactual Testing (EDCT) 协议：提取解释中引用的视觉概念，对其实施已验证的最小编辑，再检验编辑后图像下模型的答案与解释是否保持一致性。并据此构建涵盖 OK-VQA、DriveLM、3DSRBench 三领域的 EDCT-Bench 基准。

Result: 在多个 VLM 上评估发现显著的忠实度缺口，模型在视觉内容被验证性修改后仍频繁给出与之不一致的响应；微调研究显示 EDCT 生成的反事实样本是高效的训练信号。

Conclusion: EDCT 能有效暴露 VLM 解释的不忠实之处，其反事实样本可作为高价值训练数据，为提升 VLM 解释的可信度与一致性提供了方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can produce Natural Language Explanations (NLEs) that sound plausible yet remain inconsistent with the visual evidence they cite. We present Explanation-Driven Counterfactual Testing (EDCT), an intervention-based protocol that extracts visual concepts cited in a model's explanation, applies verified minimal edits to them, and tests whether the resulting answer and explanation remain consistent with the edited image. Using this protocol, we create EDCT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning three complementary domains: knowledge-intensive visual question answering (OK-VQA), safety-critical driving (DriveLM), and 3D spatial reasoning (3DSRBench). Across the evaluated VLMs, EDCT reveals substantial faithfulness gaps, with models frequently producing responses inconsistent with verified visual changes. Finally, our fine-tuning study suggests that EDCT-generated counterfactuals provide high-impact training signals.

</details>


### [22] [Newer Is Not Fairer: Gender Stereotyping in Text-to-Image AI Across Model Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18007)
*Shesh Narayan Gupta,Nik Bear Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了4代Stable Diffusion模型在20种职业中的性别表征，发现所有开放模型男性偏倚显著，且模型代际间并未稳定改善，无任何模型实现性别均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图生成模型在专业与创意场景中被广泛使用，但其在不同代际模型中如何表征职业性别、以及新模型是否更公平，仍缺乏跨代际的系统性理解。

Method: 对20种职业、5种提示模板、4代Stable Diffusion模型（SD 1.5、SD 2.1、SDXL、SD 3 Medium）共生成8,000张图像（每个职业-模型单元n=100，即5个提示×20张图），并使用DeepFace对全部图像进行性别分类；随后与美国劳工统计局（BLS）劳动力数据对比，并做Benjamini-Hochberg多重检验校正。

Result: 8,000张开源图像中76.4%呈现男性主体（95% CI [75.1%, 78.7%]，p < 2.2×10⁻¹⁶，BH校正后显著）；历史女性编码职业的图像中57.6%也呈现男性主体（BH校正后p=1.71×10⁻²¹）。与BLS数据相比，模型平均低估女性20-46个百分点，偏差尤以接近性别均衡的职业最为突出（如科学家、清洁工）。偏倚从SD 1.5到SDXL加剧，至SD 3 Medium部分回升，但并未稳步改善。与GPT-image-1的初步比较显示其偏倚较低，但效应量小（Cramer's V=0.080）且属探索性分析。

Conclusion: 所有评估的模型均未能实现职业性别均衡，开放源模型存在系统性的男性偏倚，且跨代际并未呈现一致改善；封闭模型虽可能偏倚较低但差异的实际效应有限，提示需要针对性的缓解措施。

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models are widely used in professional and creative settings, yet how they represent gender across occupations -- and whether newer models are fairer -- remains poorly understood across multiple generations. We evaluate gender representation across 20 occupations, 5 prompt templates, and 4 Stable Diffusion model generations (SD 1.5, SD 2.1, SDXL, SD 3 Medium), generating 8,000 images with n = 100 per occupation-model cell (5 prompts x 20 images), and classifying all with DeepFace. Across the 8,000 open-source images, 76.4% show male subjects (95% CI [75.1%, 78.7%], p < 2.2 x 10^-16, Benjamini-Hochberg adjusted). More strikingly, 57.6% of images for historically female-coded occupations show male subjects (raw p = 3.43 x 10^-22, BH-adjusted p = 1.71 x 10^-21). All nine significant tests reported in this paper survive BH correction across 10 tests. When compared against U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce data, models underrepresent women by 20-46pp on average, with particularly large deviations for near gender-balanced occupations: scientist (48% female in BLS, 82-99% male in model outputs) and cleaner (46% female in BLS, 80-92% male in outputs). Model generations do not improve steadily: bias worsens from SD 1.5 to SDXL before partially recovering in SD 3 Medium. A preliminary comparison with GPT-image-1 on five occupations suggests lower bias than open-source models, though the practical effect is small (Cramer's V = 0.080) and the comparison is exploratory. No model achieves gender parity.

</details>


### [23] [IRIS: Implicit Rendering Matters for Pose-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18034)
*Wenyu Li,Sidun Liu,Peng Qiao,Yong Dou,Tongrui Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IRIS 提出一种完全自监督的无位姿多视图新视角合成框架，以"隐式神经场 + 自预测相机"的中间方案，兼顾隐式建模的优化稳定性与显式表征的几何约束，在合成质量与位姿精度上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无位姿（unposed）多视图新视角合成要求模型在缺少位姿监督的条件下，同时学习场景表示与相机参数。现有方法分处两个极端：隐式潜空间渲染灵活易优化，但相机估计缺乏几何约束（grounding 弱）；显式 3D 表征几何约束强，但参数化更重、优化更脆弱。本文旨在填补两者之间的中间地带，提供兼具灵活性与几何结构的方法。

Method: 提出 IRIS 框架：将场景表示为隐式神经场（latent neural field），在自预测相机下通过查询该场来渲染新视角。具体做法是：在采样到的 3D 点上聚合参考视图的投影特征，形成逐点的隐式特征，再沿目标光线组合这些特征进行渲染。该设计保留隐式建模的灵活性与优化稳定性，同时引入比无约束隐式渲染更强的几何结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，IRIS 在完全自监督学习设定下，取得了较强的新视角合成质量，并具备具有竞争力的相机位姿估计精度。

Conclusion: IRIS 验证了"隐式神经场 + 自预测相机"的中间路线可行，既避免了纯隐式方法位姿估计弱的问题，又规避了纯显式方法优化脆弱的缺陷，为无位姿多视角新视角合成提供了实用的自监督方案。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from unposed multi-view images remains challenging, as the model must jointly learn scene representations and camera parameters without pose supervision. Existing approaches largely fall into two extremes: implicit latent-space rendering is flexible and easy to optimize, but often yields weakly grounded camera estimation; explicit 3D representations provide stronger geometric grounding, but introduce heavier parameterization and more fragile optimization. In this paper, we present IRIS, a fully self-supervised framework that provides a practical middle ground between these two paradigms. Instead of decoding free latent tokens or reconstructing fully explicit 3D primitives, IRIS represents the scene as a latent neural field and renders novel views by querying this field under self-predicted cameras. Specifically, projected features from reference views are aggregated at sampled 3D points to form point-wise latent features, which are then composed along target rays for rendering. This design preserves the flexibility and optimization stability of implicit modeling, while introducing stronger geometric structure than unconstrained latent rendering. Extensive experiments show that IRIS achieves strong novel view synthesis quality with competitive pose accuracy under fully self-supervised learning. Our project page: https://leo-frank.github.io/IRIS

</details>


### [24] [SetPlanner: A Lightweight Plug-in Point-Set Planner for Frozen SAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18037)
*Dawei Yan,Yuezhe Yang,Menglan Ruan,Chunfeng Yang,Yudong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SetPlanner，一个仅 1.52M 参数的即插即用点集规划器，用于冻结的 SAM，将自动提示建模为轻量级点集规划，实现无需人工提示的全自动器械分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 提供可复用的先验，但需要人工提示，无法支持全自动器械分割；而自动提示对细长、关节式、反光且部分遮挡的手术器械尤其困难，因为多个提示配置都可能有效。

Method: 将自动提示形式化为轻量级点集规划，并在冻结路径（frozen pathway）下隔离点源。SetPlanner 作为冻结 SAM 的 1.52M 参数插件，保留 SAM 的点提示接口并支持跨骨干复用；它用置换感知的条件流（permutation-aware conditional flow）从几何感知目标规划完整的无序 K 点集；SAM 解码 8 个候选，通过共识读出（consensus readout）得到无真值标注的预测。

Result: 在三个内窥镜数据集上，SetPlanner 在全部六条迁移路径上均胜过 LoRA 微调系统；在冻结路径协议下，于 Kvasir-Instrument 上达到 0.934 Dice，并收复了 44.4 点定位差距中的 96%；候选分歧以 0.969 AUROC 对低 Dice 病例排序。

Conclusion: SetPlanner 证明通过轻量级点集规划即可在冻结 SAM 上实现高性能、可迁移且无需真值标注的全自动器械分割，并可用候选分歧进行不确定性或质量排序。

Abstract: Segment Anything Models provide reusable priors, yet they require user prompts and cannot support fully automatic instrument segmentation. Automatic prompting is difficult for thin, articulated, reflective, and partly occluded tools, where several configurations can be valid. We formulate automatic prompting as lightweight point-set planning and isolate the point source under a frozen pathway. To this end, we present SetPlanner, a 1.52M-parameter plug-in point-set planner for frozen SAM. The plug-in preserves SAM's point-prompt interface and enables reuse across backbones. SetPlanner plans complete unordered K-point sets from geometry-aware targets with a permutation-aware conditional flow. SAM decodes eight candidates; their consensus readout yields a ground-truth-free prediction. Across three endoscopic datasets, SetPlanner wins all six transfer routes over a LoRA-adapted system. Under our frozen-pathway protocol, SetPlanner reaches 0.934 Dice on Kvasir-Instrument and recovers 96% of a 44.4-point localization gap, while candidate disagreement ranks low-Dice cases at AUROC 0.969.

</details>


### [25] [CoAtNet-DeepMoE: A Convolution-Attention Hybrid with DeepSeek Mixture-of-Experts for Parameter-Efficient Tomato Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18038)
*Md Nadim Mahamood,Md Arif Shahriar,Md Shafi Ud Doula,Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoAtNet-DeepMoE，一种卷积-注意力混合架构结合 DeepSeek 混合专家（MoE）模型，用于番茄病害识别，在仅 2.47M 参数下实现近 99.80% 的准确率，兼顾了轻量化与高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口快速增长带来巨大粮食需求，而番茄作为全球主要粮食作物，大量产量因病害损失。现有基于深度学习的病害识别模型大多参数密集，训练与推理耗时，难以用于用户友好的轻量应用；而轻量模型又往往性能下降。因此需要一种在模型规模与识别精度之间取得平衡的方案。

Method: 提出 CoAtNet-DeepMoE 模型：采用卷积（Convolution）与注意力（Attention）混合架构 CoAtNet 进行丰富的特征提取，并引入 DeepSeek 混合专家（Mixture of Experts, MoE）机制，在不大幅降低准确率的前提下显著减少参数量。模型在 Kaggle 和 PlantVillage 的平衡与不平衡数据集上进行评估。

Result: 在 Kaggle 数据集上取得 99.80% 准确率、99.80% 精确率、99.80% 召回率和 99.80% F1 分数；在 PlantVillage 数据集上取得 99.83% 准确率、99.85% 精确率、99.76% 召回率和 99.80% F1 分数，参数量仅 2.47M，达到最先进（state-of-the-art）水平。

Conclusion: CoAtNet-DeepMoE 通过在 CoAtNet 混合架构上引入 DeepSeek MoE，实现了高精度与轻量化的良好平衡，在平衡及不平衡数据集中均表现出鲁棒性，证明了该模型兼顾性能与实用性的优势，源码将开源。

Abstract: The world population is growing rapidly, and technology is improving in parallel. Meeting the huge demand for food for these 7 billion people not only depends on increasing food production but also on reducing food loss. Crop losses due to disease affect both the food supply and the financial and economic stability of a country. Tomatoes are among the top food-producing crops globally, and a significant portion of this production is lost due to disease. People have used Machine Learning techniques for feature extraction and early diagnosis of tomato diseases, and nowadays, Deep Learning-based models are widely used for disease recognition. However, most existing models are highly parameter-intensive, which increases the time required for training and inference. As a result, while lightweight models are more suitable for user-friendly applications, they often show a reduction in performance. To balance performance and model size, we propose CoAtNet-DeepMoE, a Convolution-Attention hybrid architecture for rich feature extraction, further enhanced with a DeepSeek Mixture of Experts to substantially reduce the number of parameters without sacrificing accuracy. We evaluate our model on both balanced and imbalanced datasets from Kaggle and PlantVillage, demonstrating robustness and achieving 99.80% accuracy, 99.80% precision, 99.80% recall, and 99.80% F1-score on Kaggle, and 99.83% accuracy, 99.85% precision, 99.76% recall, and 99.80% F1-score on PlantVillage, representing state-of-the-art performance with only 2.47M parameters. The source code will be available at https://github.com/nadimbrur/CoAt-MoE.

</details>


### [26] [Position Anchor Tuning: Towards Efficient Adaptation of Pre-Trained Point Cloud Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18056)
*Zheng Liu,Xin Gao,Jinchao Zhu,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出位置锚点微调（PAT），一种兼顾参数效率与推理效率的点云 Transformer 参数高效微调方法，通过令牌聚合-扩展对降低 MHA 与 FFN 的计算开销，并引入 BSLoRA 以少量可训练参数学习任务特定表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PEFT 方法虽在参数效率上表现优异，但忽视了推理效率。针对预训练点云 Transformer 中计算密集的多头注意力（MHA）和前馈网络（FFN）模块，作者希望在不牺牲性能的前提下显著降低推理计算开销，同时保持高参数效率。

Method: 提出位置锚点微调（PAT）。其核心是令牌聚合-扩展对，包含令牌聚合模块（TAM）与令牌扩展模块（TEM）。对 MHA 和 FFN 块，TAM 依据 3D 空间中的位置锚点从输入令牌中提取代表性令牌，仅由这些代表性令牌参与块的计算以减少令牌数；随后 TEM 将学习到的表示传播回原始输入令牌。此外，引入基础共享低秩适应（BSLoRA），使仅负责捕捉任务特定表示的 TAM 能以少量可训练参数有效学习。

Result: 在广泛使用的基准上进行的实验表明，PAT 在性能上与最先进方法相当，同时显著降低计算开销并减少可训练参数数量。

Conclusion: PAT 有效解决了现有 PEFT 方法忽视推理效率的问题，在保持与 SOTA 相当微调性能的同时，实现了更低的计算开销和更高的参数效率，为点云 Transformer 的高效微调提供了新方向。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has recently emerged as a pivotal research direction for adapting pre-trained point cloud transformers to diverse downstream tasks. Although existing methods achieve excellent fine-tuning performance with high parameter efficiency, they ignore inference efficiency. To tackle this problem, a novel PEFT method termed position anchor tuning (PAT) is proposed in this paper. As multi-head attention (MHA) and feed-forward network (FFN) are computation-heavy blocks in pre-trained transformers, PAT decreases their computational cost through token aggregation-expansion pairs. Each pair comprises a token aggregation module (TAM) and a token expansion module (TEM). For MHA and FFN blocks, TAMs extract representative tokens from their input tokens based on position anchors in 3D space. These extracted tokens, rather than the original input tokens, are processed by the blocks, thereby reducing the number of tokens involved in computation. Then, TEMs propagate the learned representations back to the original input tokens. Since TAMs are solely responsible for capturing task-specific representations, base-sharing low-rank adaptation (BSLoRA) is further introduced to enable them to learn such representations effectively with only a small number of trainable parameters. Extensive experiments on widely used benchmarks demonstrate that PAT performs comparably to state-of-the-art methods while incurring significantly lower computational overhead and fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [27] [Finder: Agentic Closed-Loop Object Finding for Embodied Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18058)
*Shixiong Xu,Zhiyuan Chen,Song Ding,Rui Luo,Xiaowei Liang,Dongxu Miao,Zhiying Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Finder 是一个用于具身物体接地的 agentic 闭环找物原语，它通过类型化循环状态将规划、证据收集、候选验证与接受/继续/中止控制链接起来，在证据不完整或歧义时重定向感知，在 Habitat/HM3D 与现实 RGB-D 物体检索任务中相较强基线平均 1m 成功率提升 15.75 个点，并可迁移到顺序物体接地和具身物体中心问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将物体搜索与在线探索耦合（在相关观测已被捕获时代价高昂），要么以静态、一次性方式查询预构建的开放词汇地图和场景图，缺乏对不完整或歧义证据的动态重定向能力。因此需要一种闭环、可迭代的找物原语来提升具身接地的效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 Finder，一个 agentic 闭环物体寻找原语。它不把接地视为对固定场景表示的被动检索，而是维护一个类型化循环状态，将查询条件规划、范围化证据收集、候选验证以及接受/继续/中止控制连接起来；当证据不完整或歧义时，循环会重定向后续的感知与比较，而非仅返回检索得分最高的物体。

Result: 在开放词汇具身物体检索（Habitat/HM3D 及真实世界 RGB-D 场景）中，Finder 相较强基线的平均 1m 成功率提升 15.75 个点；同一原语迁移到顺序物体接地和具身物体中心问答时，在无需修改内部接地协议的情况下提升空间与时间定位能力。

Conclusion: Finder 通过闭环、可迭代的 agentic 找物原语有效解决了静态一次性检索在证据不完整或歧义时的缺陷，显著提升具身物体检索成功率，并展现出对顺序接地与物体中心问答等下游任务的良好可迁移性。

Abstract: Finding the object referred to by language in a partially observed 3D scene is a core capability for embodied agents. Existing approaches either couple object search with online exploration, which can be costly when relevant observations have already been captured, or query pre-built open-vocabulary maps and scene graphs in a static, one-shot fashion. We present Finder, an agentic closed-loop object-finding primitive for embodied grounding. Instead of treating grounding as passive retrieval from a fixed scene representation, Finder maintains a typed loop state that links query-conditioned planning, scoped evidence gathering, candidate verification, and accept/continue/abort control. When evidence is incomplete or ambiguous, the loop can redirect subsequent perception and comparison rather than simply returning the top retrieved object. On open-vocabulary embodied Object Retrieval in Habitat/HM3D and real-world RGB-D scenes, Finder improves the averaged 1m success rate by 15.75 points over strong baselines. The same primitive also transfers to sequential object grounding and embodied object-centric question answering, improving spatial and temporal localization without changing the inner grounding protocol. Project page: https://finder-vln.github.io.

</details>


### [28] [GeoCueFormer: Geometry-Guided Wavelet Representation and Prediction-Cued Dual-Stage Decoder for Underwater Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18069)
*Xian Wu,Xinjin Li,Yiliu Xu,Yining Liu,Yong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoCueFormer，一种结合几何约束频域增强与预测引导细化的轻量级水下语义分割框架，在 SUIM 和 DUT 上分别达到 82.23% 和 73.04% mIoU 的 SOTA 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下语义分割面临光吸收与散射导致的颜色偏移、低对比度和模糊边界等严重视觉退化问题，使浅层细节特征不可靠。现有方法仅改进 RGB 特征聚合或边界预测，缺乏显式机制来区分结构相关细节与退化引起的响应，因此需要一个能有效隔离退化干扰的框架。

Method: 提出 GeoCueFormer 轻量级框架：(1) 对分层编码器特征进行分阶段小波增强，在补充浅层边界细节的同时保留深层结构语义；(2) 引入由深度信息导出的空间门控，将浅层频域增强限制在几何一致区域；(3) 采用预测引导的双阶段解码器进一步细化模糊的高分辨率特征。

Result: GeoCueFormer 在 SUIM 上取得 82.23% mIoU，在 DUT 上取得 73.04% mIoU。在可比的模型复杂度以及 SUIM 和 DUT 标准基准设置下，达到 SOTA 性能，同时保持良好的精度-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，区分结构细节与退化诱导干扰是更有效的水下分割策略。GeoCueFormer 通过几何约束频域增强与预测引导细化，成功将结构信息与退化响应分离，验证了该思路在水下场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Underwater semantic segmentation is essential for marine ecosystem monitoring, yet remains challenging due to severe visual degradation. Light absorption and scattering often lead to color shifts, low contrast, and blurred boundaries, making shallow detail features unreliable. Existing underwater segmentation methods improve RGB feature aggregation or boundary prediction, but still lack an explicit mechanism to distinguish structure-related details from degradation-induced responses. To address this limitation, we propose GeoCueFormer, a lightweight framework that combines geometry-constrained frequency enhancement with prediction-cued refinement. GeoCueFormer performs stage-specific wavelet enhancement on hierarchical encoder features to complement shallow boundary details while preserving deep structural semantics. A depth-derived spatial gate constrains shallow frequency enhancement toward geometry-consistent regions, and a prediction-cued dual-stage decoder further refines ambiguous high-resolution features. GeoCueFormer obtains 82.23% and 73.04% mIoU on SUIM and DUT, respectively. Under comparable model complexity and standard benchmark settings on SUIM and DUT, it achieves SOTA performance while maintaining a favorable accuracy-complexity trade-off. These results show that distinguishing structural details from degradation-induced interference is more effective for underwater segmentation.

</details>


### [29] [vidax: A Unified JAX Framework for Video Generative Models on Accelerator Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18077)
*Congyue Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: vidax 是一个开源的 JAX/Flax 视频生成推理引擎，提供零拷贝的 PyTorch 权重转换，使现代视频生成模型能够在 Cloud TPU 上生产级运行，覆盖 DiT、MoT、3D VAE、文本编码器与采样器，并集成张量并行、序列并行、TPU flash-attention 及逐层权重卸载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源视频生成模型几乎都以 PyTorch/CUDA 参考实现发布，导致 Cloud TPU pod 缺乏生产可用的推理路径；而 TPU 拥有大容量、高性价比的加速器内存池，非常适合长序列时空注意力计算，需要填补这一推理空白。

Method: 提出 vidax 框架：JAX/Flax 推理引擎与零拷贝 PyTorch-to-JAX 权重转换器；覆盖 Diffusion Transformers、omnimodal Mixture-of-Transformers、3D VAE、文本编码器与原生采样器；执行路径零 PyTorch 依赖；在单一 JAX sharding mesh 上统一 1D 张量并行与 DeepSpeed-Ulysses 序列并行；集成 TPU flash-attention 内核；实现逐层权重卸载以支持超过单设备内存的参考分辨率。

Result: 在 TPU v4-8 硬件上基准测试了编译时间、延迟和峰值内存利用率，并记录了 checkpoint 转换过程中发现的真实数值 bug；vidax 已开源，作为 JAX 与 TPU 视频生成研究的基线。

Conclusion: vidax 为视频生成架构提供了生产就绪的 TPU 推理路径，填补了开源生态在非 CUDA 平台上的空白，并为后续 JAX/TPU 视频生成研究奠定了可复现的工程基线。

Abstract: Open-source video generative models ship almost exclusively as PyTorch/CUDA reference implementations. This leaves Cloud TPU pods without a production-ready inference path, despite offering large, cost-effective accelerator memory pools ideal for long-sequence spatiotemporal attention. We present vidax, an open-source JAX/Flax inference engine and zero-copy PyTorch-to-JAX weight translator for modern video generation architectures. vidax covers a diverse set of spatiotemporal models --- including Diffusion Transformers, omnimodal Mixture-of-Transformers, 3D VAEs, text encoders, and native samplers --- with zero PyTorch dependency in the execution path. The framework unifies 1D tensor parallelism with DeepSpeed-Ulysses sequence parallelism on a single JAX sharding mesh, integrates TPU flash-attention kernels, and implements per-layer weight offloading to support reference resolutions that exceed single-device memory. We benchmark compile times, latency, and peak memory utilization on TPU v4-8 hardware, and document real-world numerical bugs surfaced during checkpoint translation. vidax is released open-source as a baseline for JAX and TPU video generation research.

</details>


### [30] [Mask 2D-3D: Adaptive Dual-Masked Autoencoder Network for Image-to-Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18088)
*Zhixin Cheng,Jiacheng Deng,Xiaotian Yin,Baoqun Yin,Richang Hong,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ID-MAE框架，结合基于相似度的强化学习掩码策略（SRLM），通过跨模态相似度与强化学习自适应掩码信息位置来缩小模态差异，在图像-点云配准任务上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-点云配准的无检测（detection-free）方法容易因域/模态差异、特征提取器敏感性有限以及非重叠区域的存在而产生错误对应；标准MAE的随机掩码可能因相机视角有限而遗漏关键区域，降低配准效果。

Method: 提出跨模态双MAE框架（ID-MAE）并设计基于相似度的强化学习掩码策略（SRLM）。该方法利用跨模态相似度与强化学习自适应地掩码信息丰富的位置，缩小模态差距；在特征提取阶段通过强制表示一致性来增强跨模态表示学习，从而获得更可靠的2D-3D对应估计。

Result: 在RGB-D Scenes v2和7-Scenes两个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在图像-点云配准任务上达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能。

Conclusion: 通过自适应掩码和跨模态表示一致性约束，ID-MAE有效缓解了域/模态差异与非重叠区域带来的错误对应问题，显著提升了图像-点云配准的准确性与可靠性。

Abstract: Detection-free methods for image-to-point cloud registration are prone to erroneous correspondences caused by domain and modality discrepancies, limited sensitivity of feature extractors, and the presence of non-overlapping regions. The Masked Autoencoder (MAE) has shown strong performance in visual representation for images and point clouds. It may be helpful to apply this approach to image-to-point cloud registration, a task that requires unified feature extraction and accurate cross-modal correspondences. Standard MAE's random masking may overlook key regions due to limited camera views, reducing registration effectiveness. To address this, we propose the Intermodal Dual-MAE Framework (ID-MAE) with a Similarity-based RL Masking Strategy (SRLM), which adaptively masks informative positions by leveraging cross-modal similarity and reinforcement learning, thus narrowing the modality gap. Our method enhances cross-modal representation learning by enforcing representation consistency during feature extraction, thereby enabling more reliable 2D-3D correspondence estimation. Experiments on RGB-D Scenes v2 and 7-Scenes benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in image-to-point cloud registration.

</details>


### [31] [Aligned Consensus Teaching for Label-Efficient Oriented Object Detection in Weakly-Aligned Visible-Infrared Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18124)
*Qi Ming,Xiaxin Yuan,Jiahuan Zhou,Jiangmeng Li,Xudong Zhao,Zhanchao Huang,Juan Fang,Shaoguang Huang,Aleksandra Pizurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Aligned Consensus Teacher (ACT)，首个面向图像对级别标注设置的半监督可见光-红外目标检测框架，通过循环一致性对齐、跨模态共识均值教师和文本引导实例增广，大幅降低标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外目标检测（VIOD）依赖昂贵的双模态标注。将其拓展至半监督学习存在图像对级别设置的三大难点：(i) 标注框过少导致跨模态对齐不稳；(ii) 自训练中分支漏检产生的伪标签错误会累积；(iii) 标注预算下降时尾类标注严重稀缺。因此需要一种能在此设置下高效利用少量标注的框架。

Method: 提出 ACT 框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) Cycle-Consistent Region Alignment (CRA)，结合循环一致性与稀疏锚点，并采用可靠性加权的区域匹配实现稳健跨模态对齐；(2) Cross-Modal Consensus Mean-Teacher (CMC-MT)，在保持成对视图下形成共识伪标签，以恢复分支漏检并监督未标注图像对；(3) Text-Guided Cross-Modal Instance Augmentation (TG-CMIA)，利用视觉-语言场景先验合成尾类实例对，同时保持 RGB-IR 偏移。

Result: 在 DroneVehicle 和 VEDAI 数据集上，ACT 在不同标注比例下均取得一致提升。在 DroneVehicle 上仅用 10% 标注图像对时，ACT 达到同检测器全监督下 mAP 的 94.3%。

Conclusion: ACT 是首个研究图像对级别半监督 VIOD 的框架，有效解决了标注稀缺下的对齐不稳、伪标签误差累积和尾类稀缺问题，显著降低标注成本并接近全监督性能，代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: Visible-infrared object detection (VIOD) detects objects with oriented bounding boxes from paired visible and infrared images. Existing methods depend on costly dual-modality annotations. Semi-supervised learning can reduce this burden, but extending it from single-modal detection to VIOD is challenging. In the practical image-pair-level setting considered here, only a few pairs are labeled in both modalities, while the rest are completely unlabeled. This limited supervision creates three challenges: (i) too few labeled boxes for robust cross-modal alignment; (ii) pseudo-label errors caused by branch-wise misses accumulate during self-training; and (iii) tail-class annotations become critically scarce as the labeling budget decreases. We propose Aligned Consensus Teacher (ACT) for label-efficient VIOD in this setting. Its Cycle-Consistent Region Alignment (CRA) combines cycle consistency and sparse anchors with reliability-weighted regional matching. Cross-Modal Consensus Mean-Teacher (CMC-MT) forms consensus pseudo labels under pair-preserving views to recover branch-wise misses and supervise unlabeled pairs. Text-Guided Cross-Modal Instance Augmentation (TG-CMIA) uses a vision-language scene prior to compose tail-class instance pairs while preserving RGB--IR offsets. To the best of our knowledge, ACT is the first framework to study semi-supervised VIOD under this image-pair-level setting. Experiments on DroneVehicle and VEDAI show consistent gains across annotation ratios. With 10\% labeled pairs on DroneVehicle, ACT reaches 94.3\% of the mAP obtained by the same detector under full supervision. Code and models will be available on GitHub to facilitate future work.

</details>


### [32] [PRISM: Predictive Representation of Interaction Style and Motion for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18125)
*Bo-Han Chen,Hiromu Taketsugu,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM 框架通过被动观察人类间交互，推断行人的交互特质并编码为连续有序潜在空间，结合时间稳定性评分辅助机器人导航，在随机人群模拟中降低了碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在互动前会观察他人并调整行为，但现有机器人在人群中的导航主要依赖观测到的几何状态，忽略了个体在交互倾向上的差异。作者希望隐式地利用这些个体差异，以改善社会导航性能。

Method: 提出 PRISM 框架：使用经过 Rank-N-Contrast 损失训练的 transformer 编码器，将人类轨迹编码到连续有序潜在空间，以推断交互特质；并将每个推断出的特质与一个时间稳定性评分配对，提供给导航策略使用。

Result: 在随机人群模拟中，PRISM 相比仅使用几何信息的基线降低了碰撞率，并在导航时间和路径长度指标上取得小幅提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，被动潜在特质推断在动态人群的社会导航中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Humans often observe others before interacting and adjust their behavior accordingly. Robot navigation in crowds, however, often represents pedestrians mainly by observed geometric states, leaving individual differences in interaction tendencies implicit. We propose PRISM (Predictive Representation of Interaction Style and Motion), a framework that infers interaction traits from passive observations of human-human interactions. PRISM encodes human trajectories into a continuous ordinal latent space with a transformer encoder trained by Rank-N-Contrast loss, and pairs each inferred trait with a temporal-stability score supplied to the navigation policy. In randomized crowd simulations, PRISM reduces collision rates over the geometry-only baseline and yields small improvements in navigation-time and path-length metrics. These results suggest the utility of passive latent-trait inference for social navigation in dynamic crowds.

</details>


### [33] [MCLC-NET: Multimodal Continual Learning for Leaf Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18129)
*Ruchi Bhatt,Pratibha Kumari,Shreya Bansal,Vedant Agnihotri,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Mukesh Saini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MCLC-NET，一个面向叶片计数的多模态持续学习框架，并配套构建了真实世界多模态叶片计数数据集 MMLC（用于领域增量场景）。实验表明该方法在三种任务顺序下均优于现有方法，取得最低平均均方误差（AMSE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶片计数对植物表型分析和作物估产至关重要，但现有方法多依赖 RGB 图像，易受遮挡和光照变化影响；深度与热成像等额外模态可提供互补信息，但多模态叶片计数研究不足。同时，现有方法大多假设所有训练数据一次性可得，与真实农业场景中数据随时间和多来源逐步采集的实际情况不符，因此需要同时解决多模态融合与持续学习两大挑战。

Method: 提出 MCLC-NET，一个多模态持续学习框架，采用基于记忆（memory-based）的策略，通过记忆缓冲区保留先前任务的重要样本，从而顺序学习各任务以缓解灾难性遗忘。同时引入 MMLC 数据集，包含 RGB、深度和热成像图像，覆盖不同作物类型和不同环境条件，并按领域增量场景（DIS）组织为作物序（crop-wise）、时间序（time-wise）和混合序（mixed）三种任务排序。

Result: 在三个随机种子上取平均的实验结果表明，MCLC-NET 在三种任务排序下均一致优于现有方法，分别取得最低 AMSE 0.675±0.027（crop-wise）、0.542±0.069（time-wise）和 0.745±0.057（mixed）。

Conclusion: 通过多模态信息与基于记忆的持续学习相结合，MCLC-NET 有效提升了真实场景下叶片计数的鲁棒性和性能，并超越了现有方法，同时 MMLC 为后续多模态持续学习研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Leaf counting is an important task in plant phenotyping for monitoring plant growth and estimating crop yield. Most existing methods rely on RGB images, but their performance is often affected by occlusion, lighting variations, and other real-world challenges. Additional modalities, such as depth and thermal images, can provide useful complementary information. However, multimodal leaf counting remains underexplored. Also, many existing methods assume that all training data are available simultaneously, which is impractical in real agricultural settings, where data is collected over time from multiple sources.
