We formalize a different design target, structured unpredictability, as conditional dependence between an output and a persistent hidden state beyond what an observer can infer from the transcript. A selection layer updates a low-dimensional style-and-attention state from a capacity-limited stream, generates several responses with a fixed base model, and selects for novelty and state affinity. Evaluation uses scripted sequences of independent prompt turns: the base model receives the current turn and rendered state, but not the preceding dialogue; cross-turn dependence resides in the wrapper state and response selector. A synthetic implementation validates the pipeline and matches four prospectively hash-frozen divergence features at point level. In the final real-model grid (mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit; 56 sequences per arm), the mechanism increased lexical novelty over the low-variance and consistency-only controls by 0.073 and 0.023, respectively. Its stylometric-consistency contrast with novelty-matched sampling was equivalent to zero under the registered smallest-effect rule, so the joint novelty-consistency criterion failed. The original two-part accumulation criterion also failed; a revised final-grid contrast, frozen after the powered grid, found higher consistency than the memory-reset ablation (0.028, 95% CI [0.018,0.039]), but does not establish path dependence. Twin separation was not established (0.003, 95% CI [-0.011,0.019]); the mean curve's saturating curvature matched the frozen prediction, which without separation does not support path dependence. Probe-level capability equivalence held within +/-0.10 on a near-ceiling battery, while output quality was not evaluated. All outcomes are machine-scored; no claims about perceived mind or consciousness are tested.

</details>


### [169] [The Death of Schema Linking? Text-to-SQL in the Age of Well-Reasoned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07702)
*Karime Maamari,Fadhil Abubaker,Daniel Jaroslawicz,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 Text-to-SQL 中的 schema linking 环节，发现新一代大语言模型即使面对大量无关 schema 元素也能有效利用相关部分，因此提出完全跳过 schema linking、改用增强/选择/纠正等技术，在 BIRD 基准上以 71.83% 准确率排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schema linking 是 Text-to-SQL 的关键步骤，但不完美的 schema linking 常会遗漏生成准确查询所必需的列，反而损害最终效果。作者希望借助最新一代大语言模型的能力，重新审视并解决这一矛盾。

Method: 当数据库 schema 能放入模型上下文窗口时，完全放弃 schema linking，将全部 schema 元素直接交给 LLM；同时采用增强（augmentation）、选择（selection）和纠正（correction）等技术来提升 pipeline 的准确率，而不是过滤上下文信息。

Result: 该方法在 BIRD 基准测试上取得 71.83% 的准确率，排名第一。

Conclusion: 在新一代大语言模型时代，schema linking 可以被省略，通过保留完整 schema 并运用增强、选择与纠正等策略，能有效避免因过滤必要列而引入的错误，从而获得更优的 Text-to-SQL 性能。

Abstract: Schema linking is a crucial step in Text-to-SQL pipelines. Its goal is to retrieve the relevant tables and columns of a target database for a user's query while disregarding irrelevant ones. However, imperfect schema linking can often exclude required columns needed for accurate query generation. In this work, we revisit schema linking when using the latest generation of large language models (LLMs). We find empirically that newer models are adept at utilizing relevant schema elements during generation even in the presence of large numbers of irrelevant ones. As such, our Text-to-SQL pipeline entirely forgoes schema linking in cases where the schema fits within the model's context window in order to minimize issues due to filtering required schema elements. Furthermore, instead of filtering contextual information, we highlight techniques such as augmentation, selection, and correction, and adopt them to improve the accuracy of our Text-to-SQL pipeline. Our approach ranks first on the BIRD benchmark achieving an accuracy of 71.83%.

</details>


### [170] [MIRAGE: How Conversation State Shapes Historical Evidence Use in Multimodal Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19059)
*Yu Liu,Wenxiao Zhang,Cheng Hu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Xinyu Wang,Jin B. Hong,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIRAGE 是一个受控评估框架，用于研究多模态个人智能体在对话状态变化下对历史证据的使用情况，发现证据使用应基于状态变异来评估，而非仅凭结果正确性推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型智能体作为长期任务的个人助手时，其效用取决于连续性——必须检索并使用跨对话、文件和工作区状态的早期证据。但智能体在历史访问退化时仍可能生成看似合理的答案，导致仅基于结果的评估高估了其真实的证据使用能力。

Method: 提出 MIRAGE（Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation），在固定证据对象、问题和评分标准的前提下，仅改变对话状态，评估智能体能否判断可回答性、恢复正确来源并据此作答。在七个前沿与开源多模态骨干模型上进行受控实验。

Result: 1) 压缩前深度与压缩后延续形成两个截然不同且非单调的失效机制，而非单一退化曲线；2) 开源模型严重依赖上下文连续性，当来源失效时不情愿自发切换到工具中介检索；3) 检索压力能改善工具顺从模型在深层压缩前状态下的来源归因，但在压缩后（存储证据已退化）会持续退化。

Conclusion: 历史证据使用应在状态变异下进行评估，而不应仅从结果正确性推断。该研究为多模态个人智能体的连续性能力提供了更严格、更细粒度的评估视角。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents are increasingly used as personal assistants for long-running tasks. Their utility depends on continuity: agents must retrieve and use earlier evidence across dialogue, files, and workspace state. However, agents can generate plausible answers even when access to that history has degraded, causing outcome-only evaluation to overestimate true evidence use. We present MIRAGE (Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation), a controlled study of historical evidence use under conversation-state variation in multimodal personal agents. MIRAGE holds evidence objects, questions, and scoring fixed while varying only conversation state, and evaluates whether an agent can determine answerability, recover the correct source, and answer from it. Across seven frontier and open-weight multimodal backbones, we find that: 1) pre-compaction depth and post-compaction continuation form distinct, non-monotonic failure regimes rather than a single degradation curve; 2) open-weight models rely heavily on context continuity and are reluctant to spontaneously switch to tool-mediated retrieval when provenance fails; and 3) retrieval pressure improves source attribution in deep pre-compaction states for tool-compliant models, but consistently regresses after compaction, where stored evidence has already degraded. These findings show that historical evidence use should be evaluated under state variation, rather than inferred from outcome-only correctness.

</details>


### [171] [Reading Between the Lines: Can LLMs Discover the Question Behind the Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19070)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"问题考古学"这一评测任务，即从完整文本中推断其最初的"起源问题"（genesis question），并构建数据集评估模型对作者意图的理解能力，发现当前LLM已超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务存在不足：问题生成（question generation）只针对任何合理问题，话语框架（discourse frameworks）只建模话语层面的行为，均无法衡量模型对作者创作意图的深层把握。因此需要一个新的评测任务来衡量模型推断文本"起源问题"的能力。

Method: 提出"问题考古学"评测任务，构建一个包含委托撰写文本及其原始研究问题和干扰项的专属数据集；在多种模型上评测，包括闭源模型（Gemini Flash、Gemini Pro）、开源模型（Mistral、Qwen）以及BERT类模型。

Result: 评测显示该任务上模型取得显著进展，新版本模型优于旧版本；BERT类模型表现较差；值得注意的是，当前LLM在该任务上超越了人类表现，表明其对作者意图具有高级理解能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个新的评测框架和具有挑战性的基准，LLM在推断作者意图方面已超过人类，这对AI在需要细致解读人类沟通的任务中具有重要应用意义。

Abstract: This paper introduces ``question archaeology'', a specific evaluation task focused on inferring the single, authentic "genesis question" that motivated the creation of a complete text. Distinct from question generation, which targets any plausible question, or discourse frameworks that model utterance-level acts, our task assesses a model's grasp of authorial intent. We present a new dataset of commissioned texts paired with their original research questions and plausible distractors. Our evaluation of both proprietary models, like Gemini Flash and Pro, as well as open source models like Mistral and Qwen, reveals significant progress in this task, with the newer versions outperforming the earlier ones, while BERT-based models performed poorly. Notably, our findings indicate that current LLMs surpass human performance on this task, suggesting advanced understanding of authorial intent. This capability has important implications for AI's role in tasks requiring nuanced interpretation of human communication. Our work thus provides a new framework and a challenging benchmark for future models.

</details>


### [172] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Biomedical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19071)
*Claudiu Creanga,Teodor Marchitan,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文基准评测了多种NLP模型（含掩码语言模型、混合架构及主流大语言模型），在SNPPhenA语料库上完成三项任务（句级、摘要级、关联强度分类），发现OpenAI O1与Gemini 2.0 Pro等专有大模型取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生物医学文献中提取SNP-表型关联对基因组学研究至关重要但极具挑战，需要系统评测现代NLP模型（尤其大语言模型）在此任务上的能力并确立性能基准。

Method: 在SNPPhenA语料库上对多种NLP模型进行基准测试，涵盖掩码语言模型（MLMs）、混合架构以及最新的LLMs（Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列、Qwen、Mistral），并设计三个任务：句级分类、摘要级分类和关联强度分类；同时采用少样本学习和微调等不同范式。

Result: OpenAI O1在非微调的句级分类中通过少样本学习达到SOTA（F1 0.89），并为摘要级分类建立新SOTA（F1 0.82）。关联强度分类难度较大，微调后的Gemini 2.0 Pro取得最佳（F1 0.60），这是该任务首次LLM评测。专有LLM在少样本（O1）或微调（Gemini 2.0 Pro）设置下显著优于其他模型。

Conclusion: 现代大语言模型在基因组知识提取任务中展现出强大能力，专有LLM在少样本或微调设置下优势显著，验证了LLM用于生物医学文献SNP-表型关联抽取的潜力。

Abstract: Extracting SNP-phenotype associations from biomedical literature is vital but challenging. We benchmarked diverse NLP models, including MLMs, hybrid architectures, and state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemini 2.0, OpenAI O-series, Qwen, Mistral), on the SNPPhenA corpus across three tasks: sentence-level, abstract-level, and association strength classification. OpenAI O1 achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) results using few-shot learning for non-finetuned sentence-level classification (F1 0.89) and established a new SOTA for abstract-level classification (F1 0.82). Association strength classification proved difficult, though fine-tuned Gemini 2.0 Pro performed best (F1 0.60) in the first LLM evaluation of this task. Proprietary LLMs, especially in few-shot (O1) or fine-tuned (Gemini 2.0 Pro) settings, significantly outperformed other models. These findings confirm the power of modern LLMs for genomic knowledge extraction.

</details>


### [173] [Safety-Flag: A Unified Benchmark for the Reliability and Calibration of LLM Content Moderators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19072)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Safety-Flag 基准，将七个主流安全基准统一为 "标记/不标记" 协议，并系统评估了六个通用 LLM 和四个专用安全守卫在错误方向、概率校准和基于置信度的错误排序三个可靠性维度上的表现，发现聚合准确率会掩盖重要的可靠性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 用于内容审核的评估大多只报告单个基准上的聚合准确率，无法揭示模型的错误方向（误报 vs 漏报）、置信度校准质量以及置信度能否可靠地用于人工复核排序等关键可靠性问题。

Method: 作者构建 Safety-Flag 基准，将 BeaverTails、XSTest、Ethics、WildGuard、Aegis、ToxiChat、ToxiGen 七个基准合并为统一的标记/不标记（flag / do-not-flag）协议；发布逐条决策与置信度分数，覆盖六个通用 LLM、四个专用守卫及三个参考模型；从错误方向、概率校准、基于置信度的错误排序三个维度测量审核可靠性，并尝试用单温度缩放进行校准修正。

Result: 三个可靠性维度之间常常不一致。聚合准确率掩盖了错误方向：一个模型将 85% 的良性内容误标，另一个则漏掉 54% 的有害内容。六个通用模型均过度自信，按每模型拟合一个温度可在不改变预测标签或置信度排序的前提下将校准误差降低 2.8–6.0 倍。基于置信度的弃权对每个模型都能降低选择性风险，但收益取决于置信度对错误的排序能力。专用守卫误报更少、校准更好，但部分在其声明覆盖范围之外漏报率更高。

Conclusion: 仅看聚合准确率不足以评估内容审核模型的可靠性；Safety-Flag 通过统一的标记协议和多维度可靠性度量揭示了不同模型在误报、漏报与校准上的显著差异，并公开了基准、固定条目列表、评估代码、逐条输出及排行榜，以推动更全面的审核可靠性评估。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for content moderation, but most evaluations still report aggregate accuracy on individual benchmarks. We introduce Safety-Flag, which places seven widely used safety benchmarks (BeaverTails, XSTest, Ethics, WildGuard, Aegis, ToxiChat, and ToxiGen) into a single balanced flag / do-not-flag protocol. We release item-level decisions and confidence scores for six general-purpose LLMs and four dedicated guards, together with three reference models, evaluated on the same items. Safety-Flag measures three dimensions of moderator reliability: error direction, probability calibration, and confidence-based error ranking for human review. They often disagree. Aggregate accuracy does not reveal error direction: one model flags $85\%$ of benign content, whereas another misses $54\%$ of harmful content. All six general-purpose models are overconfident; fitting one temperature per model reduces calibration error by $2.8$--$6.0\times$ without changing predicted labels or confidence ordering. Confidence-based abstention lowers selective risk for every model, although the gains depend on how well confidence ranks errors. Dedicated guards produce fewer false alarms and are better calibrated, but several have higher miss rates outside their documented coverage. We release the benchmark, fixed item lists, evaluation code, per-item model outputs, and leaderboard at: https://github.com/yibo-hu-lab/safety-flag-benchmark.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Rényi Tracking Bounds for Langevin Dynamics with Moving Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17577)
*Yuchen Xin,Jingxin Zhan,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究目标分布随时间变化的Langevin扩散与Langevin蒙特卡洛（LMC）算法，在对数Sobolev不等式（LSI）条件下，推导出跟踪当前目标的非渐近Rényi散度保证，并将结果应用于基于连续Moreau包络的非光滑采样方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的采样问题常涉及随时间变化的目标分布（如在线学习、变分推断、数据流场景），而已有Langevin算法分析大多假设目标分布固定不变。因此需要为非平稳目标下的Langevin动力学建立非渐近收敛/跟踪理论。

Method: 在目标分布满足对数Sobolev不等式（LSI）的前提下，对连续时间Langevin扩散及其离散化（LMC）进行分析，利用Rényi散度度量算法分布与当前目标分布之间的差距，推导非渐近跟踪界。并将该框架应用于基于连续Moreau包络的非光滑采样，显式给出平滑参数与步长的选取以及相应的复杂度界。

Result: 在LSI条件下，获得了Langevin扩散及其离散化的非渐近Rényi散度跟踪保证；针对基于连续Moreau包络的非光滑采样方案，给出了显式的平滑参数、步长选择及复杂度界。这是首个针对离散目标更新的Langevin动力学的非渐近Rényi散度跟踪界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Langevin动力学在一系列随时间变化目标下的Rényi散度跟踪理论，并展示了其在实际非光滑采样问题中的可用性，为时变目标采样的理论分析与算法设计提供了基础。

Abstract: We study Langevin diffusion and Langevin Monte Carlo (LMC) when the target distribution changes over time. Under a log-Sobolev inequality (LSI), we derive non-asymptotic Rényi-divergence guarantees for tracking the current target. The framework covers continuous-time Langevin diffusion and its discretizations. We then apply the results to nonsmooth sampling based on successive Moreau envelopes. For this scheme, we give explicit choices of the smoothing parameters and step sizes, together with corresponding complexity bounds. To our knowledge, these are the first non-asymptotic Rényi-divergence tracking bounds for Langevin dynamics with discrete target updates.

</details>


### [175] [Stability-Constrained Approximation in Spline KANs: Exact Layer Balancing and Budget-Compatible Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17619)
*Aleksander Tankman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究深度样条叠加网络在硬性逐层Lipschitz预算约束下的逼近问题，围绕两个核心量——给定深度分解的"分解稳定性复杂度"与离散化算子的"预算兼容逼近复杂度"展开，给出了有限深度对角平衡的精确最优解、保预算的样条离散化定理及其反向下界，并证明层误差在复合下不会抵消，从而上界中随深度线性积累的误差并非证明假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度样条叠加网络在逼近阶数与跨深度稳定性之间存在张力。既有工作对硬性逐层Lipschitz预算下的逼近行为缺乏系统性刻画，尤其缺少：(1)有限深度对角平衡问题的精确解；(2)保预算离散化的定量构造；(3)说明上界误差随深度线性累积是否为证明技巧导致的假象，而非网络固有现象。

Method: (1) 对固定非负包络矩阵链，精确求解有限深度对角平衡问题，给出最优均匀逐层预算为 $\|M_{L-1}\cdots M_0\|_{\infty\to\infty}^{1/L}$，并用显式单遍最小化器实现，完整处理矩形层、退化与非取到情形。(2) 构造保预算（到受控松弛）的样条离散化定理，给出显式网格阈值；反面地，对精确保预算的线性样条值算子，在同时受一阶与三阶导数范数约束的函数类上证明预算兼容的极小极大下界。(3) 证明该类中每个算子都存在一个稳定深度-$L$ 塔，实现累积误差的常数比例，从而证明层误差在复合下不必然抵消。

Result: (1) 最优均匀逐层预算等于包络矩阵乘积的 $\infty\to\infty$ 算子范数的 $1/L$ 次幂，可由显式单遍最小化器达到；该最优值可任意大于网络自身Lipschitz常数，因为取包络会破坏符号相消。(2) 得到保预算样条离散化的构造性定理及显式网格阈值，并在一阶+三阶导数范数同时约束的类上建立预算兼容的极小极大下界。(3) 证明存在稳定的深度-$L$ 塔可达到累积误差的常数比例，即上界中误差随深度线性累积是真实的，而非证明过程中的伪影。

Conclusion: 在硬性逐层Lipschitz预算下，逼近理论可围绕分解稳定性复杂度与预算兼容逼近复杂度统一展开。有限深度对角平衡有精确解且可由显式程序实现；包络取法可能破坏符号相消从而使最优预算远大于网络自身Lipschitz常数。保预算离散化可实现到受控松弛，且在强化的导数约束类下存在匹配的极小极大下界。由于层误差复合后不抵消，误差随深度线性累积是内在的，排除了常用的缩放逃逸路径。

Abstract: Deep spline superposition networks face a tension between approximation order and stability across depth. We study approximation under a hard layerwise Lipschitz budget, and organise it around two quantities: the factorisation stability complexity of a given deep factorisation, and the budget-compatible approximation complexity of a discretisation operator.
  First, we solve exactly the finite-depth diagonal balancing problem for a fixed chain of nonnegative envelope matrices: the optimal uniform layer budget equals $\|M_{L-1}\cdots M_0\|_{\infty\to\infty}^{1/L}$, attained by an explicit one-pass minimiser, for rectangular layers, with a complete treatment of degeneracies and non-attainment. The optimum can be arbitrarily larger than the Lipschitz constant of the network itself, because passing to envelopes destroys sign cancellation.
  Second, we give a constructive spline discretisation theorem preserving the budget up to a controlled slack, with an explicit grid threshold. Conversely, for linear spline-valued operators that preserve the budget exactly, we prove budget-compatible minimax lower bounds on classes constrained simultaneously in the first and third derivative norms -- a constraint pair that is forced by the problem and that rules out the usual scaling escapes.
  Finally, we show that the corresponding layer errors need not cancel under composition: for every operator of the class there is a stable depth-$L$ tower realising a constant fraction of the accumulated error, so the linear-in-depth accumulation of the upper bound is not a proof artefact.

</details>


### [176] [METALICA: METAdynamics and repLICA exchange for enhanced diffusion sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17823)
*Alireza Omidi,Jiajun He,Jörg Gsponer,Saifuddin Syed*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: METALICA 在预训练扩散模型上实现基于副本交换的元动力学采样，通过沿集体变量累积偏置势并重加权，将并行性放在扩散时间层级上，从而能生成长马尔可夫链样本，用于发现蛋白质构象空间中的稀有状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多蛋白质通过构象状态间跃迁发挥功能，但稀有状态很少被训练于平衡系综的扩散模型采样到，因此需要更高效的采样方法来发现这些稀有事件。

Method: METALICA 在预训练扩散模型上通过副本交换（Replica Exchange）实现元动力学（Metadynamics）：沿集体变量（CV）累积偏置势，通过偏置采样使新样本远离先前样本，再将样本重加权回无偏分布。每个扩散层级持有一个副本，通过副本间通信形成马尔可夫链，并随偏置增长原地精炼，是 Sequential Monte Carlo（顺序蒙特卡洛）在批次粒子并行化上的对偶方案。

Result: 在已知自由能的双模态目标上验证了方法；并将其应用于蛋白质去折叠。在顺序控制无法生成任何去折叠结构的预算下，METALICA 成功填充了该势阱并解析出第二个自由能极小值。

Conclusion: METALICA 通过将并行性部署在扩散时间层级而非粒子批次上，能生成长链样本，准确度由运行长度而非可用内存决定，从而有效发现稀有事件（如蛋白质去折叠），优于顺序控制方法。

Abstract: Many proteins function through transitions between conformational states, yet rare states are rarely sampled by diffusion models trained on an equilibrium ensemble, demanding better sampling methods. We introduce METALICA, which implements Metadynamics on a pretrained diffusion model via Replica Exchange. It accumulates a bias potential along a Collective Variable, repels new samples from previous ones through biased sampling, and reweights samples onto the unbiased distribution. METALICA holds one replica per diffusion level, forming a Markov Chain that evolves through inter-replica communication and is refined in place as the bias grows. METALICA is the dual of sequential control, in which Sequential Monte Carlo parallelizes the sampler over a batch of particles. Parallelism over the levels of the diffusion-time schedule instead allows METALICA to generate samples from long chains, essential for the discovery of rare events, with accuracy set by run length rather than by the memory available. We validate on a bimodal target with known free energies, then apply METALICA to the unfolding of a protein. At a budget for which sequential control yields no unfolded structure, METALICA populates the basin and resolves a second free energy minimum.

</details>


### [177] [Sharp margin-based generalization bounds for realizable SVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17845)
*Steve Hanneke,Aryeh Kontorovich*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为精确齐次硬间隔支持向量机建立了有限样本的风险上界：数据可分时，误分类风险以 $\frac{C}{m}(K_m+\log(1/\delta))$ 为界，其中 $K_m$ 由最大训练半径与最小范数单位间隔分离超平面的范数平方决定，并以概率 $1-\delta$ 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典支持向量机的泛化界通常依赖覆盖数或复杂度测度，难以刻画精确硬间隔解的结构特性。作者希望通过一个确定性的"约束删除"问题，直接利用 KKT 表示与几何结构，得到无需贝叶斯条件的、依赖于 $K_m=r_m^2/\gamma_m^2$ 这一自然量的高概率风险界。

Method: 核心是一个确定性删除问题：对单位球内向量族，删除若干约束后，令 $u_B$ 为满足所有保留单位间隔约束且距原点最近的点；假设 $\|u_B\|^2\le k$ 且所有被删向量在 $u_B$ 下得分非正，则基数 $q$ 的删除集族规模不超过 $\exp(8k+2q)$。关键步骤是由 KKT 表示导出的精确恒等式，它将随机删除集下分离超平面的均方扩展转化为得分的加权和，从而强制存在一个坐标，其删除状态使两个条件均值产生足够大的分离；暴露该坐标能降低条件方差，控制分裂的二元熵。最后通过熵归纳得到删除计数，并用精确阶乘"幻影样本"恒等式将计数转化为所述高概率 SVM 界。

Result: 证明了存在通用数值常数 $C$，在 $\gamma_m>0$ 且把零得分计为错误时，有 $\Pp(\gamma_m>0,\ \Risk(u_m)>\frac{C}{m}(K_m+\log\frac1\delta))\le\delta$，其中 $K_m=r_m^2\|u_m\|^2=r_m^2/\gamma_m^2$。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了精确齐次硬间隔 SVM 的一个不依赖额外假设、以自然量 $K_m$ 表征的有限样本高概率风险界；方法上以确定性删除计数为驱动，借助 KKT 精确恒等式、熵归纳及幻影样本恒等式完成证明，绕开了传统的覆盖数途径。

Abstract: Let the exact homogeneous hard-margin support vector machine be trained on \(m\) independent observations from a Borel probability law on a real Hilbert space. We prove that, with score zero counted as an error, there is a universal numerical constant \(C\) such that \[
  \Pp\left(
  γ_m>0,\quad
  \Risk(u_m)>
  \frac{C}{m}
  \left(
  K_m+\log\frac1δ
  \right)
  \right)
  \le δ. \] Here \(γ_m\) is the empirical homogeneous margin, \(u_m\) is the exact minimum-norm unit-margin separator, \(r_m\) is the largest training radius, and \(K_m:=r_m^2\norm{u_m}^2=r_m^2/γ_m^2\) on \(\{γ_m>0\}\).
  The proof is driven by a deterministic deletion problem. Given vectors \(x_1,\ldots,x_n\) in the unit ball, delete a set \(B\) of constraints and let \(u_B\) be the closest point to the origin that satisfies every retained unit-margin constraint. Suppose that \(\norm{u_B}^2\le k\) and that every deleted vector has nonpositive score under \(u_B\). We prove that a family of such deletion sets of cardinality \(q\) has size at most \(\exp(8k+2q)\).
  The conceptual step is an exact identity obtained from the KKT representation of \(u_B\). For a random deletion set, the identity converts the mean squared spread of the separators into a weighted sum of score deficits. It therefore forces a coordinate whose deletion status separates the two conditional means by a quantitatively large amount. Revealing that coordinate decreases the conditional separator variance enough to control the binary entropy of the split. An entropy induction gives the deletion count, and an exact factorial ghost-sample identity converts that count into the stated high-probability SVM bound.

</details>


### [178] [Bracketing Uncertainty in Clustering Under the Manifold Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17892)
*Savik Kinger,Luciano Dyballa,Steven W. Zucker*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文基于流形假设，将聚类可分离性形式化为环境分离度与采样最大间隙之间的几何权衡，在互k近邻图上推导出分离保持与融合的阈值现象，并界定出一个聚类数不可识别的几何不确定区。作者提出流形聚类(MBC)方法，返回一个区间(bracket)来量化聚类数的不确定性，而非强行给出单一估计；实验表明许多真实数据正落于该不确定区，聚类数的模糊性往往内在于数据本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法强行输出单一聚类数，即便数据不支持唯一答案；流形假设下组件可分离性取决于环境分离度与采样最大间隙的几何权衡，但这一权衡在实践中很少被显式评估，导致方法过度承诺单一划分。

Method: 结合内在流形几何(体积增长与reach)与样本级量(填充距离与密度)，在互k近邻图上形式化分离阈值现象：偏移-填充比超过保守上阈值时组件分离保持，低于下阈值时组件融合；据此定义几何不确定区。进而提出流形聚类(MBC)，返回显式的bracket区间作为经验校准诊断，并对比扫参法(工程方法)与生成混合模型(模型方法)。

Result: MBC的bracket区间在单一分辨率受支持时变窄，多分辨率共存时变宽，无可分离结构时坍缩为一；实验发现许多真实数据集处于不确定区内，而非支持唯一清晰答案。

Conclusion: 聚类数目的模糊性往往是内在的，应当被量化而非强行消解；不强制单一估计而返回不确定性区间的MBC，能更忠实地反映数据本身的几何结构。

Abstract: The manifold hypothesis suggests a natural criterion for clustering: partition data according to the manifold component from which each point is drawn. Whether two components are separable depends on a geometric tradeoff: the ambient separation between components versus the largest gap in sampling. In practice, this tradeoff is rarely assessed explicitly, leading standard methods to over-commit to a single clustering assignment even when the data do not support a unique answer. We formalize this tradeoff by combining intrinsic manifold geometry (volume growth and reach) with sample-level quantities (fill distance and density), yielding a threshold phenomenon for mutual-$k$-nearest-neighbor graphs: when the offset-to-fill ratio exceeds a conservative upper threshold, component separation is preserved; below a lower threshold, components fuse. The gap between these thresholds defines a geometric uncertainty zone in which the number of clusters is not identifiable from the data. Nevertheless, conventional approaches still seek one: sweeping parameters (an engineering approach) or fitting a generative mixture model (a model-based approach). Rather than forcing a single estimate of the number of clusters, we propose Manifold-Based Clustering (MBC), which returns an explicit bracket interval to quantify the underlying data uncertainty. This bracket acts as an empirically calibrated diagnostic: it narrows when a single resolution is supported, widens when multiple resolutions coexist, and collapses to one when no separated structure is detectable. Empirically, we find that many real datasets lie within the uncertainty zone rather than admitting one clear answer. Our results suggest that ambiguity in cluster number is often intrinsic, and should be quantified rather than resolved.

</details>


### [179] [Generalized DCCQ: From Binary Quotients to Multinomial Simplex Geometry and Critical-Strip Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17899)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将离散复补商（DCCQ）框架从二项伯努利计数推广到多项计数组合，定义了 m 维概率单纯形上的多项 DCCQ 坐标映射，并证明其为实解析微分同胚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DCCQ 框架仅适用于二元伯努利计数（m=1），作者希望将其推广到多类别（m+1 类）的多项计数，以覆盖更高维度的概率单纯形，并刻画不同类别数下坐标的几何结构差异。

Method: 基于标准单纯形与对数比（log-ratio）坐标几何，对于 m≥2 定义完整的多项 DCCQ 坐标映射，并证明其为实解析微分同胚。此外给出 one-versus-rest 特化、三元坐标的整数格精确实现，以及其对数比的超几何表示。

Result: 二元基线 m=1 对应临界线坐标；三元情形 m=2 给出完整开临界带；更高阶多项模型额外保留 m-2 个实对比（real contrasts）。全文明确不主张任何零点位置定理或黎曼猜想的证明。

Conclusion: DCCQ 框架可自然推广至多项计数，通过实解析微分同胚将整数计数向量模去公缩放映射到概率单纯形的有理点，且不同 m 对应不同几何结构；作者谨慎声明该工作并不涉及黎曼猜想的证明。

Abstract: We extend the discrete complex complement quotient (DCCQ) framework from binary Bernoulli counts to multinomial count compositions. For m+1 categories, m is the number of independent probability degrees of freedom. Integer count vectors modulo common scaling determine rational points of the m-dimensional probability simplex. Building on standard simplex and log-ratio coordinate geometry, for m >= 2 we define the full multinomial DCCQ coordinate map and show that it is a real-analytic diffeomorphism The previously established binary baseline m=1 gives a critical-line coordinate, while the ternary case m=2 gives the full open critical strip; higher multinomial models retain m-2 additional real contrasts. We also give a one-versus-rest specialization, an exact integer-lattice realization of the ternary coordinate, and a hyperbolic representation of its log-ratio. No zero-location theorem or proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [180] [Preservation of Log-Concavity and Convergence of Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18118)
*Francesca Romana Crucinio,Sahani Pathiraja*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao（WFR）梯度流在采样问题中的收敛性，证明在强对数凹目标分布下WFR流保持强对数凹性，并导出对称KL散度的显式非渐近收敛速率，无需warm-start，且速率可加性分解为Wasserstein与Fisher-Rao贡献，证实了近期猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速从仅知归一化常数的概率分布中采样，超越Langevin动力学的收敛速度。WFR流结合Wasserstein输运与Fisher-Rao生灭动力学，被视为有前景的加速机制，但其理论收敛保证尚不完善（现有估计依赖warm-start），且缺乏强对数凹性保持等结构性质的支持。

Method: 研究WFR梯度流的收敛性，结合Wasserstein输运与Fisher-Rao生灭动力学。对于满足额外曲率条件的强对数凹目标分布，证明WFR流保持强对数凹性（对比Wasserstein流仅在Gauss情形具备此性质），并利用该性质推导对称Kullback-Leibler散度的显式非渐近收敛速率。

Result: 证明了在强对数凹（附加曲率条件）目标分布下，WFR流保持强对数凹性；导出了对称KL散度的显式非渐近收敛速率，且无需warm-start；收敛速率可加性分解为Wasserstein与Fisher-Rao贡献之和，从而在该设定下证实了近期猜想。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了更精细的收敛保证，并进一步发展了WFR梯度流用于采样与贝叶斯推断的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the convergence of Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao (WFR) gradient flows for sampling from probability distributions known up to a normalisation constant. By combining Wasserstein transport with Fisher-Rao birth-death dynamics, WFR flows balance exploration and selection. These flows have been recognised as a promising mechanism to accelerate convergence beyond Langevin dynamics. We show that for a class of strongly log-concave target distributions satisfying additional curvature conditions, WFR flows preserve strong log-concavity, in contrast to Wasserstein flows which enjoy this property only in the Gaussian setting. Exploiting this result, we derive explicit non-asymptotic convergence rates for the symmetrised Kullback-Leibler divergence, without requiring a warm-start as required in current estimates. In particular, we show that the convergence rate decomposes additively into Wasserstein and Fisher-Rao contributions, thereby confirming a recent conjecture within this setting. These results provide refined convergence guarantees and further develop the theoretical foundations of WFR gradient flows for sampling and Bayesian inference.

</details>


### [181] [Rank and computation of the pathlifting Jacobian of a DAG ReLU network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18682)
*Manon Verbockhaven*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文给出DAG ReLU网络路径提升（pathlifting）Jacobian矩阵秩的自包含证明，通过对隐节点数目做归纳，并借助骨架矩阵（skeleton matrix）将隐神经元转化为输出节点，从而在无需反向传播的情况下高效计算该Jacobian矩阵的秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径提升Jacobian矩阵的秩刻画了ReLU网络在其路径结构下的表达能力与几何性质，但现有理解缺乏简洁、自包含的证明；同时传统反向传播计算Jacobian开销较大，作者希望提供一个既证明秩、又能以更低计算成本高效计算该Jacobian的方法。

Method: 对网络隐节点数量做初等归纳；核心技巧是引入编码网络路径的稀疏"骨架矩阵"，并把其中一个隐神经元的表示转换为输出节点来变换矩阵表示。证明依赖一系列中间命题，这些命题把路径提升、其Jacobian、网络参数与骨架矩阵联系在一起，从而得出Jacobian秩的结论，并得到一种无需反向传播、实践中计算开销极低的Jacobian计算方法；并附Python模块实现各命题。

Result: 通过归纳与骨架矩阵变换，给出了DAG ReLU网络路径提升Jacobian秩的自包含证明，并提供无需反向传播的计算方法；实验在Python模块中对前馈网络量化了该方法相比传统反向传播在计算上的显著增益。

Conclusion: 借助骨架矩阵的初等归纳证明，路径提升Jacobian的秩可用一种计算上比反向传播更高效的方式求得，理论既给出了秩的结论，也提供了实用的快速计算方法，并通过实验验证了计算效率优势。

Abstract: This paper provides a self-contained proof of the rank of the pathlifting Jacobian of a DAG ReLU network by performing an induction on the network's number of hidden nodes. In fact, the induction is elementary, and the key recipe is to consider the skeleton matrix of the network, a sparse matrix encoding the network paths, and transform the representation of one of its hidden neurons into an output node. The proof relies on intermediate propositions which link the pathlifting, its Jacobian, the network parameters, and its skeleton matrix, which, on top of permitting to conclude on the rank of the pathlifting Jacobian, also provide a way to compute it without backpropagation and whose computation cost is super efficient in practice compare to usual backpropagation. The paper is provided with a Python module that implements the different propositions of the paper for feed forward networks and is used to experimentally quantifies the computational gain of computing the pathlifting Jacobian with the proposed theory.

</details>


### [182] [Fast Learning Rates for Physics-Informed Kernel Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18901)
*Luc Brogat-Motte,Joachim Bona-Pellissier,Giacomo Meanti,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究物理信息核估计器中，微分信息（含噪声微分观测或已知物理约束）如何定量改善预测精度，证明有限样本界并揭示误差的两阶段结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理信息机器学习中，仅靠噪声值观测通常只能达到较慢的非参数收敛速率。作者关心如何将微分信息（如噪声观测或物理约束 Du*=v）融入核估计，以及这种改进如何定量依赖于值观测数 n、微分观测数 m 和线性微分算子 D。

Method: 针对线性微分算子 D，构造联合 n 个值观测与 m 个微分观测的物理信息核估计器 û；通过证明有限样本误差界，并辅以数值模拟，刻画误差随 n、m、D 的变化；在再生核 Hilbert 空间的 Sobolev 空间框架下给出具体实例（如环面上的部分 Laplacian 约束、有界区域上的梯度观测）；最后在联合控制 û 与 Dû 误差的更强范数下推导物理一致的收敛速率。

Result: 预测误差呈现两阶段结构：当 m 有限时，速率联合依赖 n 和 m；当 m 超过问题相关阈值时速率饱和，达到施加完美约束 Dû=Du* 时的 oracle 速率。实例表明学习速率可从标准非参数 n^{-1/4} 提升至参数速率 n^{-1/2}。

Conclusion: 微分信息能显著改善物理信息核估计的预测精度，其收益由 n、m 和算子 D 定量刻画，并在样本量较大时趋近 oracle 速率；更强的联合范数下仍可获得物理一致的收敛保证。

Abstract: In physics-informed machine learning, a target function $u^*$ is learned from noisy value observations $y_i=u^*(x_i)+ \varepsilon_i$, together with differential information, given either by noisy observations $d_j=(Du^*)(z_j)+ξ_j$ or by a known physical constraint $Du^*=v$. We consider the setting where $D$ is a linear differential operator and analyze a physics-informed kernel estimator $\hat u$ combining $n$ value observations and $m$ differential observations. In this context, we ask how much can differential information improve predictions, and how does this improvement depend quantitatively on $n$, $m$, and $D$. We prove finite-sample bounds, supported by numerical simulations, revealing a two-regime structure for the prediction error. When $m$ is limited, the rate depends jointly on $n$ and $m$; when $m$ exceeds a problem-dependent threshold, the rate saturates and matches the oracle rate obtained when the perfect constraint $D \hat u = Du^*$ is imposed. Examples are discussed for Sobolev spaces which are reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces and include partial Laplacian constraints on the torus and gradient observations on bounded domains. These examples illustrate the range of possible learning rate improvements --- from the standard nonparametric $n^{-1/4}$ to the parametric rate $n^{-1/2}$. Finally, we derive physically consistent rates in a stronger norm that jointly controls the errors in $\hat u$ and $D\hat u$.

</details>


### [183] [A General Kernel Framework for Non-CND Distance Measures Using |D|-Dimensional Sparse Landmark Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19083)
*Marcus M. Noack,Maher B. Alghalayini,Mark D. Risser*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏地标嵌入（SLE）核，通过以所有训练点为中心的紧支撑碰撞函数将输入嵌入稀疏特征向量，从而对任意距离度量都能保证核矩阵半正定，无需希尔伯特距离条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核方法（尤其是高斯过程）要求距离度量的平方满足条件负定（CND）才能保证核矩阵半正定（PSD）。但许多自然输入空间不满足这一条件，如光滑流形和概率分布空间，限制了核方法在这些领域的应用。为此需要一种能对任意距离度量都保证 PSD 的核构造方法。

Method: 提出稀疏地标嵌入（SLE）核：将每个输入通过以所有 |D| 个训练点为中心的紧支撑碰撞函数嵌入为稀疏特征向量，再在该嵌入空间中应用任意标准 PSD 核，从而对任意距离度量都严格保证所得核为 PSD。紧支撑性自动控制嵌入稀疏度，使核矩阵在极高环境维度下仍保持良态和计算可行。

Result: 给出关于 PSD、稀疏性、稳定性和通用逼近的理论保证；在测地距离和 Wasserstein 距离上的实验表明，SLE 核在预测精度和不确定性量化方面与领域专用基线相当或显著超越。

Conclusion: SLE 核消除了核方法对 CND 距离的依赖，使任意距离度量都能安全用于核方法，并在流形与概率分布等非标准输入空间上取得有竞争力的预测与不确定性量化性能。

Abstract: Kernel methods, and Gaussian Processes (GPs) in particular, require a Hilbertian distance measure---one whose square is conditionally negative definite (CND)---to guarantee positive semi-definiteness (PSD) of the kernel matrix; a condition that fails for many natural input spaces, including smooth manifolds and spaces of probability distributions. We propose the Sparse Landmark Embedding (SLE) kernel, which eliminates this requirement entirely. Each input is embedded into a sparse feature vector via compactly supported bump functions centered at all |D| training points; applying any standard PSD kernel in this embedding space yields a kernel that is provably PSD for arbitrary distance measures. The compact support automatically controls embedding sparsity, keeping kernel matrices well-conditioned and computationally tractable despite the high ambient dimension. We provide theoretical guarantees on PSD, sparsity, stability, and universal approximation, and demonstrate, using geodesic and Wasserstein distances, that the SLE kernel matches or substantially exceeds domain-specific baselines in both predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Making AI-Assisted Claims Independently Challengeable: Publication Authority and a Protocol for Falsifiable Publication Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17631)
*Torsten Olivi Tiltack,Yifei Dong,Kun Yu,Xu Wang,Wei Liu,Jianlong Zhou,Ren Ping Liu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Publication Authority（发布权威）这一精确状态、不可转让、单次使用的发布能力概念，并以 PAC-2026（AIJIM 协议候选）实现；其第四次有界语义冻结（SF-4）经大量状态空间探索与历史/实例盲测验证了内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性与有限可构造性，但明确不主张事实真实性、通用精化、盲互操作、现场效力或标准地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助声明在证据、分析、人工授权、呈现与更正历史指向不同状态时可能显得权威；来源、证明与透明性虽能暴露历史，却未规定本文所考察的“发布转移”本身。因此需要一个能精确刻画“何时、何种记录才能授权一次原子发布转移”的机制。

Method: 开发 Publication Authority 作为精确状态、不可转让、单次使用的发布能力，并将其实例化为机器可读的 AIJIM 协议候选 PAC-2026。重点评估其第四次有界语义冻结 SF-4（固定轮廓规格，面向可替换绑定）。六项义务分别治理证据、运行与产物、测量披露、授权、表面对应与生命周期连续性，每项义务产生目标绑定见证、局部反例或局部不可验证性，且不能互相补偿；仅全新、完整的全通过记录才能派生出被一次原子发布转移所消费的许可。采用身份向量、对抗用例、有限模型与历史实现：十个模型探索了 110,764 个安全可达状态，76 个不安全配置产生了预期违规或观察者反模型；历史前驱路径复现了 17 个冻结的授权-后继结果；后期内部实例盲测匹配全部 183 个评分期望，同主机包执行复现其 240 条归档观测。

Result: 结果支持内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性与有限可构造性。读者表面对应检查通过本身不能授权发布，除非被接受的记录认可该表面；SF-4 将证据视界与验证时间分离，并拒绝真实但因果无效的授权。验证规模包括 110,764 个安全可达状态、76 个不安全配置、17 个历史授权-后继结果、183 个盲测评分期望与 240 条归档观测复现。

Conclusion: PAC-2026 的 SF-4 在内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性与有限可构造性上得到支持，但不主张事实真实性、通用精化、盲互操作性、现场效力或标准地位；其定位为一种面向可替换绑定、可验证发布转移的候选协议规范。

Abstract: AI-assisted claims can appear authoritative when evidence, analysis, human authorization, presentation, and correction history refer to different states. Provenance, attestation, and transparency expose history but alone do not specify the publication transition examined here. We develop Publication Authority as an exact-state, non-transferable, single-use publication capability and instantiate it in PAC-2026 (Publication-Accountability Calculus), a machine-readable AIJIM Protocol candidate. We evaluate its fourth bounded semantic freeze (SF-4), a fixed-profile specification designed for replaceable bindings. Six obligations govern evidence, runs and artifacts, measurement disclosure, authorization, surface correspondence, and lifecycle continuity. Each yields a target-bound witness, localized counterexample, or localized unverifiability; none can compensate for another. Only a fresh, complete all-pass record derives the permit consumed by one atomic publication transition. We use identity vectors, adversarial cases, finite models, and historical implementations. Ten models explored 110,764 safe reachable states; 76 unsafe configurations produced the expected violation or observer countermodel. A reader surface passing its correspondence check cannot authorize publication unless the accepted record admits that surface. SF-4 separates evidence horizon from verification time and rejects an authentic but causally invalid authorization. A historical predecessor path reproduced 17 frozen authorization-successor outcomes. A later in-house, instance-blind test of known case classes matched all 183 scored expectations; same-host package execution reproduced its 240 archived observations. Results support internal coherence, bounded safety, fault sensitivity, and limited constructibility, but not factual truth, general refinement, blind interoperability, field efficacy, or standards status.

</details>


### [185] [EvolveTrade: Experience-Driven Policy Refinement for Self-Evolving LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17632)
*Sehee Kim,Yumin Choi,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvolveTrade，一种让 LLM 交易智能体通过自我进化其"系统提示（文本参数化策略）"来适应市场状态变化的框架，实验表明其在 Sharpe Ratio 和累计收益上多优于固定策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 交易智能体的行为由部署前写死的静态工具使用策略控制，无法在市场状态变化时自适应地调整证据收集、工具调用、信号验证和风险管理方式。

Method: EvolveTrade 将工具型交易智能体的系统提示视为一个文本参数化策略。每个更新周期结束后，Policy Agent 利用累积的决策轨迹和实际组合反馈对这一策略进行修订，同时保持骨干 LLM 不变；更新后的策略用于下一批交易决策，从而逐步改进信息获取与组合构建流程。

Result: 在多个市场状态和两种 LLM 骨干上的实验表明，EvolveTrade 在 Sharpe Ratio 和累计收益上普遍优于固定策略 LLM 基线，并在大多数评估设定中取得改善。行为分析显示自我进化策略提升了代码介导的分析并激活了与状态相关的计算；案例级策略-收益归因追踪了策略引发的配置变化如何贡献于实际收益差异。

Conclusion: 适应可复用的工具使用流程是构建更稳健 LLM 交易智能体的关键方向，EvolveTrade 通过文本参数化策略的自我进化验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) trading agents can combine market data, news, and executable analysis, but their behavior is often controlled by static hand-written tool-use policies that are fixed before deployment. This limits their ability to adapt how they gather evidence, invoke tools, verify signals, and manage risk under changing market regimes. We introduce EvolveTrade, a self-evolving framework that treats the system prompt of a tool-using trading agent as a text-parameterized policy. After each update interval, a Policy Agent revises this policy using accumulated decision traces and realized portfolio feedback, while keeping the backbone LLM fixed. The updated policy is then used for the next batch of trading decisions, enabling the agent to refine its information-acquisition and portfolio-construction procedure over time. Experiments across multiple market regimes and two LLM backbones show that EvolveTrade often improves Sharpe Ratio and Cumulative Return over fixed-policy LLM baselines, achieving the improved SR and CR in most evaluated settings. Behavioral analyses further show that self-evolved policies increase code-mediated analysis and activate regime-relevant computations; case-level policy-to-return attributions trace how policy-induced allocation changes contribute to realized return differences. These results suggest that adapting the reusable procedure governing tool use is a key direction for building more robust LLM trading agents.

</details>


### [186] [One Color Preprocessing Improves DSATUR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17633)
*Adam Nouira,Lucas Isenmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSLD（Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR），通过半定规划（SDP，类似 Lovász theta 数的计算）预先挑选一个优质颜色类，再用 DSATUR 完成剩余着色，从而改进 DSATUR 图着色启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题（GCP）是 NP 困难的，DSATUR 是最快的启发式之一，但其着色所用颜色数通常多于最先进算法。作者希望在不牺牲太多时间的前提下，通过预处理提升 DSATUR 的着色质量，并探索固定颜色类预处理这一尚未被研究的改进方向。

Method: 提出 SSLD 方法：先用半定规划（与计算 Lovász theta 数所用的 SDP 类似）从图中选出一个“好的”首个颜色类，将其固定后，再交给 DSATUR 完成对剩余图的着色。同时与朴素的 1 颜色类预处理基线 GISD 对比，以验证 SDP 引导选色的价值。

Result: 在超过 1600 个基准实例（DIMACS 实例、随机图 Erdős–Rényi/Watts-Strogatz/Barabási–Albert、频率分配、作业车间调度）上，SSLD 几乎在所有情况下追平或优于 DSATUR，并优于朴素 GISD 基线，证实了 SDP 引导选择首个颜色类带来的价值。代价是运行时间约为 DSATUR 的 195 倍。

Conclusion: SSLD 是首个通过固定颜色类预处理改进 DSATUR 的方法，实验证明 SDP 引导的首个颜色类预处理能稳定提升着色质量，尽管耗时较高，但表明这一方向值得未来进一步优化。

Abstract: The Graph Coloring Problem (GCP) is NP-hard and DSATUR stands as one of the fastest heuristics for it despite producing colorings that typically use more colors than state-of-the-art coloring algorithms. We propose SSLD (Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR), which improves DSATUR by preprocessing a first good color class before letting DSATUR complete coloring the rest of the given graph. We obtain this color class from a Semidefinite Programming (SDP), similar to an SDP used to compute the Lovász theta number. To the best of our knowledge, SSLD is the first approach to improve DSATUR by preprocessing through fixed color classes. We evaluate SSLD against DSATUR and against a naive 1-color-class preprocessing algorithm on DIMACS instances, random graphs (Erdős--Rényi, Watts-Strogatz, Barabási--Albert), Frequency Assignment and Job Shop Scheduling instances. SSLD matches or beats DSATUR in almost every case across over 1600 benchmark instances, and out performs the naive GISD baseline, allows us to confirm the value brought by the SDP-guided choice of the first color class. This quality comes at a runtime cost of roughly 195 times slower that DSATUR, but demonstrating that SDP-guided preprocessing of a first color class is a direction for future improvements.

</details>


### [187] [Physics-Constrained Digital Twins for Sensor Integrity in Urban Pedestrian Flow: Detecting Stealthy False Data Injection with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17635)
*Oscar Mogollon Gutierrez,Fatemeh Ghasemi,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Andres Caro,Mar Avila*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究城市行人计数系统中隐蔽式虚假数据注入攻击，提出一种基于图流守恒残差约束的双胞胎模型来估计有向行人流，并通过自适应共形校准设定告警阈值。在墨尔本六年数据上，面对白盒对手，攻击裕度可达0.54（单设备被攻陷），并在仅1.18%路段被计量的网络上验证了物理守恒律相较于距离图的增益优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市行人计数系统被用于经济指标、规划决策和安全管理，但构建其上的数字孪生将输入数据流视为真实值（ground truth）。作者指出现实中该假设可能不成立，并针对城市尺度行人感知系统开展隐蔽式虚假数据注入研究。与已充分刻画隐蔽性的电力和水网不同，行人网络中从潜在流到观测的映射秩亏缺程度远更严重，这使得传统隐蔽攻击理论不再适用，亟需建立针对该场景的攻击与防御框架。

Method: 作者将隐蔽虚假数据注入形式化为城市尺度行人感知问题，其中潜在流到观测的映射高度秩亏。数字孪生在行人街道图上估计有向流，通过一个学习到的图局部化增益同化计数数据，并针对流动守恒残差进行训练，该残差耦合了被计量与未被计量的路段。检测将创新（innovation）与该守恒残差相结合，告警阈值由自适应共形校准设定而非人工指定。为量化物理约束带来的增益，定义“攻击裕度”（attack margin），即相对最坏情况下估计流场被破坏程度的降低量，并针对一个直接通过孪生模型优化的白盒对手进行评估。

Result: 在墨尔本六年数据上，当单一设备被攻陷时攻击裕度达到0.54；当三分之一设备被攻陷时降至0.19。该网络中仅1.18%的可步行路段被计量。若将街道图替换为距离图，攻击裕度崩塌至0.09，表明增益来源于流守恒定律而非图局部性。

Conclusion: 物理流守恒残差约束能显著提升城市行人感知数字孪生对隐蔽虚假数据注入的鲁棒性，其增益本质来自守恒定律而非地理局部性；但在传感器大规模被攻陷时防护能力会明显下降，提示需要进一步研究以应对高度受损场景。

Abstract: City pedestrian counting systems now feed economic indicators, planning decisions and safety operations, yet the twins built on top of them treat the incoming stream as ground truth. We study what happens when it is not. We formalise stealthy false data injection for city-scale pedestrian sensing, where the map from latent flow to observation is far more rank deficient than in the power and water networks for which stealth has been characterised. Our twin estimates directed flows on the pedestrian street graph, assimilates counts through a learned graph-localised gain, and is trained against a flow conservation residual that couples metered and unmetered segments. Detection combines the innovation with that residual, and the alarm threshold is set by adaptive conformal calibration rather than by hand. To measure what the physics buys, we define the attack margin, the relative reduction in worst-case corruption of the estimated flow field, achieved against a white-box adversary that optimises directly through the twin. On six years of Melbourne data the margin reaches 0.54 against a single compromised device and falls to 0.19 when a third of the fleet is compromised, on a network where only 1.18 per cent of walkable segments are metered. Replacing the street graph by a distance graph collapses it to 0.09, which shows that the gain comes from the conservation law rather than from locality.

</details>


### [188] [What You Can't See Is Still What You Learn: A Preregistered Sixty-Society Confirmation That Evidence Masking Drives Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17637)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项预注册的确认实验，在共享冻结语言模型骨干、通过可学习连续数据包通信的 64 个细胞系统中，验证限制模块可读信息（证据掩码）能显著提升组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索“限制模块可读内容是否有助于系统学习计算”，检验证据掩码机制能否提升模型对未见组合任务（二操作、三操作组合）的泛化性能，并试图厘清该优势背后的归因（角色信息、填充分解等）。

Method: 预注册确认实验：64 个四细胞系统共享冻结语言模型骨干，通过可学习连续数据包通信。设置五个条件，在有无标记两种机制下变化证据掩码、所有权标记、用中性填充替换外部证据；跨越 6 个初始化簇×2 种数据顺序，共 12 对配对。评估留出的二操作与三操作组合精度，并采用中位配对差、预注册行为准则、标记跟随检查、数据包干预审计进行验证。

Result: 在两种机制均有标记可用时，掩码使留出的二/三操作组合精度中位配对差分别提升 0.846 与 0.859，12 对全部达到所需裕度，完整预注册行为准则通过；无标记复现也通过。无全局可见系统通过标记跟随检查，因此可用角色信息的作用仍未解决。填充条件产生 7 个完全泛化者，但分解准则不具结论性。在 18 个受审计掩码系统中，数据包干预在合格案例上均符合预测的中间值变化；但这些有限、以成功为条件的审计不能确立中介关系。

Conclusion: 实验确证了所测掩码机制带来的显著优势，但其更精细的归因与通用性仍待探究。协议、结果与检查点已公开。

Abstract: Restricting what a module can read may improve what a system learns to compute. We test this in a preregistered confirmation with sixty four-cell systems sharing a frozen language-model backbone and communicating through learned continuous packets. Five conditions vary evidence masking, ownership markers, and replacement of foreign evidence with neutral filler, across six initialization clusters, each with two data orders, on one fresh task world. With markers available in both regimes, masking improved accuracy on held-out two- and three-operation compositions by median paired differences of 0.846 and 0.859; all twelve pairs cleared the required margins, and the full preregistered behavioral criterion passed. The unmarked replication also passed. No globally visible system passed the marker-following check, so the effect of usable role information remains unresolved. The filler condition yielded seven full generalizers, but its decomposition criteria were inconclusive. Packet interventions in all eighteen audited masked systems followed the predicted intermediate-value changes on eligible cases; these finite, success-conditioned audits do not establish mediation. The results confirm a large advantage of the tested masking regime, while leaving its finer attribution and generality open. Protocols, results, and checkpoints are public.

</details>


### [189] [CapMem: A Benchmark for Caption-Based Episodic Memory in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17688)
*Dingli Liang,Yiqiao Xie,Yukai Huang,Zhaokai Wang,Weitong Cai,Guangwen Feng,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在帧预算受限、视觉token开销大、长上下文检索易失效的实用约束下，用文本字幕作为可复用的情景记忆来支持可穿戴助手的自我中心视频问答，提出CapMem基准并验证字幕记忆的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手需要对自我中心视频具备情景记忆，但现有视觉-语言模型受限于有限的帧预算、不断增长的视觉token成本以及长上下文检索失败，因此探索能否用文本字幕作为可复用的情景记忆来缓解这些问题。

Method: 定义了情景记忆视频字幕问答（Episodic Memory Video Caption QA）任务；构建了人工标注的CapMem基准（75个视频、33.7小时、16个场景、1000道多选题）；采用全覆盖率CaptionQA（30s/60s字幕窗口）、跨六款Qwen模型的匹配帧对照实验，以及字幕引导的检索-验证（retrieve-and-verify）框架。

Result: 在长视频（>20分钟）上，30s和60s字幕窗口的全覆盖率CaptionQA分别在12个模型中优于直接VideoQA的10个和8个；匹配帧对照下六款Qwen模型平均准确率分别提升3.22和2.55个百分点；字幕引导的检索-验证框架进一步将准确率最高提升5.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 文本字幕记忆对长时自我中心视频的情景推理是有效的，可作为视频问答在受限条件下的实用替代或补充方案。

Abstract: Wearable assistants require episodic memory over egocentric video, yet current vision-language models face bounded frame budgets, growing visual-token costs, and long-context retrieval failures. Under these practical constraints, we study whether textual captions can serve as reusable episodic memory. We define the Episodic Memory Video Caption QA task and introduce CapMem, a human-annotated benchmark with 75 videos totaling 33.7 hours, and 1,000 multiple-choice questions across 16 scenarios. On long videos (>20 min), full-coverage CaptionQA with 30s and 60s caption windows outperforms direct VideoQA for 10/12 and 8/12 models, respectively. On the same video subset, a matched-frame control across six Qwen models retains mean accuracy gains of 3.22 and 2.55 points, respectively. Our caption-guided retrieve-and-verify harness further improves accuracy by up to 5.3 points. These results support the effectiveness of caption memory for episodic reasoning over long egocentric video.

</details>


### [190] [GraphEcho: Structural Redundancy and Evidence Provenance in LLM Graph Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17695)
*Sikun Wang,Yixi Zhou,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphEcho 基准测试揭示LLM智能体在图证据探索中的"回声确认"偏差：智能体将重复遍历同一证据路径误当作额外佐证。来源感知后训练(PAPT)虽能减少重复访问，却导致覆盖到的独立来源减少、科学声明上的准确率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图智能体研究中，智能体可沿更多路径探索，但缺乏对"重复证据是否被误判为独立佐证"的受控检验。作者希望区分"探索效率"与"证据有效利用"，揭示智能体在路径重复与证据来源覆盖之间的取舍。

Method: 构建GraphEcho基准：在证据内容固定不变的前提下，系统性地变化路径数量与证据来源，分别评估智能体的判断(judgment)与主动探索(exploration)行为。通过受控合成实验观察不同冻结模型对冗余路径的判断偏移，并用来源感知后训练(PAPT)干预模型，比较其后训练前后的重复访问率、合成准确率、来源覆盖数与科学声明准确率。

Result: 1) 合成实验中，路径减少对应的判断偏移呈模型依赖，但冗余支持路径在所有冻结模型的重复遍历占比上均告上升；2) PAPT降低重复访问并提升合成准确率，却减少了对不同来源的覆盖；3) 在科学声明上，PAPT继续减少重复遍历，但准确率下降。

Conclusion: 探索效率与证据有效利用之间存在鸿沟：智能体可以学会停止重复自身，却反而忽略所需信息。GraphEcho为评估图智能体的结论及其探索是否真正触达独立证据来源提供了受控手段。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) agent can follow more graph paths without acquiring more independent evidence. GraphEcho tests whether agents mistake these repeated encounters for additional corroboration. The benchmark varies path counts and evidential origins while holding evidence content fixed, and evaluates both judgments and active exploration. Controlled synthetic experiments reveal model-dependent judgment shifts, but redundant supporting paths increase the share of repeated walks across all evaluated frozen agents. Provenance-aware post-training (PAPT) reduces revisits and improves synthetic accuracy, yet covers fewer distinct sources. On scientific claims, it continues to reduce repetition while accuracy declines. These findings expose a gap between efficient exploration and effective evidence use: an agent can learn to stop repeating itself while overlooking information it needs. GraphEcho provides a controlled way to evaluate both what graph agents conclude and whether their exploration reaches distinct evidential sources.

</details>


### [191] [GVD: Governed Versioning and Deduplication for Document Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17696)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GVD 框架，将跨文档版本链接与规则级冲突解决统一在同一可审计的更新策略下，无需大语言模型即可在 120 份企业文档上实现高精度版本家族构建与规则一致性检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档仓库持续演化，同一内容以不同措辞反复出现，新版本会细化甚至与旧版本相互矛盾。这些不一致性属于整个不断增长的文档集合，而非单个文档，但现有工作把版本检测、重复检测和矛盾检测当作孤立的成对任务，且一旦标注完某对文档即停止，缺乏整体协同与可审计性。

Method: 提出 GVD（Governed Versioning and Deduplication）框架：通过双向规则对齐将新文档归入版本家族；将其规则与家族记忆比对，识别重复、矛盾、非对称细化和新知识；采用反事实跨度探测（CSP）解决推理误判为中性的相关规则对；关系特定策略抑制重复，仅将重大变更升级供人工审查，并保留版本谱系作为审计痕迹。整个流程完全本地运行，不使用大语言模型。

Result: 在 120 份企业文档（作为 140 次摄取、跨越 59 个版本家族处理）上，GVD 的版本家族构建 F1 达到 0.97，规则级一致性 F1 为 0.94；其中 CSP 将规则一致性从 0.90 提升至 0.94。

Conclusion: GVD 通过统一的版本治理与去重框架，将孤立的两两任务整合为可审计的更新策略，在无需大语言模型的前提下实现了高效、可靠的版本家族构建与规则级冲突解决，显著优于既有分散式处理方法。

Abstract: Document repositories evolve continuously. Guidelines and policies are revised, superseded, and re-uploaded, so the same content recurs in different wording and newer versions refine or contradict earlier ones. These inconsistencies belong to the growing collection rather than to any single document, yet existing work treats versioning, duplicate detection, and contradiction detection as isolated pairwise tasks and stops once a pair is labeled. We present GVD (Governed Versioning and Deduplication), a framework that unifies cross-document version linking with rule-level conflict resolution under an auditable update policy. Incoming documents are assigned to version families through bidirectional rule alignment, and their rules are compared against the family memory to identify duplicates, contradictions, asymmetric refinements, and new knowledge, with Counterfactual Span Probing (CSP) resolving related pairs that inference misclassifies as neutral. Relation-specific policies suppress duplicates and escalate only consequential changes for review, retaining version lineage as an audit trail. The pipeline runs fully locally, with no large language model. On 120 enterprise documents processed as 140 ingestions across 59 version families, GVD reaches an F1 of 0.97 for version-family construction and 0.94 for rule-level consistency, with CSP raising rule consistency from 0.90 to 0.94.

</details>


### [192] [NeMo Data Designer: An Extensible Framework for Multimodal Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17699)
*Johnny Greco,Nabin Mulepati,Andre Manoel,Eric Tramel,Kirit Thadaka,Mike Knepper,Dhruv Nathawani,Dane Corneil,Yev Meyer,Alex Watson,Maarten Van Segbroeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 NeMo Data Designer (NDD)，一个开源、通用的多模态合成数据生成框架。它通过声明式配置定义数据列，支持文本、代码、结构化输出、图像、嵌入和统计采样等多类型列，并提供插件系统和预览-修订迭代工作流，已在 Nemotron 模型开发及企业生产中应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成是一个天然迭代的过程，现有方法往往缺乏对数据集多样性、可复现性和快速迭代的直接支持。作者希望提供一个直观易用、可扩展且能支撑工作流共享与复现的通用框架，以满足多模态、结构化、智能体等多种任务的数据生成需求。

Method: NDD 采用声明式配置格式，由人或智能体用户定义每个数据列，列类型涵盖文本、代码、结构化输出、图像、嵌入及显式控制多样性的统计采样器，并通过插件系统扩展新类型。配置作为可审查工件支持共享与复现。核心工作流内置 preview-and-revision 循环：先生成少量记录供检查、再细化规格、最后全量生成。运行时负责解析依赖、调度对用户提供的模型端点的调用，并对失败请求重试。

Result: 通过结构化、智能体、多模态和领域专用任务等案例研究表明，NDD 能够支撑包括 Nemotron 模型开发数据集在内的多种场景，并已在生产级企业部署中投入使用，证明了框架的通用性和实用性。

Conclusion: NDD 提供了一个直观、通用、可扩展且可复现的多模态合成数据生成框架，其声明式配置、插件机制和预览-修订式迭代工作流有效满足了多样化的数据生成需求，并已在模型开发与生产部署中得到验证。

Abstract: We present NeMo Data Designer (NDD), an open-source, general-purpose framework for multi-modal synthetic data generation (SDG). Designed to be intuitive to use, NDD provides a declarative configuration format in which human and/or agent users define each dataset column, with column types spanning text, code, structured outputs, images, embeddings, and statistical samplers that are explicitly configured to steer dataset diversity. Additional column types and functionality can be introduced using the framework's flexible plugin system. NDD's configuration is an inspectable artifact, supporting workflow sharing and reproducibility. SDG is an inherently iterative process. NDD therefore builds a preview-and-revision loop into its core workflow, allowing users to generate and inspect a small number of records, refine the specification, and rerun generation at full scale. At runtime, NDD resolves dependencies, schedules calls to user-provided model endpoints, and retries failed requests. We describe NDD's architecture and programming model and present case studies spanning structured, agentic, multimodal, and domain-specialized tasks, including datasets used in Nemotron model development and in production enterprise deployments.

</details>


### [193] [A Systematic Evaluation of the COTQ Provincial Land Cover Product: Structural Consistency, Spectral Separability, and Relative Positioning Against ESA, ESRI, and Google Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17731)
*Étienne Clabaut,Samuel Foucher,Yacine Bouroubi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对加拿大魁北克省 10 米分辨率土地覆盖产品 COTQ 进行系统评估，与 ESA WorldCover、ESRI LandCover 和 Google DynamicWorld 三个全球 10 米产品对比，通过结构指标、光谱可分性和差异区照片解译等多准则分析，发现 COTQ 与 ESA WorldCover 最相似，但在城市、湿地和岩石/隐花植被表面存在系统差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 Sentinel-2 的全球 10 米 LULC 产品在生态梯度复杂、地表异质性强的地区表现不一，魁北克省因此开发了省级产品 COTQ 以支持土地占用和土壤人工化的年度监测，需要客观评估其相对全球产品的表现与定位。

Method: 本文不提出新制图方法，而是采用互补评估手段：将 COTQ 与三个全球产品统一到共同图例后，利用结构指标（对象尺寸分布、形状复杂度、调整兰德指数 ARI、交并比 IoU）、基于 Sentinel-2 反射率的光谱可分性指标，以及对不一致区域的定向照片解译进行对比。分析覆盖代表魁北克主要生物气候域的 8 个 Sentinel-2 景（从温带、北方森林到北部苔原）。

Result: COTQ 在结构与光谱特征上与 ESA WorldCover 最为相似，但在城市区域、湿地以及岩石或隐花（地衣苔藓）表面存在与类别定义和主题优先级相关的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 该多准则评估为 COTQ 产品提供了客观特征刻画，并明确了其相对于现有全球土地覆盖数据集在魁北克省业务化土地监测中的定位。

Abstract: High-resolution land use and land cover (LULC) products derived from Sentinel-2 imagery are widely used for environmental monitoring and land management, yet their performance can vary across regions with complex ecological gradients and heterogeneous surface conditions. In Quebec, these limitations motivated the development of a provincial 10-m land-cover product, the COTQ, designed to support annual monitoring of land occupation and soil artificialisation. This study presents a systematic evaluation of the COTQ product relative to three global 10-m LULC datasets: ESA WorldCover, ESRI LandCover, and Google DynamicWorld. This paper does not introduce a new mapping methodology but focuses on analysing the behaviour and consistency of the COTQ using complementary evaluation approaches. All products are harmonized under a common legend and compared using structural indicators (object-size distributions, shape complexity, Adjusted Rand Index, and Intersection over Union), spectral separability metrics derived from Sentinel-2 reflectance data, and a targeted photo-interpretation of disagreement areas. The analysis is conducted over eight Sentinel-2 tiles selected to represent the main bioclimatic domains of Québec, from temperate and boreal forests to northern tundra environments. The results show that the COTQ exhibits structural and spectral characteristics most similar to ESA WorldCover among the reference global products, while revealing systematic differences linked to class definitions and thematic priorities, particularly for urban areas, wetlands, and rocky or cryptogamic surfaces. This multi-criteria evaluation provides an objective characterization of the COTQ product and clarifies its relative positioning with respect to existing global land-cover datasets for operational land monitoring in Québec.

</details>


### [194] [Imitation Learning for Autonomous Driving in CARLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17757)
*Jordy Kieto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究一个紧凑的多模态行为克隆策略，利用离线专家示范在CARLA模拟器中学习闭路驾驶能力，仅以136万参数即在训练及未见路线上实现数小时无碰撞自动驾驶，并开源代码、模型和数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆通常离线训练，但部署时是闭环的，即策略的动作会影响后续观测。作者希望量化一个紧凑的多模态策略能从离线示范中获得多少闭路驾驶能力，并区分可测量的离线指标与定性的闭环观察，避免过度声称。

Method: 策略以五帧历史的RGB图像、LiDAR、车辆遥测数据和车道航点作为输入，以20 Hz输出油门、刹车和转向。示范分三阶段收集，最后采用系统化路线生成流程枚举生成点和可行机动并验证完成的自动驾驶路线。最终策略拥有136万参数，在236,882个窗口上训练，对应448次采集、约3.3小时驾驶数据。

Result: 所得策略在训练路线和留出的测试路线上可自动驾驶数小时且无碰撞，并能定性迁移到具有不同道路几何的未见过CARLA城镇；还观察到从大幅轨迹偏离中恢复的现象，但因缺乏受控评估而不作系统性恢复的声称。

Conclusion: 紧凑的多模态行为克隆策略能够从离线示范中获得显著的闭路驾驶能力。作者将测量结果与定性闭环观察明确区分，并发布代码、训练检查点、ONNX模型、数据样本及针对所报告结论的证据审计，以提升可复现性与可信度。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning trains a policy offline on expert demonstrations, but deployment is closed loop: each action affects the observations the policy receives next. We study how much closed-loop driving competence a compact multimodal policy can acquire from offline demonstrations in the CARLA simulator. The policy uses five-frame histories of RGB images, LiDAR, vehicle telemetry, and lane waypoints to predict throttle, brake, and steering at 20 Hz. Demonstrations were collected in three stages, ending with a systematic route-generation procedure that enumerates spawn points and feasible maneuvers and verifies completed autopilot routes. The released 1.36 million parameter policy was trained on 236,882 windows, representing about 3.3 hours of driving from 448 captures. The resulting policy drives autonomously for hours on training and held-out routes. In our runs, it did so without collisions and also transferred qualitatively to an unseen CARLA town with different road geometry. We also observed recovery from large trajectory deviations, although we do not claim systematic recovery without controlled evaluation. We report offline metrics and distinguish measured results from qualitative closed-loop observations. We release the code, trained checkpoint, ONNX model, data sample, and an evidence audit for the reported claims.

</details>


### [195] [SAGE: Governed Artifact Generation from Enterprise Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17775)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Srinivasa Karthikeya Reddy Kovvuri,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE 是一个受治理的多阶段 LLM 流水线，用于将企业指南文档转化为结构化工件，通过共享版本化规则库、模式校验的阶段性契约与端到端溯源，将周转时间从数天缩短至 20-100 分钟，并把幻觉率从 15.7% 降至 3.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业指南文档混合了叙述文本、复杂表格和嵌入图像，将其转化为结构化工作工件目前仍需 2-3 天的人工工作量。现有语言与视觉-语言模型虽能抽取内容，但缺乏抽取之外的治理工作流：没有校验、没有一致性检查、也没有可溯源的工件生成，导致高风险幻觉与不可信输出。

Method: SAGE 以共享的版本化规则库（含稳定标识符）为核心，组织多阶段 LLM 流水线，并在阶段间使用模式校验的契约。抽取的规则先经过确定性的结构校验和基于 LLM 的语义评分，再由一致性模块去重、标记矛盾并暴露规格缺口；仅不确定或被标记的条目送交人工审核，高置信度输出则自动批准。

Result: 在 120 份文档上，SAGE 将周转时间从数天缩短到 20-100 分钟，文档级成功率达 96%，幻觉率仅 3.2%，共抽取 3,896 条规则并生成 812 个可供人工审核的工件；而在无治理设置下，幻觉率升至 15.7%。

Conclusion: 通过引入规则库、模式校验与溯源等治理机制，SAGE 显著提升了文档转工件的效率与可信度，证明治理性多阶段 LLM 流水线能有效抑制幻觉并将高置信度输出自动化处理，实现快速且可追溯的规模化转化。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents mix narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images, and converting them into structured work artifacts still takes two to three days of manual effort each. Current language and vision-language models extract from such documents but offer no governed workflow beyond extraction: no validation, no consistency checking, no traceable artifact generation. We introduce SAGE, a governed multi-stage LLM pipeline organized around a shared versioned rule store with stable identifiers, schema-validated inter-stage contracts, and end-to-end provenance tracking. Extracted rules undergo deterministic structural validation and LLM-based semantic scoring, then a consistency module that removes duplicates, flags contradictions, and surfaces specification gaps; only uncertain or flagged items reach reviewers, while high-confidence outputs are auto-approved. On 120 documents, SAGE cuts turnaround from days to 20-100 minutes, achieving a 96% document-level success rate with 3.2% hallucination, extracting 3,896 rules and producing 812 artifacts ready for human review; without governance, hallucination rises to 15.7%.

</details>


### [196] [FairCompressAgent: An Agentic Framework for Fairness-Aware Model Compression for FPGA Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17786)
*Yuanbo Guo,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FairCompressAgent（FCA），一个基于语言模型规划器的智能体框架，整合公平性感知剪枝、增量量化和稀疏低秩分解，通过测量反馈与显式约束交互式地选择公平性感知的模型压缩配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性感知的模型压缩需要在准确率、公平性与部署成本之间权衡，尤其当多种压缩方法组合使用或用户需求动态变化时，决策难度显著增加。

Method: FCA 通过统一的算子接口整合公平性感知剪枝、增量量化和稀疏低秩分解三种压缩方法；语言模型规划器利用模型画像与实测结果选择压缩配置，执行层负责压缩、微调、评估和基于约束的筛选；框架支持需求更新，并在无法满足请求时报告剩余违规情况。

Result: 在 Fitzpatrick-17k 数据集与 VGG-11 模型上对 40 个测量配置比较四种搜索方法。在准确率约束请求下，FCA 选出推理张量存储减少 59.54% 的压缩模型，验证平均精度从 0.5141 升至 0.5233，机会均等（EOpp）从 0.2251 降至 0.2168。在各自停止策略下，FCA 与一次性规划得到相同的最终选择，但平均仅需 7.33 次候选评估，而后者为 12 次。重复微调、留出测试和在线需求更新体现了该工作流的稳定性与交互性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，测量反馈与显式约束能够有效支持公平性感知压缩配置的选择和交互式优化，验证了 FCA 框架在平衡准确率、公平性与部署成本方面的有效性。

Abstract: Fairness-aware model compression requires selecting methods and configurations that balance accuracy, fairness, and deployment cost. These decisions become more difficult when compression methods are composed or the user's requirements change. In this paper, we propose FairCompressAgent (FCA), an agentic framework that integrates fairness-aware pruning, incremental quantization, and sparse low-rank factorization through a common operator interface. A language-model planner uses model profiles and measured outcomes to select compression configurations, while an execution layer performs compression, fine-tuning, evaluation, and constraint-based selection. FCA also supports requirement updates and reports the remaining violation when a request cannot be satisfied. Experiments on Fitzpatrick-17k with VGG-11 compare four search methods over 40 measured configurations. Under the accuracy-constrained request, FCA selects a compressed model with 59.54% less inference tensor storage, while validation average precision increases from 0.5141 to 0.5233 and equalized opportunity (EOpp) decreases from 0.2251 to 0.2168. It reaches the same final selection as one-shot planning with 7.33 versus 12 candidate evaluations on average, under their respective stopping policies. Repeated fine-tuning, held-out testing, and online requirement updates characterize the stability and interactive use of this compression workflow. The results demonstrate how measured feedback and explicit constraints support the selection and interactive refinement of fairness-aware compression configurations.

</details>


### [197] [A Four-Stage Decomposition of Word-Problem Solving and Mechanistic Fragility in LLM Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17804)
*Zhongdi Qu,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对大语言模型解数学应用题的内部机制进行拆解，发现其计算过程可分解为四个顺序阶段，并定位了无关干扰句导致失败的具体阶段——运算规划及其对应的注意力头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能高精度解答小学数学应用题，但插入一个无关从句便会导致性能崩溃。作者希望调和这两种看似矛盾的现象，从机制层面解释模型为何成功、又为何在干扰下失败。

Method: 作者提出一个机械论分析框架：将模型内部计算分解为"模式抽象（Schema Abstraction）→ 运算规划（Operation Planning）→ 操作数绑定（Operand Binding）→ 计算（Computation）"的四阶段顺序流水线，每一阶段在可识别的层区间内产生不同的中间表征。作者利用该框架诊断干扰导致的失败，将故障定位于"运算规划"阶段，并通过双向因果验证确认了实现该阶段的一组注意力头的作用。

Result: 模型内部计算确实分解为四阶段顺序流水线，各阶段产生不同的中间表征；干扰导致的失败被精确定位到"运算规划"这一单一阶段，该阶段由一组注意力头实现，其因果作用经双向验证得到确认。

Conclusion: 作者为 LLM 的数学应用题推理及其受干扰时的失败提供了机制层面的解释：成功源于清晰的四阶段流水线，而失败源于无关子句对"运算规划"阶段的特定干扰。

Abstract: Large language models solve grade-school math word problems with high accuracy, yet a single irrelevant clause inserted into the problem can collapse it. We reconcile these observations with a mechanistic account. We show that the model's internal computation decomposes into a four-stage sequential pipeline, Schema Abstraction, Operation Planning, Operand Binding, and Computation, each stage producing a distinct intermediate representation in an identifiable band of layers. Using the same scaffold to diagnose distractor-induced failure, we localize the corruption to a single stage, Operation Planning, implemented by a set of attention heads whose causal role we validate bidirectionally. In short, we provide a mechanistic interpretation of math word problem reasoning in LLMs, and their failure when distracted.

</details>


### [198] [Learning Heterogeneous Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17847)
*Shiwali Mohan,Matt Hong,Dule Shu,Aniek Fransen,Shabnam Hakimi,Matt Klenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究主观偏好学习问题，提出以“个体化效用函数”（同时以个体及其决策情境为条件）替代传统“普遍效用”假设，并设计多阶段架构从多模态数据中估计该函数，在一个包含57.5万条汽车轮毂审美判断的数据集上显著优于通用效用模型和基础模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人类反馈学习的方法假定人群中存在共享的“普遍效用”函数，把标注者之间的分歧视为随机噪声。这在客观任务中可行，但在主观领域，偏好会随个体系统地变化，该假设失效。因此需要在理论上和方法上对主观偏好（异质但内部一致的效用）进行建模。

Method: 借鉴理性选择理论（RCT），提出“个体化效用”（individuated utility）函数，其以个体和决策情境为条件；并设计一种多阶段（multi-stage）架构，从多模态数据中估计该效用函数。评估基于新收集的数据集：2398名参与者、575000+条汽车轮毂设计的成对审美判断。

Result: 实验表明，个体化效用模型显著优于通用效用模型以及基础模型基线；分歧反映的是有意义的偏好异质性，而非标注噪声。

Conclusion: 研究强调收集标注者属性并学习个体化效用函数的重要性，从而构建能明确表征“所代表的是谁的偏好”的奖励模型，忠实捕捉人类决策的多样性。

Abstract: Learning from human feedback has become a central paradigm for training modern AI systems, where models of human utility are used as reward models in policy learning. Existing methods typically assume a \emph{universal utility} function shared across a population and treat disagreement between annotators as stochastic variation. While suitable for objective tasks, this assumption breaks down in subjective domains where preferences vary systematically across individuals. We study the problem of subjective preference learning, in which observed choices arise from heterogeneous but internally consistent utility functions. Drawing upon rational choice theory, RCT \parencite{tversky1981framing}, we introduce \emph{individuated utility} functions conditioned on both the individual and their decision context, and propose a novel multi-stage architecture for estimating them from multi-modal data. We evaluate our framework on a newly collected dataset of more than $575{,}000$ pairwise aesthetic judgments from $2{,}398$ participants comparing automotive wheel designs. Our experiments show that individuated utility models substantially outperform universal utility models including foundation model baselines. Our results demonstrate that disagreement reflects meaningful preference heterogeneity rather than annotation noise. More broadly, our findings highlight the importance of collecting annotator attributes and learning individuated utility functions, enabling reward models that explicitly account for whose preferences they represent and faithfully capture human decision diversity.

</details>


### [199] [Bad Genius: Counterfactual-Guided Harness Evolution Beyond Task-Specific Shortcuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18366)
*Guojun Zhu,Xunheng Huang,Peng Yin,Jiahui Xie,Sanguo Zhang,Doudou Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHASE方法，通过反事实基准变换将智体评估中的“作弊捷径”中性化，在保持基准收益的同时显著降低有效协议变更下的收益损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的智体评估面临自动基准优化问题：优化器反复利用公开基准引导“提议者”修改提示、记忆、检索、工具和控制代码，导致可能产生利用基准整体捷径的“作弊”行为。现有任务留出法虽更换语义任务，但基准协议固定不变，无法识别这种作弊捷径。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Harness Search and Evolution (CHASE)，将基准演化建模为在保有效性的反事实基准上的约束生成：每次提议者更新后，挑战者搜索能造成大幅收益破坏的可执行协议变换；有效性防火墙确保任务语义不变；确认集决定反事实是否进入有限档案。并形式化精确的捷径中性化基准B0。

Result: 建立了将有限反事实档案与B0关联的统计保证，并刻画了顺序挑战者搜索的性质。在合成基准和OfficeQA上的评估表明，CHASE在保持较强公开基准收益的同时，大幅降低了有效协议变更下的收益破坏。

Conclusion: CHASE通过反事实约束生成有效中和基准整体捷径，兼顾公开基准性能与协议鲁棒性，为可靠的智体评估提供了理论保证与实证支持。

Abstract: Reliable agent evaluation is complicated by automatic harness optimization, which repeatedly uses a released benchmark $B_{\mathrm{rel}}$ to guide a Proposer that edits prompts, memory, retrieval, tools, and control code around a fixed target agent. Task holdout varies semantic tasks but leaves the benchmark protocol fixed, so a "bad genius" Proposer can produce a cheating harness whose released-benchmark gain depends on a benchmark-wide shortcut. We introduce Counterfactual Harness Search and Evolution (CHASE), which casts harness evolution as constraint generation over validity-preserving benchmark counterfactuals. After each Proposer update, a Challenger searches for an executable protocol transformation with large gain destruction. A validity firewall checks that task semantics are preserved, while a confirmation set determines whether the counterfactual enters a finite archive. We formalize an exact shortcut-neutralized benchmark $B_0$ and establish statistical guarantees linking finite counterfactual archives to $B_0$ and characterizing sequential Challenger search. We evaluate CHASE on a synthetic benchmark and on OfficeQA, where CHASE retains strong released-benchmark gains while substantially reducing gain destruction under valid protocol changes.

</details>


### [200] [SNOMED CT Concept Recommendation from Masked Clinical Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17855)
*Ali Noori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个掩码概念推荐基准（基于SNOMED CT实体链接挑战数据），系统在局部临床上下文中对训练期见过的概念进行排序。稀疏TF-IDF原型效果最佳（Recall@10为33.43%），检索增强未带来提升，且性能严重受概念频率和未见概念影响，为低资源场景提供了可复现基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言标准化到SNOMED CT有助于互操作性、分析和可复用表型，但当相关概念在训练数据中罕见或缺失时，概念推荐仍很困难。因此需要一个面向低资源场景的、可复现的推荐基准来量化并诊断这一困难。

Method: 作者使用来自MIMIC-IV-Note的SNOMED CT实体链接挑战v1.2.1数据构建基准，包含272份出院小结的75,491条标注（204份训练、68份历史测试）。对每个唯一的"note-concept"对，将目标提及从局部临床上下文中掩码，系统对训练期观察到的SNOMED CT概念进行排序。比较的方法包括：流行度基线、稀疏TF-IDF概念原型、稠密潜在语义分析嵌入、稀疏-稠密融合、检索笔记证据，以及检索增强混合模型。评估指标为Recall@1、Recall@10、MRR和nDCG@10。

Result: 稀疏TF-IDF表现最佳：Recall@1为14.81%、Recall@10为33.43%、MRR为0.2114、nDCG@10为0.2297。检索增强未提升基线（Recall@10为31.99%、MRR为0.1937）。性能受概念频率强烈影响：仅出现在1-2个训练笔记中的概念Recall@10为7.74%，而出现超过10个的概念为43.90%。此外，9.66%的测试note-concept对包含训练中未见过的概念。

Conclusion: 局部词汇上下文和术语覆盖度是低资源场景下推荐质量的主要决定因素。该研究发现为未来基于本体和生物医学编码器的检索系统提供了可复现的基线。

Abstract: Standardizing clinical language to SNOMED CT supports interoperability, analytics, and reusable phenotyping, but concept recommendation remains difficult when relevant concepts are rare or absent from training data. We present a masked-concept recommendation benchmark using the SNOMED CT Entity Linking Challenge v1.2.1 data derived from MIMIC-IV-Note. The dataset contains 75,491 annotations across 272 discharge summaries, with 204 notes used for training and 68 for historical testing. For each unique note-concept pair, the target mention is masked from a local clinical context and the system ranks SNOMED CT concepts observed during training. We compare a popularity baseline, sparse TF-IDF concept prototypes, dense latent semantic analysis embeddings, sparse-dense fusion, retrieved-note evidence, and a retrieval-augmented hybrid. Sparse TF-IDF performs best, achieving Recall@1 of 14.81%, Recall@10 of 33.43%, MRR of 0.2114, and nDCG@10 of 0.2297. Retrieval augmentation does not improve this baseline, with Recall@10 of 31.99% and MRR of 0.1937. Performance is strongly affected by concept frequency: Recall@10 is 7.74% for concepts appearing in only one or two training notes versus 43.90% for concepts appearing in more than ten. In addition, 9.66% of test note-concept pairs contain concepts unseen during training. These findings show that local lexical context and terminology coverage are major determinants of recommendation quality in low-resource settings and provide a reproducible baseline for future ontology-grounded and biomedical-encoder retrieval systems.

</details>


### [201] [The Inference Engineering Pareto Atlas: Which Optimizations Dominate the Cost, Quality, and Latency Frontier?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17863)
*Srikanta Datta Tumkur,Jay Iyer,Mehar Simhadri,Sai Pavan Kumar,Sai Kapil Kumar,Ramesh Nampelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个覆盖成本、质量和延迟的 Pareto 前沿图谱，用于系统比较和组合 LLM 推理优化技术。作者测量了 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 在 vLLM 0.12 上、跨 L4/A100/H100 三种 GPU 的 54 种配置，并用这些锚点校准模拟器（跨批次漂移低于 1.5%），结合独立质量测试筛选出不同部署约束下的最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理优化报告的速度提升依赖于不同的模型、GPU、提示词和质量指标，彼此难以比较或组合，导致实际部署时难以判断何种优化真正有效且质量可控。作者希望建立一个统一、可校准、可预测的评估框架，以识别在不同部署约束（延迟、吞吐、成本）下的最佳配置。

Method: 构建"成本-质量-延迟" Pareto 图谱：对 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 在 vLLM 0.12 上，跨 L4、A100、H100 三种 GPU 实测 54 种配置作为锚点，校准一个模拟器（在锚定批次大小上复现实测，跨批次漂移低于 1.5%）。质量评估单独测试 FP16、AWQ 4bit、FP8 权重和 FP8 KV cache，在 200 道 GSM8K 题目上（每题 5 个示例）进行；稀疏注意力仅在模拟中评估。共考察 36 种配置，分析其是否达到 Pareto 前沿。

Result: 在 36 种配置中有 18 种达到 Pareto 前沿；组合方法（9/15）比单一方法（9/21）更常达到前沿。质量测试改变了胜者：AWQ 4bit 在 L4 上将每 token 延迟降至基线的 0.34 倍，但严格 GSM8K 准确率下降 5.9 个百分点，在采样不确定性下差一点未达到 95% 质量下限；灵活的答案提取与 FP16 准确率相当，表明损失来自格式而非算术。FP8 权重保留基线 99.4% 的准确率，延迟为基线的 0.61–0.65 倍（三块 GPU 均如此），并出现在四种部署场景中的三种胜者中。朴素的 FP8 KV cache 吞吐正常但对 200 道题全错，说明仅有速度不足。两种提示设计下 n-gram 推测解码测得为基线的 0.90–0.98 倍，在本技术栈上未带来收益。最优选择取决于约束与 GPU：H100 在严格延迟约束下胜出，A100 在吞吐和低成本下胜出（0.106 美元/百万 token）。

Conclusion: 通过统一的 Pareto 图谱框架，本文揭示了推理优化中"速度"与"质量"常被割裂评估的问题——例如 FP8 KV cache 和 n-gram 推测解码的纯速度优势在质量或综合收益上并不成立，而 FP8 权重在本技术栈中表现稳健。结论是：最佳推理配置高度依赖具体部署约束（延迟、吞吐、成本）和硬件，需要以质量下限为前提进行联合评估，而非仅优化单一速度指标。

Abstract: LLM inference optimizations report speedups on different models, GPUs, prompts, and quality metrics, making them hard to compare or combine. We build a cost, quality, and latency Pareto atlas to identify the best configurations for different deployment constraints. Since exhaustive testing is impractical, we measure 54 configurations of Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct running on vLLM 0.12 across L4, A100, and H100 GPUs and use these anchors to calibrate a simulator. It reproduces measurements at anchored batch sizes, with cross campaign drift below 1.5 percent. A separate quality evaluation tests FP16, AWQ 4bit, FP8 weights, and FP8 KV cache on 200 GSM8K questions with five examples per prompt. Sparse attention is evaluated only in simulation. On the calibrated grid, 18 of 36 configurations reach the Pareto frontier. Combined methods reach it more often than individual methods, with 9 of 15 combinations versus 9 of 21 single methods. Quality testing changes the winners. AWQ 4bit reduces per token latency to 0.34 times baseline on L4 but loses 5.9 percent of strict GSM8K accuracy, narrowly missing the 95 percent quality floor within sampling uncertainty. Flexible answer extraction matches FP16 accuracy, suggesting the loss comes from formatting rather than arithmetic. FP8 weights retain 99.4 percent of baseline accuracy at 0.61 to 0.65 times baseline latency across all three GPUs and appear in three of four regime winners. A naive FP8 KV cache maintains normal throughput but answers none of the 200 questions correctly, showing why speed alone is insufficient. Under two prompt designs, n gram speculative decoding measures at 0.90 to 0.98 times baseline and adds no benefit on this stack. The best choice depends on the constraint and GPU: H100 wins for tight latency, while A100 wins for throughput and low cost at 0.106 dollars per million tokens.

</details>


### [202] [Do Frontier Models Seek Safety Evidence Before Acting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17865)
*Omer Tafveez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出 SAFE 基准，考察模型在部署前是否会主动获取安全相关证据，发现不同前沿模型（GPT-5.5、o3、Claude Opus 4.8、Sonnet 4.6）存在明显差异的证据获取策略，且行为受严重程度与检索成本影响大、受概率影响小，其行为与解释之间存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估大多关注模型在安全信息"已置于上下文中"之后如何响应，而忽略了更早的决策点——模型是否会在行动之前主动获取安全相关证据。部署时安全的真正前提不仅在于对已知风险的响应，还在于模型能否获取用以判断"行动是安全的"所需的证据。

Method: 作者构建了受控基准 SAFE，让模型在证据可选（检索成本、风险概率、严重程度和呈现方式均可变）的条件下做出部署决策；在 GPT-5.5、o3、Claude Opus 4.8 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6 上进行了评估，并通过成本-义务分解与反事实干预分析行为与解释之间的关系。

Result: 各模型呈现不同的证据获取策略：Opus 几乎默认检查，o3 最倾向跳过且对阈值敏感，GPT-5.5 与 Sonnet 居中。检查行为随严重程度显著上升、随检索成本下降，而概率影响很弱（概率从 10% 升到 70% 最多只改变 21 个百分点）。尽管策略各异，各模型的阶段一理由都以期望值推理为主；成本-义务分解显示规避主要由检索摩擦和对部署回报的显性威胁驱动，而非"知情"带来的补救义务。反事实干预揭示行为与解释错配：证据措辞可在检查边界附近强烈改变决策却几乎不被提及，而概率虽常被引用却几乎没有因果影响。

Conclusion: 部署时安全不仅取决于模型如何响应已知风险，还取决于模型是否主动获取必要的安全证据；行为解释与实际因果机制之间存在显著偏差，提示需要关注模型在"知"与"行"之间的证据获取环节。

Abstract: Frontier models are often evaluated on how they respond to safety information once it is already in context. We study an earlier decision point: whether models choose to acquire safety-relevant evidence before acting. We introduce SAFE, a controlled benchmark in which models make deployment decisions with optional evidence that varies in retrieval cost, probability, severity, and presentation. Across GPT-5.5, o3, Claude Opus 4.8, and Claude Sonnet 4.6, we find distinct evidence-acquisition policies: Opus inspects nearly by default, o3 is the most skip-heavy and threshold-sensitive, and GPT-5.5 and Sonnet occupy intermediate regimes. Inspection increases strongly with severity and decreases with retrieval cost, whereas probability has much weaker behavioral influence: increasing the stated likelihood of a problem from 10% to 70% changes inspection by at most 21 percentage points. Despite these differences, Stage 1 rationales are dominated by expected-value reasoning across models. A cost-obligation decomposition further shows that avoidance is driven primarily by retrieval friction and explicit threats to the deployment payoff rather than by the remediation duties created by knowing. Counterfactual interventions reveal a further mismatch between behavior and explanation: evidence framing can strongly change decisions near the inspection boundary while going largely unmentioned, whereas probability is frequently cited despite having little causal influence.
