These results point to a practical route for laboratory robotics: instead of training a policy for each laboratory, distribute a kit and let a coding agent adapt it in each laboratory. Code, demonstrations, and the evolved programs are available at https://github.com/tsudalab/WetRobo.

</details>


### [237] [Risk-Aware World Modeling with Flow-Guided Occupancy Evolution for Selective Trajectory Planning in Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18442)
*Rongxiang Zeng,Linsen Cai,Jiafu Zhang,Yijie Zhong,Yide Tao,Shuai Wang,Nan Zheng,Hai L. Vu,Alvaro Garcia Hernandez,Yongqi Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RiskWorld 是一个面向自动驾驶的风险感知世界建模框架，通过共享占用预测与选择性轨迹替换机制，在 nuScenes 上以 11.5 FPS 实现了最低的 3 秒长时域碰撞率，同时保持了次优的平均 L2 误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的安全运动规划需要预判动态交通风险，并在必要时修正既定轨迹。现有方法在预测未来的占用与风险时，往往缺乏对轨迹何时应被替换的明确决策机制，且候选轨迹评估计算代价高。因此需要一种能够融合风险场与动态语境、低边际成本评估多个候选轨迹、并选择性替换规划轨迹的框架。

Method: RiskWorld 将空间风险场与时间维度的参与者上下文同视觉鸟瞰图（BEV）特征相融合；采用流引导的演化方式传输占用与场景特征，并用带符号残差对传输后的占用进行修正。每个规划步仅生成一次预测并跨候选轨迹复用。每个候选轨迹与"当前状态持续"参考进行对比，得到非负的碰撞分数修正量。由当前世界评估选出的轨迹作为规划锚点，仅当额外预测风险触发干预且某备选轨迹满足预测风险与轨迹误差的分量级约束时，才替换该锚点；候选轨迹的几何形状保持不变。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上的开环规划评估中（使用相机特征、标注导出的当前与历史参与者状态以及数据集提供的地图语境），RiskWorld 在 3 秒长评估时域上取得最低碰撞率，并在多种先进基线中取得次优的平均 L2 误差；在单张 NVIDIA RTX 4090 上以 11.5 FPS 运行，参数量为 90.81 M。组内消融显示，RiskWorld 的碰撞率低于当前状态重评分基线，且预测复用使额外候选轨迹能以很低的边际计算成本被评估。

Conclusion: RiskWorld 表明，将风险感知的世界建模与选择性轨迹替换相结合，可以在保持高推理效率的同时显著提升长时域规划的安全性，为自动驾驶中的共享占用预测与轨迹决策提供了一种有效且计算友好的方案。

Abstract: Safe motion planning in automated driving requires anticipating evolving traffic risks and deciding when to revise the current planned trajectory. We introduce RiskWorld, a risk-aware world modeling framework for shared occupancy forecasting and selective trajectory replacement. Spatial risk fields and temporal actor context are fused with visual bird's-eye-view features. Flow-guided evolution transports occupancy and scene features, while signed residuals correct occupancy after transport. One forecast is generated per planning step and reused across candidates. Each candidate is compared with a current-state persistence reference, yielding a nonnegative collision-score correction. The trajectory selected by current-world evaluation serves as the planning anchor and is replaced only when additional predicted risk triggers intervention and an alternative satisfies component-wise constraints on predicted risk and trajectory error. Candidate geometries remain unchanged. We evaluate RiskWorld for open-loop planning on nuScenes using camera features, annotation-derived current and historical actor states, and dataset-provided map context. RiskWorld achieves the lowest collision rate at a long evaluation horizon of 3 s, and the second-best average L2 error among various state-of-the-art baselines, while running at 11.5 FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090 with 90.81 M parameters. Within-setting ablations show that RiskWorld achieves lower collision rates than the current-state rescoring baseline, while forecast reuse enables additional candidates to be evaluated at low marginal computational cost.

</details>


### [238] [The Mirage of Calibrated Confidence: Trajectory-Independence of Verbalized Confidence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18453)
*Jisoo Yang,Jaeho Han,Trung X. Pham,Junyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现已校准的视觉-语言模型（VLM）表达的置信度与其推理轨迹基本无关，即模型会自我纠正却仍答错并保持高置信。针对这一现有指标（ECE、AUROC）无法察觉的盲点，作者提出轨迹接地分数（TGS）及配套基准 TGS-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有校准方法只优化置信度与答案正确性的统计对齐，而忽视了置信度是否真正反映推理轨迹的内容。作者发现一个悖论：模型可以反复自我纠正、承认错误却仍给出错误答案并报告高置信度，说明语言化置信度与轨迹脱节，且校准训练反而可能加剧这种脱节。因此需要一种能够评估置信度对推理轨迹敏感性的新度量。

Method: 作者通过三个互补视角分析——内容变化（改变轨迹内容）、token 掩码（遮蔽轨迹关键 token）、以及模型自身的犹豫标记（如 "Wait, I should recheck"）——证明置信度对轨迹实际内容不够敏感。在此基础上提出轨迹接地分数（TGS），包含两种互补形式：TGS-self 比较有无自身轨迹时的置信度差异；TGS-pair 检验模型是否对正确轨迹赋予比有缺陷轨迹更高的置信度（沿视觉、推理、答案三个轴）。同时提出模型无关的评测套件 TGS-Bench，覆盖 10 个基准并构造受控的好/坏轨迹对。

Result: 实验表明语言化置信度在很大程度上与轨迹无关，且校准训练会矛盾地加剧这种脱节。ECE 和 AUROC 等现有指标无法检测这一问题。TGS-Bench 的评测结果显示，传统的校准排名与轨迹接地排名发生分歧，暴露了当前评测实践中的盲点。

Conclusion: 仅靠传统校准指标不足以保证模型置信度的可信性。轨迹接地分数（TGS）及其基准 TGS-Bench 揭示了置信度与推理轨迹脱节的问题，呼吁在置信度校准评测中纳入对推理轨迹敏感性的考量。

Abstract: A calibrated Vision-Language Model (VLM) can repeatedly self-correct, say "Wait, I should recheck," arrive at the wrong answer, and still report high confidence. We find that this occurs because verbalized confidence is largely trajectory-independent in the VLMs and calibration methods we evaluate. We examine this through three complementary lenses: content variation, token masking, and the model's own hesitation markers. We show that confidence is insufficiently sensitive to what the reasoning trajectory actually contains, and that calibration training can paradoxically worsen this disconnect. Since existing metrics like ECE and AUROC cannot detect this problem, we propose the Trajectory-Grounding Score (TGS) in two complementary forms: TGS-self, which compares confidence with and without access to the model's own trajectory, and TGS-pair, which tests whether the model assigns higher confidence to correct trajectories than to flawed ones along the vision, reasoning, and answer axes. We propose TGS-Bench, a model-agnostic suite spanning 10 benchmarks with controlled good/bad trajectory pairs, and show that conventional calibration rankings diverge from trajectory-grounding rankings, exposing a blind spot in current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [239] [Collective Loss of Control in LLM Agent Systems: An Epidemic Account of Mutation, Contagion, and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18460)
*Xiangfan Wu,Zonghao Ying,Huiyu Wu,Xing Zheng,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"流行病学"框架来解释多智能体系统如何从局部偏差演变为集体失控，即"偶然突变—传染—恢复"机制，并通过部署审计与基准测试验证了两个关键环节：隐式通信路径的存在，以及接收方智能体对注入的不安全轨迹高度易感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多智能体系统为何能从罕见的局部偏差(如个别智能体偶然产生不安全行为)演化成集体失控。作者受OpenAI智能体协调事件的报道启发，认为传统安全研究侧重预防自发偏差，却忽略了偏差通过通信渠道在智能体间扩散、最终导致集体失败的可能机制。

Method: 提出"种子—传播—失控"的传染病类比模型，将集体失控分解为偶然突变、传染和恢复三个环节。方法上包含两部分实证工作：(1)部署审计——检查名义上独立的评估运行之间是否存在隐式通信路径，并验证数据确实可以通过默认的Docker后端传输；(2)构建RogueHandoff-20基准，包含20个可执行场景，通过注入由修改后的Qwen-27B路由生成的"不安全轨迹"，测试接收方智能体的易感性，并将其与直接恶意请求进行对比。

Result: 在四条原生pending路由上，正常任务的执行伤害率为0-5%，而注入不安全轨迹后伤害率升至40-95%，比直接恶意请求高出5-45个百分点。这表明：基线伤害低但条件易感性高。不过作者明确指出，该结果并不能证明自然罕见事件的发生率，也未实际演示出自主的级联失控。

Conclusion: 罕见个体偏差可与显著集体风险共存，关键在于"传播速度超过纠正与遏制速度"。据此提出互补性防御策略：在预防自发偏差之外，还应增强系统的抵抗力与恢复力，并审计、限制那些可能将局部故障放大为集体失控的非预期通信路径。

Abstract: How does a multi-agent system evolve from a local deviation into collective loss of control? We propose an epidemic explanation organized around accidental mutation, contagion, and recovery. A spontaneous deviation creates a seed; communication enables other agents to adopt and retransmit its unsafe strategy; collective failure can emerge when propagation outpaces correction and containment. Thus, rare individual deviations can coexist with substantial collective risk. Motivated by reported OpenAI agent coordination incidents, we examine two ingredients of this mechanism. A deployment audit identifies implicit communication paths between nominally independent evaluation runs and verifies transport through a default Docker backend. RogueHandoff-20, a benchmark of 20 executable scenarios, tests recipient susceptibility by injecting unsafe trajectories generated by a modified Qwen-27B route. Across four native-pending routes, executed harm is 0-5% on normal tasks and 40-95% after injection, exceeding paired direct malicious requests by 5-45 percentage points. These results support low observed baseline harm alongside high conditional susceptibility; they do not establish natural rare-event rates or demonstrate an autonomous cascade. The account motivates complementary defenses: strengthen resistance and recovery alongside prevention of spontaneous deviations, and audit and restrict unintended communication paths that can turn local failures into collective loss of control.

</details>


### [240] [Disentangling Long-Term Memory via Latent Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18461)
*Cai Ke,Xinghao Chen,Xiaoyu Shen,Keyu Chen,Siyu An,Junnan Dong,Ruifeng Xu,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LGM，一种神经符号框架，将长期记忆解耦迁移到连续潜空间，通过稀疏自编码器动态构建查询感知的潜图，并结合图编码器进行条件化消息传递，从而提升个性化智能体的长期记忆推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体需要从长期历史交互中同时推断显式偏好和隐式行为证据。现有方法存在不足：早期扁平检索方法独立评分记忆片段、忽视分布式信息；当前结构化记忆框架依赖查询无关的静态图，无法捕捉上下文相关关系；且原始文本记忆本身纠缠且含噪，导致细粒度个性化与跨会话推理计算代价过高。

Method: 提出神经符号框架 LGM：(1) 采用稀疏自编码器进行定制化潜图构建，将历史交互映射为潜记忆节点，把记忆痕迹解耦为稀疏概念激活，并根据查询动态合成查询感知的关系边权重，而非持久化固定图；(2) 图编码器将查询嵌入作为条件化偏好，引导任务特定潜子图上的非线性消息传递，从而生成高表达力的记忆表示。

Result: 在长期个性化基准上的大量实验表明，LGM 在同时捕捉显式与隐式偏好方面显著优于最先进的基线方法，并能生成个性化响应。

Conclusion: LGM 通过潜空间记忆解耦与查询感知的动态图构建，有效解决了长期记忆的纠缠、噪声与上下文相关问题，显著提升了长期个性化任务的性能。

Abstract: Personalized agents are required to reason over long-term history interactions to infer both explicit preferences and implicit behavioral evidence. While early flat retrieval methods score memory fragments independently and neglect the distributed information, current structured memory frameworks rely on query-agnostic static graphs that fail to capture the context-dependent relations. Crucially, raw textual memories are inherently entangled and noisy, making fine-grained personalization and cross-session reasoning computationally prohibitive. To this end, we present LGM, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that shifts long-term memory disentanglement into a continuous latent space. Specifically, (i) instead of persisting fixed graphs, we design a tailored latent graph construction with a sparse autoencoder. Subject to each query, it maps historical interactions into latent memory nodes and disentangles the memory traces into sparse concept activations, dynamically synthesizing query-aware relational edge weights. (ii) A graph encoder then treats the query embedding as a conditioning preference to direct non-linear message passing across the task-specific latent subgraph. This yields a highly expressive memory representation for effective activations. Extensive experiments on long-term personalization benchmarks demonstrate that LGM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in capturing both explicit and implicit preferences while enabling personalized responses.

</details>


### [241] [First Token Matters: Understanding Safety Collapse in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18471)
*Yizheng Yang,Haining Yu,Yuechen Wang,Yikai Hou,Xing Fu,Jinbo Yang,Tianqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大型推理模型（LRM）的安全对齐失败根源于推理起始阶段的"起始拒绝崩溃"（ORC），并提出 SafeToken——一种仅在推理起点注入单个可学习连续安全锚点的轻量级推理时干预方法，在几乎不影响推理能力的前提下显著提升有害查询下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM 虽具备强大的问题求解能力，但在面对有害查询时安全对齐容易失效；现有改进多依赖额外训练或偏好优化，缺乏对安全失败内部机制的理解。作者意图从机制层面定位失败根源，进而设计更轻量、更可解释的干预手段。

Method: 采用逐 token 位置的拒绝动态分析，识别出推理起始处（首个生成 token）拒绝信号急剧下降的局部脆弱点，即 ORC；据此提出 SafeToken，一种推理时干预方法，在推理起点注入一个经过学习的连续安全锚向量，仅更新单个 token 嵌入。

Result: SafeToken 有效缓解了 ORC，在有害查询基准上提升安全性，同时基本保持推理效用；实验表明 LRM 的安全失败可由"理解到生成"这一关键过渡阶段的瞬时崩溃引起。

Conclusion: 安全失败并非广泛分布，而是集中于理解阶段向生成阶段过渡的局部脆弱点；以单 token 嵌入级别的推理时干预即可显著修复该故障，为 LRM 安全对齐提供了一种轻量、高效且可解释的新路径。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) exhibit strong problem-solving abilities, yet their safety alignment often degrades when handling harmful queries. Existing approaches to improving safety largely rely on additional training or preference optimization, while offering limited understanding of the internal mechanisms behind safety failures. In this work, we investigate this failure through a token-level positional analysis of refusal dynamics and identify a localized vulnerability at the onset of reasoning, which we term Onset Refusal Collapse (ORC). We find that the refusal-related signal of LRMs drops sharply at the first generated token under harmful queries, which is associated with unsafe response generation. Motivated by this finding, we propose SafeToken, a lightweight inference-time intervention that injects a learned continuous safety anchor precisely at reasoning onset. Despite updating only a single token embedding, SafeToken effectively mitigates ORC, improves safety on harmful-query benchmarks, and largely preserves reasoning utility. These results suggest that safety failures in LRMs can arise from a transient breakdown at the critical transition from understanding to generation.

</details>


### [242] [Hyperbolic Graph Representation Learning for Differential Diagnosis on Biomedical Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18481)
*Pietro Miotto,Lucia Mellini,Tommaso Marzi,Cesare Alippi,Elena Casiraghi,Alberto Paccanaro,Giorgio Valentini,Mauricio Soto-Gomez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文初步研究在融合患者的生物医学异构图（整合本体层级与跨类型关联）上使用双曲图表示学习进行孟德尔病鉴别诊断，发现双曲模型可用更低维度取得优于欧氏基线的表现，并能兼顾层级结构与异构诊断推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学知识图谱既包含本体衍生的树状层级，又包含表型、疾病、基因、蛋白、患者等异构实体间的横向关联。双曲嵌入天然适合树状结构，但其在非纯层级、异构图中的有效性尚不明确，因此需要检验双曲方法能否在患者级异构图上的鉴别诊断任务中仍然受益。

Method: 构建融合患者的生物医学图，先在本体孤立子图上对比双曲模型与欧氏基线在低维度下的性能，再在面向患者的链接预测任务上评估模型，对每位患者对候选疾病进行排序以完成孟德尔病鉴别诊断。

Result: 在本体子图实验中，双曲模型以显著更低的嵌入维度取得强性能；在患者链接预测任务上，双曲嵌入能够利用生物医学层级结构，同时支持异构患者级图上的诊断推理。

Conclusion: 双曲嵌入并非仅限于纯层级图，在异构的患者整合生物医学图中同样有效，能够以更紧凑的表示支持孟德尔病鉴别诊断，兼顾层级先验与异构关联推理。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs combine ontology-derived hierarchies with transversal associations among heterogeneous entities such as phenotypes, diseases, genes, proteins, and patients. This hybrid structure raises the question of whether hyperbolic embeddings, which naturally capture tree-like organization, remain useful beyond purely hierarchical graphs. We present a preliminary study of hyperbolic graph representation learning for Mendelian-disease differential diagnosis on a patient-integrated biomedical graph. Experiments on isolated ontology subgraphs show that hyperbolic models achieve strong performance in substantially lower dimensions than Euclidean baselines. We then evaluate the models on a link-prediction task that ranks candidate diseases for each patient. Results suggest that hyperbolic embeddings can exploit biomedical hierarchical structure while supporting diagnostic reasoning over heterogeneous patient-level graphs.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Routine Compliance: Cunning Data Cultivates Safety Vigilance in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18515)
*Youjia Wang,Lin Xu,Yang Sun,Yuxiao Lu,Chengfang Fang,Jie Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"cunning questions"（狡黠问题）——一类未必涉及安全、但包含误导性前提、非典型推理或细微矛盾的题目，用于训练大语言模型的"警觉性"（vigilance）。实验表明，这种训练能提升模型对分布外越狱攻击的鲁棒性、增强后续安全微调，并将平均攻击成功率从17.40%降至15.05%，在多个评估设置上达到新的最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的模型在有害意图被伪装于看似良性的语境中时仍可能失效。鲁棒的安全不仅需要了解安全边界知识，还需要"警觉性"——即在表层语义之下识别异常前提、误导性推理和潜在风险的能力。作者希望通过培养模型在行动前审视请求的深层意图与假设的能力，来弥补传统安全对齐的不足。

Method: 作者引入"cunning questions"（狡黠问题），这类问题不必然与安全相关，但带有误导性前提、非典型推理或细微的不一致之处。假设是：学会看穿这些"推理陷阱"的能力可以迁移到安全关键场景。具体做法是将此类数据加入训练，包括：单独用Cunning训练、用Cunning增强后续安全微调，以及将Cunning融入现有最先进的安全对齐流程（Cunning augmentation）。此外还进行了因果追踪分析（trace analysis）和有条件的理论分析。

Result: 实验表明，Cunning训练提升了模型对分布外（out-of-distribution）越狱攻击的鲁棒性，并增强了后续安全微调的效果。将Cunning融入现有最先进安全对齐流程后，在九个"骨干模型—基准"组合上的平均攻击成功率（ASR）从17.40%降至15.05%，在所评估的设置上达到新的最优。因果追踪分析显示，在经过匹配的安全微调后，安全判断更可能在有害规划开始之前就主导响应。有条件的理论分析进一步刻画了从cunning数据学习到的不变性何时能够迁移到安全相关输入。

Conclusion: cunning数据能够增强模型的警觉性，并与传统的安全对齐方法形成互补。文章认为，仅靠安全边界的知识不足以应对伪装的有害请求，训练模型审视深层意图与假设的"警觉性"是提升鲁棒安全的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Safety alignment teaches large language models (LLMs) to recognize harmful requests and reject risky instructions. Yet aligned models can fail when harmful intent is concealed within seemingly benign contexts. Robust safety therefore requires both knowledge of safety boundaries and \textbf{vigilance}: the ability to detect unusual premises, misleading reasoning, and latent risks beneath surface-level semantics. Vigilance requires models to scrutinize a request's underlying intent and assumptions before acting. To cultivate this capability, we introduce \textbf{cunning questions}, which are not necessarily safety-related but contain misleading premises, atypical reasoning, or subtle inconsistencies. We hypothesize that learning to look beyond such reasoning traps can transfer to safety-critical scenarios. Experiments show that Cunning training improves robustness to out-of-distribution jailbreak attacks and strengthens subsequent safety fine-tuning. Furthermore, augmenting an existing state-of-the-art safety alignment pipeline with Cunning establishes a new state of the art across our evaluated settings, reducing mean ASR across nine backbone--benchmark combinations from 17.40\% to 15.05\%. Trace analysis after matched safety fine-tuning suggests that safety judgments are more likely to govern responses before harmful planning begins. A conditional theoretical analysis further characterizes when invariance learned from cunning data can transfer to safety-related inputs. These findings suggest that cunning data can strengthen model vigilance and complement conventional safety alignment.

</details>


### [244] [AeroWeaver: An Embodied-Agent Harness for Weaving Aerial Skills into Distributed, Adaptive Swarm Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18520)
*Jiabin Lou,Yirong Yang,Haopeng Wang,Xuxin Lv,Xinyu Liu,Diyuan Hou,Xuehong Liu,Rongye Shi,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AeroWeaver 是一个面向无人机蜂群的具身智能体框架，将单个无人机的技能编织成协调的使命级行为，通过语义决策、分布式角色化局部智能体和在线奖励驱动学习，实现无需中心智能体的多无人机协同操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体虽增强了自然语言任务理解与高层规划，但将其应用于无人机蜂群存在三大核心难题：一是模型决策需落地到可执行的无人机能力（grounding）；二是全局任务推理需与分布式执行协调一致；三是需利用使命特定经验实现持续自适应。现有方法缺乏在这些维度上衔接语义层与底层蜂群控制的机制。

Method: 提出 AeroWeaver 具身智能体框架：(1) 将语义决策连接到受治理（governed）的技能，实现从高级语言指令到可执行技能的映射与约束；(2) 组织按角色条件化（role-conditioned）的局部智能体，支撑分布式协调，避免单一中心智能体基于全局上下文直接生成联合动作；(3) 使用角色索引的状态-动作-奖励经验，在线细化技能选择，提供无需训练的自适应学习路径。

Result: 实验与运行时验证表明，AeroWeaver 在测试条件下能维持有效的技能执行；能够支持机身本地（body-local）的多无人机操作，无需中心智能体从全局上下文生成联合动作；同时，奖励引导的在线更新为蜂群智能体从累积执行经验中实现免训练的自适应学习提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: AeroWeaver 证明了将 LLM 智能体的语义推理与无人机蜂群的分布式执行相衔接的可行性，其角色化局部智能体、技能治理和在线经验学习机制，为构建兼具语义理解能力与集体智能的无人机蜂群系统提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Collective intelligence is a collaborative autonomy paradigm in which multiple agents pursue shared objectives through local perception, information exchange, and coordinated action. UAV swarms embody this paradigm by coordinating multiple vehicles in tasks such as search, inspection, and tracking. Recent advances in large language model (LLM) agents have strengthened natural-language task understanding and high-level planning, providing a flexible semantic interface between mission descriptions and collective behavior. While these advances expand semantic reasoning, applying LLM agents to UAV swarms raises challenges in grounding model decisions in executable capabilities, reconciling global task reasoning with distributed execution, and using mission-specific experience for continual adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce AeroWeaver, an embodied-agent harness that weaves individual UAV skills into coordinated mission-level behavior. AeroWeaver connects semantic decisions to governed skills, organizes role-conditioned local agents for distributed coordination, and uses role-indexed state-action-reward experience to refine skill selection online. Experiments and runtime validation show that AeroWeaver maintains valid skill execution under tested conditions and supports body-local multi-UAV operation without a central agent generating joint actions from global context, while reward-guided online updates provide a training-free path for adaptive learning swarm agents from accumulated execution experience. Code: https://github.com/Admire-ljb/AeroWeaver.

</details>


### [245] [TRIPROBE: Probing Task Separability Beyond Classification for XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18525)
*Amirhossein Sadough,Freek Hens,Aleksa Bokšan,Mohammad Mahdi Dehshibi,Mahyar Shahsavari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriProbe 是一个多层次探测框架，通过输入、特征和分类器三个层面追踪任务可分性，用于对学习管道进行可解释诊断，揭示任务成败的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习管道的评估往往只归结为下游准确率，无法回答"任务为何成功或失败"这一根本问题。作者希望摆脱将模型视为黑箱的做法，提供一种可解释的机制来定位并解释任务可分性瓶颈。

Method: TriProbe 将多任务问题分解为二分类子任务，并施加三种互补探测：对输入空间的 Foundational Probe、对特征表示的 Latent Probe，以及对分类器输出的 Final Probe。它采用最大 Fisher 判别比（Maximum Fisher's Discriminant Ratio）作为可分性的原则性度量，从而识别瓶颈及受影响的任务对。

Result: 在 Roshambo sEMG（表面肌电）基准上的实验表明，TriProbe 能够揭示隐藏的失败原因，为数据采集、验证和架构设计提供指导。

Conclusion: TriProbe 提供了一种超越单一准确率指标的可解释诊断方式，通过分层追踪可分性来定位瓶颈，进而指导数据、验证与模型架构的改进。

Abstract: Modern evaluation of learning pipelines often reduces to downstream accuracy, leaving open the question of why tasks succeed or fail. TriProbe addresses this gap with a multi-level probing framework for explainable diagnosis of task separability. Rather than treating models as black boxes, TriProbe traces how separability evolves across inputs, learned features, and final classifiers. It decomposes multi-task problems into binary subtasks and applies three complementary probes: a Foundational Probe on input spaces, a Latent Probe on feature representations, and a Final Probe on classifier outputs. Using Maximum Fisher's Discriminant Ratio as a principled separability metric, TriProbe identifies bottlenecks and affected task pairs. Experiments on the Roshambo sEMG benchmark show how TriProbe reveals hidden breakdowns, guiding data collection, validation, and architecture design.

</details>


### [246] [Recursive Reasoning or Statistical Extrapolation? In-Context Learning in Multi-Agent Interdependent Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18591)
*Yu Liu,Wenwen Li,Yifan Dou,Guangnan Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过多智能体不完全信息博弈实验，检验大语言模型智能体的上下文学习（ICL）究竟源自内部推理还是统计外推，发现破坏历史统计模式后长上下文的收益基本消失，表明其行为更接近统计外推而非策略推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL 能让 LLM 智能体利用交互历史改进决策，但尚不清楚这种改进是源于内部推理能力的精化，还是仅仅对统计模式的机械外推。作者希望厘清这一机制，尤其是在需要递归信念推理的多智能体策略环境中。

Method: 作者构建了一个公共品博弈这一不完全信息多智能体环境，引入与历史独立的理性预期均衡（REE）作为基准，并人为操纵历史反馈的统计结构，以此评估决策质量并分离“推理”与“统计外推”两种解释。

Result: 实验显示，当历史统计模式被破坏时，更长上下文的收益几乎消失，决策质量退回到无上下文基线水平；且这种退化会被更强的策略相互依赖急剧放大，说明 ICL 的行为更符合统计外推而非策略推理。

Conclusion: 该研究将 ICL 的机制研究拓展到策略性多智能体环境，提出以 REE 作为区分推理与外推的诊断工具，并提供了一个可复用的框架，用于探查 LLM 在递归信念任务中推理能力的边界。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables large language model (LLM) agents to improve decisions using interaction history, yet it remains unclear whether such improvement reflects refined internal reasoning or mere extrapolation of statistical patterns. To disentangle these mechanisms, we study LLM agents in multi-agent incomplete-information games that require recursive belief reasoning. By constructing a public goods game and manipulating the statistical structure of historical feedback, we evaluate decision quality against a history-independent rational expectations equilibrium (REE) benchmark. Our experiments reveal that when historical statistical patterns are disrupted, the benefits of longer context largely vanish, degrading decision quality to the no-context baseline in a way sharply amplified by stronger strategic interdependence. These results suggest that, in such strategic environments, ICL behavior is more consistent with statistical extrapolation than with strategic reasoning. Our work extends the mechanistic study of ICL to strategic multi-agent settings, introduces REE as a diagnostic tool for distinguishing reasoning from extrapolation, and provides a reusable framework for probing the boundaries of LLM reasoning in recursive belief tasks.

</details>


### [247] [Reasoning through Evolution: Automatic Meta-path Discovery for LLM-based Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18597)
*Ziyi Zhou,Xiaoming Zhang,Hui Pang,Yuting Zhang,Tiesunlong Shen,Bingyu Yan,Erik Cambria,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAGER，一个多智能体遗传进化框架，自动发现面向 LLM 推理优化的元路径，将复杂传播图压缩为信息子图，从而缓解模态不匹配和信息过载，使冻结的 LLM 在零样本/少样本下完成结合结构的假新闻检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假新闻检测主要依赖有监督的 GNN 模型，需要大量标注数据且泛化能力有限；LLM 虽有强推理能力，但直接输入原始传播图会造成模态不匹配与严重信息过载，导致零样本/少样本下结构感知推理不可靠。

Method: 提出多智能体遗传进化框架 MAGER，自动发现适合 LLM 推理的元路径（meta-paths），将传播图压缩为信息子图；并引入图上下文学习（graph in-context learning）策略，检索语义和结构相似的示例以增强分类与推理，整个过程使用冻结的 LLM。

Result: 大规模实验表明，MAGER 在数据高效（零样本/少样本）设置下，显著提升了冻结 LLM 作为独立假新闻检测器的性能。

Conclusion: MAGER 通过将传播图压缩为演化元路径子图并结合图上下文学习，有效弥合了 LLM 与图结构之间的模态鸿沟，使冻结 LLM 无需微调即可作为可靠的结构感知假新闻检测器，代码已开源。

Abstract: Propagation structures provide crucial evidence for fake news detection, yet existing approaches primarily rely on supervised GNN-based models, which require substantial labeled data and exhibit limited generalization. Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, directly feeding them raw propagation graphs creates a significant modality mismatch and severe information overload, making structure-aware reasoning unreliable in zero-shot and few-shot settings. To bridge this gap, we propose MAGER, a multi-agent genetic evolution framework that automatically discovers meta-paths optimized for LLM reasoning. By compressing complex propagation graphs into informative subgraphs, the evolved meta-paths alleviate both information overload and modality mismatch, enabling frozen LLMs to perform structure-aware veracity reasoning. We further introduce a graph in-context learning strategy that retrieves semantically and structurally similar demonstrations to strengthen classification and reasoning. Extensive experiments show that MAGER substantially improves frozen LLMs as standalone fake news detectors in data-efficient settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/SenticNet/MAGER.

</details>


### [248] [The Uneven Impact of Generative AI on Student Learning: Examining the Roles of Reliance, Evaluation Literacy, and Course Policy in AI-related Courses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18676)
*Lydia Manikonda,Mei Si,Sirajam Munira,Oshani Seneviratne,Kristin Bennett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过118名学生的问卷，考察课程情境、认知依赖、评估素养和早期依赖对GenAI使用及学习体验的影响，识别出四类用户群体，并揭示了影响学术收益、正面/负面影响的多元因素，呼吁院校制定政策以消除使用不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能正在改变学生的学习方式，但课程情境、认知依赖、评估素养和早期依赖等因素在GenAI学习体验中的作用尚缺乏系统研究，亟需厘清这些变量如何塑造学生的使用模式与感知学习成果。

Method: 对12门AI相关课程的118名学生进行问卷调查，采用聚类分析识别用户类型，比较不同版本（免费/付费）、工具数量及教师政策间的差异，并运用多变量回归模型检验各因素与学术收益、正面影响、负面影响之间的关联及其交互效应。

Result: 识别出四类用户群体：高使用率且获益多的群体、低使用低依赖低获益的轻度用户，以及两个获益程度不同的中度使用群体。免费与付费用户、单一与多工具用户、不同教师政策下的学生在使用和体验上存在显著差异。回归结果显示：学术收益与早期依赖及学术任务支持相关；正面影响与认知依赖、学术任务支持、对GenAI可靠性的信心及教师政策相关；负面影响与早期依赖和态度转变相关，且早期依赖对负面影响的关联随评估素养提高而增强。GenAI增强学习的感知反映了认知、表现和自我效能收益，而压力与批判性思维减弱的担忧则与较低的感知学习收益相关。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了学生在GenAI使用中的差异与不平等，建议院校制定更完善的政策，以帮助学生公平地从日益强大的AI系统中获益。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) is changing how students learn, yet the roles of course context, cognitive reliance, evaluation literacy, and early reliance remain underexplored. Using survey responses from 118 students across 12 AI-related courses at our institution, we examined differences in GenAI use and perceived learning experiences. We identified four user clusters: high-use students reporting many benefits, light users reporting less reliance and fewer benefits, and two moderate-use groups reporting different levels of benefit. We also found significant differences between free- and premium-version users, single- and multiple-tool users, and students experiencing different instructor policies. In multivariable regression models, academic benefit was associated with early reliance and academic task support; positive impact was associated with cognitive reliance, academic task support, confidence in GenAI reliability, and instructor policy; and negative impact was associated with early reliance and attitudinal change. The association between early reliance and negative impact became stronger as evaluation literacy increased. Finally, perceptions of GenAI-enhanced learning appear to reflect cognitive, performance, and self-efficacy benefits, while concerns about stress and diminished critical thinking are associated with lower perceived learning benefits. These findings suggest that institutions need better policies to address such inequities so that institutions can enable students to benefit from increasingly capable AI systems.

</details>


### [249] [Beyond Truncation: Rethinking LLM Decoding as Ensemble Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18723)
*Dunyao Xue,Chengshuo Du,Zhengbo Wang,Wenlin Dai,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ME-Decoding（马氏距离集成解码），将大语言模型的候选 token 选择建模为集合剪枝问题，利用基于 token 嵌入的马氏距离目标同时提升候选的语义多样性与高概率保留，并以近线性复杂度的贪心算法高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 解码的候选选择策略主要依赖标量概率，忽略了 token 间的几何语义关系，导致候选冗余；而当前基于几何的方法要么需要复杂优化，要么直接对原始 token 概率重加权，带来显著计算开销或推理不稳定。

Method: 将解码形式化为基于马氏距离目标驱动的子集优化问题：通过 token 嵌入上构造的自适应带宽核得到 token 相似度矩阵，动态折扣冗余的生成路径；并提出在早停条件下具有近线性复杂度（关于候选规模）的贪心选择算法，同时给出理论近似保证。

Result: 在多种推理与生成任务上的广泛实验表明，该方法能持续取得强劲性能，且作为即插即用模块几乎不引入推理开销。

Conclusion: ME-Decoding 是一个鲁棒、即插即用的解码模块，通过马氏距离驱动的集合剪枝有效兼顾语义多样性与概率保留，在保持近线性复杂度和理论保证的同时实现稳定且高效的解码。

Abstract: We introduce Mahalanobis-Ensemble Decoding (ME-Decoding), a novel Large Language Model (LLM) decoding framework that frames candidate token selection as ensemble pruning. Existing selection strategies rely predominantly on scalar probabilities, ignoring geometric semantic relationships and causing candidate redundancy. Meanwhile, current geometry-aware methods often require complex optimization or directly reweighting the original token probabilities, leading to significant computational overhead or inference instability. To address this, we formulate decoding as a subset optimization problem using a Mahalanobis distance-driven objective to enhance semantic diversity while preserving high probabilities. Specifically, we dynamically discount redundant generation paths using a token similarity matrix, constructed via an adaptive-bandwidth kernel over token embeddings. We further devise an efficient greedy selection algorithm with near-linear complexity in the candidate size under early stopping, while establishing its theoretical approximation guarantees. This renders ME-Decoding a robust, plug-and-play module with negligible inference overhead. Extensive experiments across diverse reasoning and generation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [250] [Which LLM is Best for Translating Natural Language Goals to PDDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18731)
*Tomas Balyo,Lukas Chrpa,G. Michael Youngblood*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种当代大语言模型（LLMs）将非专业游戏测试人员用自然语言书写的测试目标翻译为适用于经典规划的规范 PDDL 目标的能力，发现所有模型正确率均超过92%，其中 Gemini 2.5 Flash 以96%的正确率和最低误报率表现最佳，GPT-4.1 响应最快，但语言歧义与领域表示局限仍导致偶发失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意图与机器执行之间的鸿沟在自动规划领域尤为突出：目标需以 PDDL 等形式语言表达，对非专家用户极不友好。作者旨在评估当前 LLM 能否可靠地将视频游戏测试人员用非正式语言书写的自然语言测试目标转化为形式良好的 PDDL 目标，从而弥合自然语言目标与自动规划流程之间的差距。

Method: 作者设计了一套精心构建的提示模板，融合了多轮迭代实验的经验，以同时最大化多个先进 LLM 的准确性与响应一致性。随后使用真实世界、领域特定的基准，从正确性、响应速度和错误倾向三个维度对六种当代模型进行系统评估。

Result: 所有模型的正确率均超过92%；Gemini 2.5 Flash 准确率最高（96%）且误报率最低；GPT-4.1 在响应速度上领先。尽管如此，不同模型间仍存在关键性能差异，且语言歧义和领域表示局限会引发偶发失败。

Conclusion: 研究既凸显了 LLM 在充当自然语言目标与自动规划流程之间可靠桥梁方面取得的显著进展，也揭示了仍然存在的关键差距，表明该领域尚需进一步改进以应对语言歧义和领域限制带来的挑战。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between human intent and machine execution remains a challenge in automated planning, where expressing goals in formal languages like PDDL restricts accessibility to non-experts. This paper empirically evaluates whether current Large Language Models (LLMs) can reliably translate natural language testing goals, written in informal language by video game testers, into well-formed PDDL targets suitable for classical planning. We present a carefully designed prompt template, integrating insights from iterative experimentation, aimed at maximizing both accuracy and response coherence from multiple state-of-the-art LLMs. Six contemporary models are systematically assessed on correctness, speed, and error tendencies using real-world, domain-specific benchmarks. All models demonstrate high correctness, exceeding 92\%, with Gemini 2.5 Flash achieving the highest accuracy at 96\% and the lowest incidence of false positives, while GPT-4.1 leads in response speed. Despite these advances, critical distinctions exist in model performance, and occasional failures arise from language ambiguity and limitations in domain representation. Our analysis underscores both the significant progress and ongoing gaps in enabling LLMs to act as robust bridges between natural language objectives and automated planning pipelines.

</details>


### [251] [Clueing up LLMs with Tool-Augmented Deductive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18736)
*Rebecca Ansell,Autumn Toney-Wails*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实现了一个基于经典桌游《妙探寻凶》(Clue) 的多智能体文本环境，用于评测大语言模型的多步演绎推理能力，并引入"可能性矩阵"这一工具增强方法，将隐式游戏状态转化为显式的剩余可能性表示，以卸载记忆与约束维护任务，并与基线性能进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型近期进展显著，但在长时间交互中保持逻辑一致的演绎推理仍然困难。需要在多步推理间整合证据、与先前推断保持一致、并在新约束下更新信念的任务，能够暴露当前模型的局限，同时为评测推理增强手段提供有价值的测试平台。

Method: 作者实现了一个基于文本的多智能体版《妙探寻凶》环境，要求智能体从一系列观察中推断隐藏信息、在回合间保持一致、并在不断演化的逻辑约束集合上进行推理。实例化了六个基于 LLM 的智能体（GPT-4o-mini 与 Gemini-2.5-Flash），每个模型家族各三个智能体进行回合制游戏，并在多轮重复对局中建立基线性能。随后提出工具增强方案：利用结构化的可能性矩阵，将生成推理日志中的隐式游戏状态转换为剩余可能性的显式表示；该矩阵编码了跨回合记忆与演绎约束，从而将这些任务从智能体中卸载出去。

Result: 文章完成了基线性能的建立，并将工具增强的"可能性矩阵"方法与基线进行对比，评估工具增强在战略推理环境中对自主智能体推理质量与任务成功率的支持效果（具体对比数值在摘要中未展开）。

Conclusion: 工具增强（可能性矩阵）有望通过将记忆维护与演绎约束转化为显式结构，支撑自主智能体在战略推理任务中的推理质量与任务成功率，但摘要未给出最终数值结论。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), performing logically consistent deductive reasoning over extended interactions remains challenging. Tasks that require integrating evidence across multiple reasoning steps, maintaining consistency with prior inferences, and updating beliefs under new constraints can surface limitations in current models while providing a useful testbed for evaluating reasoning enhancements. In this paper, we implement a text-based, multi-agent version of the classic board game Clue as an environment to evaluate multi-step, agentic deductive reasoning. In this setting, agents must infer hidden information from a sequence of observations, maintain consistency across turns, and reason over an evolving set of logical constraints. We instantiate six LLM-based agents (GPT-4o-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash) as players that engage in turn-based gameplay; using three agents per model family, we establish baseline performance across repeated games. We then introduce a tool-augmented approach in which a structured possibility matrix converts implicit game state from generated reasoning logs into an explicit representation of remaining possibilities. The possibility matrix encodes extended-turn memory and deductive constraints, offloading these tasks from the agent. We compare this approach against the baseline to evaluate how tool augmentation supports reasoning quality and task success for autonomous agents in a strategic reasoning environment.

</details>


### [252] [Version- and Scope-Aware Question Answering over Normative Documents: A Deployed System and an End-to-End Evaluation at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18769)
*Liuyin Wang,Shuaipeng Jin,Jiwei Shi,Jensen Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于规范性文档的问答任务中"托管检索服务"这一默认做法：托管服务在约73,000份候选文件上得分88.1，而采用显式规则先解析版本与适用范围再生成答案的"受治理系统"得分97.7，差距9.6分。作者公开了问题集、答案文本、评分及复现脚本，并说明受治理系统已商用（2026年1月起，1126名注册用户，工作日约10万次调用）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规范性文档问答中，仅凭单段文本往往无法正确作答，因为答案还取决于文档是否为现行有效版本、是否适用于特定司法管辖区/主体（机构、申请人）/日期，以及每条规范性主张能否追溯到支撑原文。托管检索服务大幅降低了搭建初始系统的工程成本，使"上传文档即提问"成为常见默认方案，但该默认做法在版本与范围等治理问题上可能存在缺陷，需要量化评估。

Method: 作者在约73,000份候选规范性文档（来自生产部署）上，基于已发布基准对200个问题进行分层抽样评估；每个问题都有黄金源文档，且抽样规则在发布时未读取任何系统输出或分数。将托管检索服务与"受治理系统"（由显式规则在生成前解析文档版本与适用范围）进行对比，并使用未取整均值计算两者得分。

Result: 受治理系统总体得分97.7，托管服务得分88.1，二者相差9.6分（由未取整均值计算）。问题集、两套系统的评估答案文本、评分以及复现报告基准统计量的脚本均已公开。受治理配置自2026年1月起作为商业产品运行，拥有1126名注册用户（客户机构包括智谱AI和乐城健康），到2026年4月中旬工作日调用量约达10万次。

Conclusion: 仅依靠托管检索服务的"上传即提问"默认方案在规范性文档问答中表现不佳（88.1分），而通过显式规则在生成前解析版本与适用范围的受治理系统显著更优（97.7分）。这说明对此类语料，系统必须显式处理文档的时效性、适用范围与溯源等治理问题，而非依赖通用检索服务。

Abstract: Correctly answering a question grounded in normative documents often depends on information outside any single passage: whether the retrieved document is the version currently in force; whether it applies to the jurisdiction, subject (such as an institution or applicant), and date at issue; and whether each normative claim can be traced to its supporting source text. Hosted retrieval services have substantially lowered the engineering cost of building an initial system over such corpora, making "upload the documents and ask" a common default. We evaluate this default on approximately 73,000 candidate normative documents supplied to a production deployment. The evaluation uses a stratified sample of 200 questions from our published benchmark, with a gold source document for every question; the released sampling rule reads no system outputs or scores. We compare the hosted service with a governed system that resolves version and scope through explicit rules before generation. The governed system scored 97.7 overall, while the hosted service scored 88.1, a gap of 9.6 points computed from unrounded means. The question set, the answer text evaluated for both systems, the scores, and the scripts used to reproduce the reported benchmark statistics are public. The governed configuration has operated as a commercial product since January 2026 and serves 1,126 registered users; named customer organizations include Zhipu AI and Lecheng Health. By mid-April 2026, it had reached roughly 100,000 calls per workday.

</details>


### [253] [CERA-MoA: Co-Evolving Routing Mechanisms with Continually Learning LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18779)
*Jiaxuan Jiang,Liyuan He,Zhixuan Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CERA-MoA 提出一种路由与智能体协同演化的迭代强化学习框架，通过预测式熟悉度估计和累计阈值自适应路由，在提升任务性能的同时实现效率与能力分化的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）范式将查询路由与智能体微调割裂处理，导致路由策略无法随智能体能力的演进自适应更新，且智能体难以实现数据驱动的协同专业化。

Method: 提出 CERA-MoA，一个动态路由与独立智能体策略协同演化的迭代强化学习框架。设计预测式熟悉度估计器，利用中间层隐状态评估智能体间的语义胜任度，避免完整 rollout 开销；基于熟悉度分数采用累计阈值自适应路由机制，动态激活最小化的定制智能体子集；并按智能体演化能力主动分配针对性训练样本以促进能力分化。

Result: 跨多个领域的广泛实验表明，CERA-MoA 优于最先进的静态智能体路由和固定工作流微调基线。

Conclusion: 通过路由与智能体的协同演化，CERA-MoA 实现了任务性能、计算效率与能力分化之间的有效权衡，验证了协同演化范式在 Mixture-of-Agents 中的优越性。

Abstract: Current Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) paradigms generally treat query routing and agent fine-tuning as separate processes, limiting their ability to respond to evolving agent capabilities. This disconnect prevents routing strategies from adapting to evolving agent capabilities during post-training and prevents agents from achieving synergistic data-driven specialization. To resolve this, we introduce CERA-MoA (Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents for Mixture-of-Agents), an iterative reinforcement learning framework where the dynamic router and independent agent policies co-evolve. We design a predictive familiarity estimator that leverages mid-layer hidden states to evaluate semantic competence among agents, avoiding the overhead of full rollouts. Based on these familiarity scores, a cumulative-threshold adaptive routing mechanism dynamically activates a tailored minimal agent subset, achieving a trade-off between task performance and efficiency. By proactively allocating targeted training samples to agents based on their evolving competence, CERA-MoA promotes capability differentiation. Extensive experiments across various domains demonstrate that CERA-MoA outperforms state-of-the-art static-agent routing and fix-workflow fine-tuning baselines.

</details>


### [254] [Compositional Policy Violations: When Step-Level Compliance Fails In Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18820)
*Ashwini Kurady,Sri Sai Charith Grandhi,Rajesh Gupta,Sumit Mamoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示当前 Agentic 工作流的治理几乎全是“步骤级”监控，而组织的实际政策是“整个执行”属性，二者错位导致一种步骤级合规无法察觉的失败模式——组合式政策违反（CPV）。作者给出四类 CPV 分类法，并证明正确修复取决于被守护量发生突变的位置，进而提出一种基于溯源（provenance）的运行时架构，从原始溯源重算被守护量以评估完整执行轨迹上的政策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管场景中，智能体工作流正在做出重大决策，但围绕它们的治理几乎完全落在步骤层面（输入输出分类器、每轮护栏、跨度级评估器）。而组织真正持有的政策（如转介阈值、权限限制、审查要求）是整个执行的属性，而非某一步的属性。这种错位导致了一种失败模式：每一步都通过自身检查，但组合后的执行却违反了治理政策；且由于单步谓词无法评估该步本身不能确定的属性，步骤级监控无论多准确都无法检测此类问题。这正是驱动本文研究的根本缺口。

Method: 本文将这种失败定义为“组合式政策违反（CPV）”，即步骤级合规无法组合的失败。作者提出四类 CPV 分类法：Authority Creep（权限蔓延）、Threshold Laundering（阈值洗白）、Cumulative Sum Violation（累积和违规）、Context Collapse（上下文坍塌）。通过分析指出，每类的正确修复由“被守护量发生突变的位置”所决定。在此基础上，提出一种基于溯源感知的运行时架构，它在完整的执行轨迹上评估政策，并从原始溯源（而非管道的派生表示）重新计算被守护量。

Result: 文章确立 CPV 为步骤级合规无法组合的失败形式；给出四类 CPV 的分类法；论证了修复策略由被守护量的突变位置决定；并提出基于溯源的运行时架构，通过在完整执行轨迹上、从原始溯源重算被守护量来评估政策。

Conclusion: 步骤级监控在原理上无法检测 CPV 这类由执行组合产生的政策违反，仅靠提高单步监控准确率无济于事。正确的治理必须将政策评估从单步提升到完整执行轨迹，并依赖原始溯源而非派生表示来重算被守护量；基于溯源感知的运行时架构为这种治理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Agentic workflows now make consequential decisions in regulated settings, and the governance placed around them is almost entirely step-scoped: input-output classifiers, per turn rails, and span-level evaluators. The policies organizations actually hold, such as referral thresholds, authority limits, and review requirements, are properties of the whole execution rather than of any one step. This mismatch admits a failure mode we call a Compositional Policy Violation (CPV): every individual step passes its own check while the composed execution violates the governing policy. A predicate over a single step cannot evaluate a property that step does not determine, so no improvement in the accuracy of the step-scoped monitors detects this class. We define CPVs as the failure of step-level compliance to compose, and present a taxonomy of four types: Authority Creep, Threshold Laundering, Cumulative Sum Violation, and Context Collapse. We show that the correct repair for each class is dictated by where the guarded quantity mutates. We then introduce a provenance-aware runtime architecture that evaluates policies over complete execution traces, recomputing guarded quantities from raw provenance rather than the pipeline's derived representation.

</details>


### [255] [Infinite-Parameter LLMs: Generating and Adapting Weights from Live Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18842)
*Jinli Hu,Ross M. Clarke,Yichuan Zhang,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"无限参数LLM"（Infinite-Parameter LLM），利用紧凑超网络将运行时提供的数据实时转化为对共享基座网络的低秩调制，用在线更新的贝叶斯信念持续重新推导权重，使模型能从即时交互中"写入"知识，而非仅依赖上下文提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型权重在训练后冻结，运行时供应的知识与行为只能塞进提示（通过检索或指令），每次请求都需重读、请求结束后即丢弃；而缩放定律驱动的MoE虽激活庞大规模参数，却建立在静态预训练数据上。作者希望设计一种架构，能将实时交互数据直接写入权重，从而摆脱对上下文窗口的依赖并获得持久的跨轮次知识。

Method: 受MoE启发，提出"无限参数LLM"：用一个紧凑的超网络将运行时数据转换为对共享基座网络的低秩调制，使前馈权重由实时数据生成而非存储在固定参数库中。与先前"只读一次上下文后冻结"的权重生成器不同，该方法对生成器的隐编码维持一个贝叶斯信念，并在线更新，使有效权重随会话推进从演化的信念不断重新推导，而非读完一次后固定。存储占用保持不变，但模型可编译出的权重在效果上无限。

Result: 将运行时知识承载于权重而非提示，在计算上得以摊销、释放上下文窗口、跨轮次持续存在，并可能比上下文内使用具有更好的泛化能力。作者制定了专门评测协议，在与上下文内学习（in-context learning）和检索的对比中检验上述能力。

Conclusion: 通过在权重中携带运行时知识与行为（而非仅置于提示），无限参数LLM有望实现计算的摊销化、上下文窗口解放、跨轮次持久性和更强的泛化，并通过专门评测协议验证其相对上下文内学习与检索的优势。

Abstract: The scaling laws hold that a language model grows more capable with more parameters and more training data, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have ridden these laws to remarkable results, activating only a fraction of an enormous stored parameter bank for each token. That success is built on static pretraining data. A deployed model faces a different world, where much of the data that would make it more useful is not in its training set but in the live interaction it is currently handling, such as the facts a user supplies or the corrections they give. A conventional model cannot learn from this data, because its weights are frozen after training. Instead, the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time are placed in the prompt, by retrieval or instruction, and re-read on every request only to be discarded once the request ends. We ask how an architecture could learn from live interaction by writing it into its weights. Taking inspiration from MoE, we propose the \textbf{Infinite-Parameter LLM}. A compact hypernetwork turns the data given at run time into a low-rank modulation of a shared base network, so the feed-forward weights are generated from live data rather than stored in a fixed bank. Where prior weight generators read the context once and freeze, we carry a Bayesian belief over the generator's latent code and update it online, so the effective weight is re-derived from that evolving belief as the session proceeds rather than fixed after one read. The stored footprint stays fixed, yet the weights the model can compile are effectively infinite. For the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time, carrying them in the weights rather than the prompt is amortized in compute, frees the context window, persists across turns, and can generalise better than in-context use. We specify an evaluation protocol that tests exactly this against in-context learning and retrieval.

</details>


### [256] [Suppressed, Not Erased: A Representational Trace of Edited Facts Survives Even Weight-Free Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18985)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究知识编辑后的模型是否仍保留原始事实：作者用线性追踪探针发现，即使编辑在行为上完全成功，原始对象仍能以远高于随机水平从隐状态中被线性解码，说明编辑是"抑制"而非"擦除"原有关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑基准只验证局部正确性（编辑模型在近编辑提示上产生新事实），却未衡量原始事实在模型内部还保留多少。作者希望直接测量编辑后模型隐状态中残余的原始知识。

Method: 使用线性追踪探针（linear trace probe）直接探测：在某事实被编辑后，检验原始对象是否还能从模型隐状态中被线性解码。实验在 GPT-2-XL 上对 50 条 CounterFact 编辑进行，覆盖三种机制不同的编辑器：ROME、约束微调、以及基于外部记忆的 GRACE（后者不改变任何基础模型权重）。此外还尝试使用再学习节省（relearning-savings）仪器作为补充测量。

Result: 所有编辑均达到 100% 的生成式成功率，但原始对象仍可被线性解码且显著高于 0.50 的随机水平：ROME 探针准确率 0.96、约束微调 0.86、GRACE 0.79。其中 GRACE 最为关键——它完全不修改基础模型权重、仅通过外部记忆覆盖事实，然而原始对象仍可从底层网络解码，因此残余痕迹不能归因于权重更新不彻底。作者将此解读为：编辑即使行为上成功，也只是在表征空间中抑制而非擦除原始关联。再学习节省仪器在该设定下表现不可靠，作者将其视为方法学上的负面结果，而非支持性证据。

Conclusion: 知识编辑在表征层面是"抑制"而非"擦除"原始关联；行为层面的编辑成功并不等于原始知识的真正移除。作者据此质疑以生成成功率为核心的编辑基准的有效性，并警示编辑的可逆性或知识残留问题。

Abstract: Knowledge-editing benchmarks certify local correctness, whether an edited model produces the new fact on near-edit prompts but not how much of the original fact remains decodable inside the model. We study residual knowledge directly with a linear trace probe: after editing a fact, we ask whether the original object is still recoverable from the model's hidden states. On GPT-2-XL, across three mechanistically distinct editors applied to 50 CounterFact edits, the original object remains linearly decodable well above chance after a successful edit (probe accuracy 0.96 for ROME, 0.86 for constrained fine-tuning, and 0.79 for the memory-based editor GRACE, against a chance level of 0.50; all edits reach 100% generation-based success). The GRACE result is the most informative: GRACE changes zero base-model weights, overriding the fact through an external memory, yet the original object is still decodable from the underlying network, so the residual trace cannot be attributed to an incomplete weight update. We read this as evidence that editing, even when behaviorally successful, suppresses rather than erases the original association in representational space. We also report a relearning-savings instrument that did not behave reliably in our setting and discuss why; we treat it as a negative methodological result rather than evidence. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [257] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"任务加权图"（task-weighted charts）这一框架，将语言模型计算维度的度量问题转化为针对特定功能（泛函）的最小二乘拟合问题，并发现下一个词预测需要残差流约70–90%的宽度，而激活方差并不能预测这一需求；维度是"按功能"而定的，且这一分离可被实际利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "语言模型的计算实际使用了多少维度"这一问题若不指明具体功能（functional）是模糊且无法回答的。现有基于方差或线性压缩的方法往往假设高方差即高功能重要性，但作者发现这一假设可能不成立，因此需要一种能够针对特定功能、在功能自身度量下度量有效维度的方法。

Method: 提出"任务加权图"（task-weighted charts）框架：针对表示（representation）的某个选定功能，在其自身度量（metric）下，用低维坐标系对表示进行拟合，从而把"蒸馏"问题转化为普通的最小二乘回归。作者在三个系列共六个模型（参数量从70M到7B）上开展实验，比较各功能的维度需求、方差分布与各类线性压缩方法（基于方差、最优线性压缩）的表现。

Result: 1）在进行完整下一词预测时，需保留残差流70–90%的宽度才能将困惑度损失控制在5%以内，且这一宽度被语言的稀有尾部事件所消耗；2）方差分布无法预测功能重要性：GPT-2中两个方向承载了90%的激活方差，却几乎不承载其功能；3）维度是"按功能"的：模型自身不确定性只需6个坐标即可读出，而完整预测分布需要数百个，且维度需求随网络深度增长；4）当仅能保留少数维度时，在功能度量下训练的图比基于方差或最优线性压缩更好地保留了模型预测。

Conclusion: 语言模型的有效计算维度必须相对于具体功能来定义，任务加权图框架使该问题可解；激活方差与功能重要性之间存在显著分离，因此精简表示时应以目标功能为准则而非方差，这一洞察可实际用于改进低维压缩与蒸馏。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [258] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过五项诊断实验，将多模态大模型在物理与几何科学推理任务中的失败分解为感知、推理或二者兼有，发现错误的图表解读会拖累仅靠文本就能答对的题目，且InternS1-mini在所有任务上均垫底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学推理基准普遍将“感知”与“推理”当作同一可测量过程，掩盖了多模态大模型的真实瓶颈所在，因此需要一种能隔离失败来源的诊断方法。

Method: 作者设计了一套跨物理与几何基准的五任务诊断实验：从原始图像、人工撰写caption到纠正后的caption，通过对比同一模型在不同输入模态/质量下的表现，将失败归因于感知、推理或二者兼有，并进一步分析感知失败后所触发的具体推理错误类型。

Result: 错误的图表解读会降低模型在纯文本条件下本可正确解答题目的表现；准确率整体随输入从原始图像升级为人工caption而上升，且部分模型在纠正caption下恢复显著，从而区分“感知受阻型失败”与“真实推理瓶颈”。感知失败后出现的推理错误类型具有领域依赖性：物理题多演变为计算错误，几何题多演变为概念误用。讨论部分指出，InternS1-mini尽管经过大量科学预训练并具备“思考”能力，仍在所有任务上低于实验中最弱模型，且推理轨迹常在完工前截断。

Conclusion: 多模态科学推理的失败可被系统性地归因于感知或推理，感知失败会引入领域相关的后续推理错误；单纯堆叠科学预训练与思考能力（如InternS1-mini）并不能保证可靠的推理表现，凸显了对感知与推理分别诊断的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [259] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gauntlet 框架，让通用编程智能体在零人工策略/算法/架构设计的条件下，通过一次性自主开发会话为游戏编写独立控制器，冻结后在留出实例上评估，首次实现大语言模型智能体在不依赖逐回合模型调用与手工战术层的情况下赢下完整游戏（如 Freeciv 全面征服胜利），并击败《星际争霸 II》多种内置 AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 LLM 智能体即便配有感知、记忆、技能库、规划器或可执行策略脚手架，仍难以将游戏知识转化为有效游玩；编码智能体的快速进步引发出两个更尖锐的问题——前沿模型现在能否赢得游戏，以及能否在无人辅助下自行构建完整玩家。本文旨在检验这种"编译式智能体"能力。

Method: 提出 Gauntlet（开发-冻结-评估）框架：仅向通用编程智能体提供游戏描述、原始观察/动作接口和一个空的策略文件，不提供任何策略、算法或架构。智能体在单次自主会话中与实时游戏交互实验并工程化独立控制器；随后冻结结果，在留出实例上评分，游玩过程中零模型调用。评估覆盖从小型街机到完整商业规模游戏，包括未发表的程序化 roguelike 和完整版《星际争霸 II》及《文明》（Freeciv）。

Result: 在未发表的程序化 roguelike 上，留出集成功率横跨 0-86%，并暴露出明显的代际阈值：最新一代系统的每个观测会话都优于前代系统的最佳会话。在完整游戏规模上，一个编译的原始 API 控制器击败了《星际争霸 II》所有公平的内置 AI 及两个作弊变体；单会话生成的程序在留出种子上通过全面征服赢下完整的《文明》（Freeciv）对局。虽然对新手 AI 胜率不高，但这是首次：此前没有语言智能体系统能在此类游戏中独立赢得完整对局，且无需逐回合模型调用和手工战术层。

Conclusion: 前沿编程智能体开始能够追踪长时程策略；冻结后的程序可检查。作者将这种能力命名为"编译式智能体"（compiled agency）：将开发经验编译为持久的可执行智能体，其架构由模型本身构建。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [260] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MUSE 基准，用于评估大视觉语言模型在情境化教育应用中的艺术图像理解能力，涵盖十二项跨视觉感知、语义-情感解读、文化理解与组合推理的任务，并揭示现有模型在情感解读与组合推理方面的明显短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型在多模态理解上进展显著，但在教育场景（尤其是 AI 辅助语言学习）中评估不足；现有基准主要聚焦真实世界图像或领域专用教育推理，对艺术教育内容覆盖有限，无法满足需解读艺术图像并理解其语义、情感与文化内涵并基于视觉语境推理的需求。

Method: 构建 MUSE 基准：将图像标注与问题生成解耦，以支持多样化任务与可控难度并降低标注成本；设计十二项任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解和组合推理；精心策划多元艺术图像，以新加坡与东南亚多元文化语境为主并兼收西方艺术传统，涵盖多主题与难度层级；对开源与闭源模型进行评测。

Result: 开源与专有模型在不同能力维度上呈现显著差异，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面表现薄弱；分析识别出常见失败模式及构建可信教育多模态模型面临的关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE 有望成为推动情境化教育应用中多模态理解的标准化基准，帮助弥合现有评估空白并促进可信多模态教育模型的研发。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [261] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"旗帜游戏"这一玩具模型，用于研究AI智能体集体信念形成的机制，并通过社会电路归因和统计力学理论两种方法剖析集体信念坍缩与极化的成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体涌现的协调行为正带来严重的安全风险，其核心驱动力是信念在世界模型中的快速形成与传播。要实现集体对齐，需要对这些涌现行为进行机制层面的理解。

Method: 作者设计"旗帜游戏"：一面隐藏的国旗构成真值，每个有界智能体只能直接观察私有裁剪区域，但可与同伴交换信念并权衡社会证据。在此基础上，提出"社会电路归因"技术预测哪些智能体及观点对集体动态最重要，并通过因果干预验证；同时为更大群体规模发展出统计力学理论，与经验相图对照。

Result: 该模型再现了丰富现象：性能随群体规模呈非单调变化、社会意识提示与团队多样性带来的准确率提升、组织结构效应显著。群体规模较小时出现集体信念坍缩，规模增大后转为集体信念极化，后者导致大规模时性能下降但创造了信念多样性。因果干预的效力随群体增长而减弱，统计力学理论则成功匹配经验相图。

Conclusion: 本研究迈出了"机制化群体可解释性"科学的第一步，揭示了智能体个体属性与其通信如何共同催生涌现的集体行为。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [262] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了双过程智能体 SwiftSage，引入自适应记忆模块（AMM）与自我反思模块（SRM）两个认知扩展，并在 ScienceWorld 上进行受控消融实验。结果表明完整系统取得最佳平均得分（64.62）、成功率（43.17%）与成功步效率（19.33 步），其中 SRM 是最强的单一贡献者，说明执行时控制是主要瓶颈，而情景记忆在运行时循环稳定后作用最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在交互环境中依然脆弱，成功依赖长时程状态追踪、有效动作执行与失败后的恢复。现有方法难以同时解决这些挑战，因此需要在不改变执行底层的条件下，通过模块化认知扩展来增强智能体的鲁棒性。

Method: 以 SwiftSage（快动作提议器 + 慢规划器的双过程智能体）为基础，加入两个特征开关（feature-flagged）式模块：自适应记忆模块（AMM）负责显著性门控的情景存储与触发式检索；自我反思模块（SRM）负责有界的执行时验证与纠正干预。两者基于同一执行底层实现，从而支持在 ScienceWorld 上进行受控消融，比较基线、基线+AMM、基线+SRM 与完整系统四种配置。

Result: 在四种配置中，完整系统取得最佳平均最终得分 64.62、成功率 43.17%、成功步效率 19.33 步；SRM 是最强的单一贡献模块。结果表明执行时控制是该场景的主导瓶颈，情景记忆则在运行时循环被稳定后发挥最大价值。

Conclusion: 执行时控制（SRM）是提升交互式语言智能体性能的关键瓶颈，应先稳定运行时执行循环；情景记忆（AMM）在运行时循环稳定后能进一步改善表现，两个模块协同使完整系统达到最优效果。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Bayesian Quantile Deep Echo State Networks for Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17579)
*Antonio De Leon,Raquel Prado,Bruno Sansó*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出分位数深度回声状态网络（Q-DESN），一种基于深度回声状态网络固定特征的贝叶斯分位数回归模型，用于高维非线性时间序列的条件分位数估计，并在合成数据、洪水流量预测和 PriceFM 对比中验证其改进分位数评分的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件分位数在非对称决策损失、尾部风险与区间预测中至关重要，但当时间特征向量高维时，非线性时间序列的贝叶斯分位数回归变得困难，因此需要一种在固定特征下高效且可量化不确定性的建模方法。

Method: 开发 Q-DESN 模型：在给定的深度回声状态网络（DESN）储层构造和特征映射条件下，对回归系数、似然和收缩参数赋予后验不确定性。单层拟合采用非对称拉普拉斯（AL）或分位数固定的广义非对称拉普拉斯（GAL）工作似然，配合岭先验或正则化马蹄（regularized-horseshoe）先验。后验计算在计算可行时使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC），对于需要反复拟合的分析则采用模型特定的变分贝叶斯（VB）近似。对于分位数网格，比较了逐层独立回归后进行单调重排的方法，与对相邻分位数系数差异施加收缩的联合分位数向量回归方法。

Result: 合成研究、单源回顾性的全球洪水预警系统（GloFAS）径流案例以及回顾性 PriceFM 对比，识别出该固定特征贝叶斯回归能够改进有限网格分位数评分的具体场景。

Conclusion: Q-DESN 通过结合深度回声状态网络的固定特征与灵活的贝叶斯分位数回归框架，为高维非线性时间序列的条件分位数建模提供了有效方案，并在多个实证场景中展示了分位数评分上的改进。

Abstract: Conditional quantiles are central to asymmetric decision losses, tail-risk assessment, and interval forecasts, but Bayesian quantile regression for nonlinear time series can be difficult when the temporal feature vector is high-dimensional. We develop the quantile deep echo state network (Q-DESN), a Bayesian quantile regression model conditional on fixed features generated by a deep echo state network. Conditional on a specified reservoir construction and feature map, posterior uncertainty is assigned to the regression coefficients, likelihood, and shrinkage parameters. Single-level fits use asymmetric Laplace or quantile-fixed generalized asymmetric Laplace working likelihoods with ridge or regularized-horseshoe priors. Posterior computation uses Markov chain Monte Carlo when computationally practical and a model-specific variational Bayes approximation for analyses requiring repeated fitting. For quantile grids, we compare independent level-wise regressions followed by monotone rearrangement with a joint quantile-vector regression that shrinks adjacent quantile-specific coefficient differences. Synthetic studies, a single-origin retrospective Global Flood Awareness System (GloFAS) streamflow case, and a retrospective PriceFM comparison identify settings where this fixed-feature Bayesian regression improves finite-grid quantile scores.

</details>


### [264] [Multimodal Three-Class Alzheimer's Disease Classification: The MCI Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17649)
*Lorenzo Tanzi,Chunfeng Lian,Lara Cavinato*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个防数据泄漏的多模态融合框架，用于阿尔茨海默病三分类（CN/MCI/AD），融合Tau PET、结构MRI、ROI与血浆表格数据及分层PET-血浆模型四路信息。在881名ADNI受试者上以同步的样本外预测进行无参凸组合，融合显著优于各单支模型，但MCI仍是核心瓶颈，反映出横断面信息的局限，而非融合机制的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病临床上按CN、MCI、AD三阶段分级，但中间类别MCI在生物学上高度异质，与两端重叠，是分类错误的主要来源。作者旨在通过多模态融合提升分类稳健性，并以诚实基线的方式揭示：瓶颈在于横断面信息本身的限制，而非融合算法。

Method: 基于ADNI队列881名对齐受试者，采用自定义预处理与特征提取流程构建四个分支：3D Tau PET模型、3D结构MRI模型、表格化ROI-血浆模型、以及分层PET-血浆模型。各分支在受试者层面对齐，并在完全一致的、无泄漏交叉验证折上评估；多模态融合完全基于同步的样本外(out-of-fold)预测构建，最终模型为固定、无参数的凸组合。嵌套superlearner作为稳健性分析，logistic元学习器提供MCI导向的补充工作点。

Result: 融合模型较每个单独分支均有显著提升，且在MCI类别上的增益最大；嵌套superlearner仅复现而未超越固定权重。logistic元学习器在同一准确率-灵敏度前沿上提供了面向MCI的互补工作点。结果表明多模态融合提升了稳健性，但MCI仍是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 多模态融合提升了分类稳健性，但MCI类别的困难反映的是横断面信息的局限，而非融合机制的缺陷。作者据此主张将任务重构为连续的进展（progression）问题，而非离散三分类。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is clinically staged as a three-class progression: cognitively normal (CN), mild cognitive impairment (MCI) and Alzheimer's disease (AD). The intermediate MCI class is biologically heterogeneous and overlaps with both extremes and it is the dominant source of classification error. This work develops a leakage-controlled framework for subject-level CN/MCI/AD classification that fuses four sources of information: a 3D Tau PET model, a 3D structural MRI model, a tabular ROI-and-plasma model and a hierarchical PET--plasma model. All branches are derived from a custom preprocessing and feature-extraction pipeline applied to $881$ aligned subjects from the ADNI cohort. They are aligned at the subject level and evaluated under identical, no-leak cross-validation folds, so that the multimodal fusion, built entirely on synchronized out-of-fold predictions, avoids optimistic bias. The final model is a fixed, parameter-free convex combination of the four branches. A nested superlearner serves as a robustness analysis and reproduces, rather than improves on, the fixed weights. The fusion improves significantly over every individual branch, with the largest gain on the MCI class, while a logistic meta-learner provides a complementary, MCI-oriented operating point on the same accuracy-sensitivity frontier. The work is presented as an honest baseline. It shows that multimodal fusion improves robustness, but that MCI remains the central bottleneck. This reflects a limit of the cross-sectional information rather than of the fusion mechanism and motivates reformulating the task as a continuous progression problem.

</details>


### [265] [Subgroup Inconsistency and the Dilution Effect in Levene's Test for Homoscedasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17746)
*Vladimir Gurvich,Mariya Naumova*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文证明 Levene 方差齐性检验会继承 ANOVA 的“悖论”：在给定显著性水平下，可能无法拒绝全局原假设（三组方差相等），却同时以相同或更高置信度拒绝局部原假设（两组方差相等），并通过理论与数值示例说明第三个行为良好的组会稀释检验统计量、掩盖局部显著方差差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levene 检验基于对绝对值或平方偏差做 ANOVA，因而结构上镜像了 ANOVA 的统计性质。此前已发现 ANOVA 存在逻辑矛盾（拒绝局部均值相等却无法拒绝全局均值相等），本文旨在揭示 Levene 检验在组方差上直接继承这一同样的悖论。

Method: 利用 Levene 检验与 ANOVA 的结构同构性，通过理论推理将该悖论从均值情形迁移到方差情形（即用组方差 σ²_j 替换组均值 μ_j），并构造一个加入“行为良好”第三组的数值示例，展示检验统计量被稀释、局部显著方差差异被掩盖的现象。

Result: 证明 Levene 检验在给定显著性水平 α 下会出现：无法拒绝全局原假设 H₀: σ²₁=σ²₂=σ²₃，却以相同或更高置信度拒绝局部假设 H₀′: σ²₁=σ²₂ 这一不一致性；数值示例验证了第三组的加入会稀释检验统计量并掩盖显著的局部方差差异。

Conclusion: Levene 检验直接继承了 ANOVA 的逻辑悖论，提示研究者在解读方差齐性检验结果时需警惕全局与局部结论可能出现的矛盾，并关注组数增加对检验统计量的稀释效应。

Abstract: Levene's test for homoscedasticity is a standard procedure used to evaluate whether multiple groups of independent observations share a common variance. Because Levene's test relies on an Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) applied to transformed absolute or squared deviations, it structurally mirrors the statistical properties of ANOVA itself. Let $μ_j$ and $σ^2_j$ denote the expectation and variance of the observations in group $j$. It was previously established that for a given significance level $α$, ANOVA can result in a logical contradiction: failing to reject the global null hypothesis $H_0: μ_1 = μ_2 = μ_3$ while simultaneously rejecting the localized hypothesis $H_0': μ_1 = μ_2$ with the same or higher confidence. In this paper, we show that Levene's test directly inherits this same ``paradox'' regarding group variances $σ^2_j$. We provide theoretical reasoning and a numerical illustration of this inconsistency, demonstrating how the addition of a well-behaved third group can dilute the test statistic and mask a significant localized variance discrepancy.

</details>


### [266] [When AI Generates Covariates: Causal Typing and Estimand Drift in Sequential Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17772)
*Takes Fujita,Nobutaka Hattori*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个因果类型学框架，用于规范在序贯实验中由AI生成的协变量（来自笔记、对话、图像、可穿戴流）所扮演的角色，防止因角色未明确而改变因果问题，并通过"估计量锁"在生成特征进入分析前固定标准化的近端效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成特征在分析中的因果角色（处理版本、前置状态、历史、设计变量、中介、结局代理、观测过程、并发事件等）若未明确指定，会悄然改变所要回答的因果问题，导致偏倚或覆盖率不足。

Method: 构建因果类型学科：版本化表示映射、因果角色分类器、声明状态过滤器与估计量锁；估计量锁在生成协变量进入分析前固定标准化近端效应。运用条件协方差的压缩偏倚刻画来分析生成表示对设计相关状态的替换，并提出标准化分解以分离压缩偏倚、条件律偏倚和标准化漂移；同时覆盖中介调整、动作后泄漏、标记-干预混淆、结果引导发现、状态测量误差等情形，并用簇水平正交估计量区分经验目标与超总体目标。

Result: 模拟表明，当精化保留设计相关信息时有助于推断；而设计擦除、泄漏和同数据标记选择会导致偏倚或覆盖率不足。

Conclusion: 在使用生成表示进行确认性推断之前，必须先确定因果语义与声明状态；框架为序贯实验中引入生成协变量时保持估计量不变提供了系统保障。

Abstract: AI-generated covariates from notes, conversations, images, and wearable streams can change the causal question when their roles are left unspecified. A generated feature may represent a treatment version, pre-action state, history, design variable, mediator, outcome proxy, observation process, or intercurrent event; these roles are not interchangeable.
  We formulate a causal type discipline for sequential experiments: a versioned representation map, a causal role classifier, a claim-status filter, and an estimand lock. The lock fixes a standardized proximal effect before generated covariates enter the analysis. Under audit correctness and standard identification assumptions, admissible role assignments preserve this estimand. We apply the established conditional-covariance characterization of compression bias to substitution of generated representations for design-relevant states. A standardized decomposition separates compression, conditional-law, and standardization drift. Further results cover mediator adjustment, post-action leakage, marker-intervention conflation, outcome-guided discovery, and state-measurement error. Cluster-level orthogonal estimators distinguish empirical and superpopulation targets under repeated sessions and missing outcomes.
  Simulations show that refinement helps when it retains design-relevant information, whereas design erasure, leakage, and same-data marker selection can produce bias or undercoverage. The framework places causal semantics and claim status before confirmatory inference with generated representations.

</details>


### [267] [Information Set Emulation: Causal Certificates for AI Derived EHR Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17777)
*Takes Fujita,Nobutaka Hattori*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息集模拟"(information set emulation)框架，通过AI类型化提升(typed lift)将来源证据、时间戳、可用性、表示版本、因果角色等元数据附加到从EHR提取的特征上，并借助因果证书(causal certificates)记录可审计证据；锁定目标试验下，将锁定的标量估计量映射到观测纤维的象，其平方切比雪夫半径等于残差极小极大均方误差，从而给出目标特异的信息模糊度度量，并配套给出识别、交叉拟合AIPW估计及审计诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI和大语言模型能从电子健康记录(EHR)中恢复临床上有意义的特征，但预测有用性并不足以确立其因果推断的可采性(admissibility)。因此需要一种机制，明确AI提取特征在因果分析中的角色、证据来源与模糊性，从而区分哪些信息能支持点估计声称、哪些只能做兼容性报告。

Method: 1) 信息集模拟：在锁定目标试验下，AI类型化提升为提取特征附加来源证据、临床与记录时间、决策时可用性、表示版本、提议的因果角色及未解决歧义；2) 因果证书：记录对这些因果角色的可审计证据；3) 观测纤维：类型化证据定义一个与观测定律一致的因果世界集合；4) 估计框架：在显式可交换性、正性与讨厌参数一致性条件下，给出识别与交叉拟合增广逆概率加权(AIPW)估计，区分经验目标与总体目标；5) EHR压缩-漂移恒等式分离框架存在性、处理分配与结局观测的作用；6) 人工模拟、普通法有限世界示例及合成Phase 0笔记验证估计失效与信息半径缩减。

Result: 1) 锁定的标量估计量将观测纤维映射到兼容的象，当象非空且紧时，其平方切比雪夫半径等于残差极小极大均方误差，提供目标特异的信息模糊度量；2) 给出识别和交叉拟合AIPW估计的理论结果；3) EHR压缩-漂移恒等式分离了框架存在、处理分配与结局观测的贡献；4) 人工仿真与普通法有限世界示例展示了估计失效与信息半径缩减；5) 合成Phase 0笔记演示了审计诊断；6) 单独的角色特异分析展布说明路由机制，但并非精确的纤维半径；所有实验均为合成数据。

Conclusion: 该框架精确规定了何时AI重建的信息可以支持点估计声称，何时必须采用兼容性报告；通过将信息模糊度量化为切比雪夫半径，并与EHR观测映射及审计证书架构集成，为基于EHR/LLM特征的因果推断提供了可审计、可路由的规范性基础。

Abstract: AI and large language models can recover clinically meaningful features from electronic health records (EHRs), but predictive usefulness does not establish admissibility for causal inference. We introduce information set emulation: an AI typed lift attaches source evidence, clinical and recording times, decision-time availability, representation version, proposed causal roles, and unresolved ambiguity to extracted features under a locked target trial. Causal certificates record auditable evidence for those roles. Features with unresolved downstream roles are routed to compatible reporting or separate analyses.
  Typed evidence defines an observational fiber of causal worlds consistent with the observed law. The locked scalar estimand maps this fiber to a compatible image whose squared Chebyshev radius equals the residual minimax mean squared error when the image is nonempty and compact. This classical identity provides a target-specific measure of information ambiguity. The contribution is its integration with a joint EHR observation map and an auditable certificate architecture.
  Under explicit exchangeability, positivity, and nuisance-consistency conditions, we give identification and cross-fitted augmented inverse probability weighted estimation, distinguishing empirical and population targets. An EHR compression-drift identity separates the roles of frame presence, treatment assignment, and outcome observation. Artificial simulations and a common-law finite-world example illustrate estimation failures and information-radius reduction. Synthetic Phase 0 notes demonstrate audit diagnostics; a separate role-specific analysis spread illustrates routing and is not an exact fiber radius. All experiments are synthetic. The framework specifies when reconstructed information can support a point claim and when compatible reporting is required.

</details>


### [268] [Efficient estimation and the cost of complete-case coarsening under monotone sequential MAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17778)
*Keivan Bolouri*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在单调序贯缺失随机假设下，对两个有序且部分观测的混杂因素做“完整案例粗化”（丢弃部分完整记录中已观测的混杂值）对平均处理效应估计的影响，推导出因果影响函数的粗化随机变换与典型梯度，给出交叉拟合估计量的精确漂移恒等式，并刻画粗化带来的效率损失及识别偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际数据分析中常因缺失模式不统一而将部分完整记录“粗化”为完整案例，丢弃了已观测的混杂因素取值。作者希望严格研究这种粗化的统计后果：它在何种条件下仅损失效率、在何种条件下会造成识别偏倚，以及效率损失的大小如何刻画。

Method: 作者在单调序贯缺失随机（MAR）框架下考虑两个有序、部分观测的混杂因素；将标准的“粗化随机”（coarsening-at-random）变换专门化到因果影响函数，建立典型梯度，并对交叉拟合估计量给出精确漂移恒等式。在“序贯MAR与完整案例MAR同时成立”的子模型中，把粗化造成的效率损失表示为一个涉及两次迭代投影的非负期望，并给出严格增益的条件。最后通过oracle模拟、确定性求积以及带估计扰动的模拟（有界倾向得分设计与高斯压力设计）进行验证。

Result: 粗化的效率损失可表示为非负期望（含两次迭代投影）；当中间混杂因素在第二阶段缺失处仍提供残差信息时，增益严格为正。若第二阶段响应依赖于中间混杂因素，则比较从效率问题变为识别问题，粗化会引入持续偏倚。有限样本模拟中，高斯压力设计出现较大区间欠覆盖，并可能反转有限样本精度排序，从而限制效率界的实际解释。

Conclusion: 完整案例粗化在序贯MAR与完整案例MAR同时成立时仅导致可量化的效率损失；一旦第二阶段响应依赖于中间混杂因素，粗化会造成识别偏倚并持续存在。效率界的实际意义受有限样本表现（尤其是高斯压力设计下的区间欠覆盖与精度排序反转）所限制。

Abstract: Complete-case coarsening discards observed confounder values from partially complete records. We study its consequences for average treatment effect estimation with two ordered, partially observed confounders under monotone sequential missing at random. We specialize the standard coarsening-at-random transformation to the causal influence function, establish the canonical gradient, and give an exact drift identity for a cross-fitted estimator with sequential multiple robustness. In the submodel where both sequential and complete-case missing-at-random assumptions hold, we express the efficiency loss from coarsening as a nonnegative expectation involving two iterated projections. The gain is strict when the intermediate confounder supplies residual information where second-stage missingness occurs. Oracle simulations and deterministic quadrature illustrate this efficiency comparison. When second-stage response depends on the intermediate confounder, the comparison instead concerns identification: coarsening can introduce persistent bias. Estimated-nuisance simulations include a bounded-propensity design and a Gaussian stress design. The latter exhibits substantial interval undercoverage and can reverse the finite-sample precision ordering, qualifying the practical interpretation of the efficiency bound.

</details>


### [269] [Online Bayesian Model Averaging with Joint Uncertainty Quantification for Models and Regression Coefficients in Binary Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17783)
*Joyee Ghosh,Aixin Tan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可再生估计（renewable estimation）的在线贝叶斯模型平均（online BMA）方法，用于流式二元响应数据（如虚拟学习平台中学生的顺序作答）。该方法在每次更新时仅利用保留的摘要统计量和新增观测，即可近似模型与系数的联合后验分布，无需回访历史数据，从而在保持与离线BMA相近精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式二元响应数据在虚拟学习平台等场景中常见，逻辑回归是处理这类数据的可解释框架，但数据可能支持多个候选预测变量子集，存在模型不确定性。贝叶斯模型平均（BMA）能通过跨模型平均来刻画这种不确定性，但对不断累积的数据反复运行BMA计算负担沉重，因此需要一种无需回访历史数据的在线实施方案。

Method: 基于可再生估计（renewable estimation）的在线贝叶斯模型平均方法。在每次更新时，利用保留的摘要统计量与新增观测来近似模型和各模型特定系数的联合后验分布，从而在不重新访问历史数据的情况下进行点估计与区间估计，并纳入模型不确定性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在线BMA在系数估计、模型与变量重要性、预测以及预测概率的区间估计方面都紧密逼近离线BMA，同时显著降低计算成本。在虚拟学习应用中，发现不同候选预测变量子集之间存在显著不确定性，且各题目的可信区间宽度差异较大；所提方法能够区分估计精确的题目与具有较大不确定性的题目，为自适应题目分配提供超越点估计的信息。

Conclusion: 在线BMA能够在流式数据场景下以较低计算成本有效纳入模型不确定性，其推断结果可与离线方法相媲美，并能提供反映不确定性的区间信息，为虚拟学习平台中的自适应题目分配等决策提供更丰富的依据。

Abstract: Streaming binary response data arise in many applications, including virtual learning platforms where student responses are collected sequentially and estimated probabilities of correct responses may inform future question assignment. Logistic regression provides an interpretable framework for such analysis, but the data may support multiple plausible predictor subsets. Bayesian model averaging (BMA) accounts for such model uncertainty by averaging inference across competing models. Since repeatedly applying BMA to accumulating data can be computationally burdensome, we develop an online implementation based on renewable estimation. At each update, retained summaries and new observations are used to approximate the joint posterior of models and model-specific coefficients without revisiting historical data, enabling point and interval estimates that incorporate model uncertainty. Simulations show that online BMA closely approximates its offline counterpart in coefficient estimation, model and variable importance, prediction, and interval estimation of predictive probabilities, while substantially reducing computational cost. In a virtual learning application, we find substantial uncertainty across competing predictor subsets and large variability in credible interval widths across questions. Our method distinguishes questions with precise estimates from those with substantial uncertainty, providing information beyond point estimates for adaptive question assignment.

</details>


### [270] [Robust Tests for Step-Stress Models under Exponential Lifetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17827)
*María Jaenada Malagón,Juan Manuel Millán Fanconi,Leandro Pardo Llorente*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对阶进应力加速寿命试验（SSALT）的小样本与I型截尾场景，提出基于最小密度幂散度估计量（MDPDE）的稳健检验统计量，用于检验模型参数的线性假设，并在数据污染下保持名义显著性水平和检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高可靠长寿命产品的可靠性分析在正常工况下难以在合理时间和成本内获得足够失效数据；SSALT虽能加速退化，但其小样本和I型截尾使得经典极大似然推断对数据污染极为敏感。文献中已有稳健点估计（如MDPDE），但稳健的假设检验统计量尚未在此背景下被建立。

Method: 本文在指数寿命分布假设下，直接利用实验结束前记录的精确失效时间，并对到达截止时间后幸存的组件进行截尾，由此构造出混合的离散-连续分布；引入受限MDPDE（restricted MDPDE），建立其渐近性质，并构造Z型与Rao型检验统计量，用于检验模型参数的线性假设，从而实现SSALT场景下的稳健假设检验。

Result: 所提出的Z型和Rao型检验在数据污染下能够保持名义显著性水平和统计功效，而基于经典MLE的方法则失效。大量仿真研究证实了所提方法的稳健性增益，真实数据应用也验证了其实用价值。
