Conclusion: 基于MDPDE的稳健检验统计量有效解决了SSALT中I型截尾与数据污染导致的推断脆弱性问题，为高可靠产品加速寿命试验提供了稳健、可靠的假设检验工具。

Abstract: Highly reliable products with extended lifetimes present a challenge in reliability analysis: obtaining enough failure data under normal operating conditions is often incompatible with reasonable time and cost constraints. Step-Stress Accelerated Life Tests (SSALTs) offer a practical solution by progressively increasing stress levels to accelerate product degradation, allowing results to be extrapolated to normal conditions. However, the small sample sizes and Type-I censoring inherent to these experiments render classical maximum likelihood-based inference vulnerable to data contamination. Robust point estimation has been studied in the literature. However, robust test statistics have not yet been developed in this context. In this paper, we propose robust test statistics for SSALTs under exponential lifetime distributions, based on the minimum density power divergence estimator (MDPDE). This paper directly works with the exact failure times recorded before the end of the experiment. Once this limit time is reached, all surviving components are censored. This creates a mixed discrete-continuous distribution, which is a major analytical breakthrough in the context of SSALT. We introduce the restricted version of the MDPDE, establish its asymptotic properties, and construct Z-type and Rao-type test statistics for linear hypotheses on the model parameters. The proposed tests are shown to maintain their nominal significance levels and statistical power under data contamination, where classical MLE-based procedures fail. An extensive simulation study confirms the robustness gains of the proposed methods, and a real data application illustrates their practical value.

</details>


### [271] [Spectral Dynamics of DeepWalk Embeddings for Dynamic Network Change-Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17893)
*Houlin Zhou,Yejin Wang,Xufei Tang,Dan Zhuang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于DeepWalk的动态网络结构变化检测与定位框架：对每个快照学习节点嵌入，经正交Procrustes对齐后均值池化为图级向量，再用多元CUSUM扫描统计量检测嵌入均值变化，并给出理论与仿真及国际贸易网络实证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态网络中的突发结构变化可能对应异常事件或重要转折，但检测这类变化需要区分真实的结构信号与网络观测/学习表示中的随机波动，故需一种具备理论保证的检测与定位方法。

Method: 在每个快照上用DeepWalk学习低维节点嵌入；通过正交Procrustes变换将对齐到固定参考嵌入；均值池化得到可比较的图级向量；构造多元累积和（CUSUM）扫描统计量检测嵌入均值的结构变化，并据此实现变化点定位。

Result: 在给定正则条件下，推导出原假设下随机波动的上界，以及备择假设下可靠检测与一致定位的充分条件；将检测性能与网络稀疏性、谱分离度、嵌入维度和变化幅度相联系；仿真显示方法在多种动态网络设定下有效，国际贸易网络应用能识别贸易配置的转移。

Conclusion: 该方法通过嵌入对齐与多元CUSUM有效区分结构信号与表示波动，具备理论保证，仿真和实证均表明其在检测与定位动态网络结构变化方面具有实用价值。

Abstract: Dynamic networks describe evolving relational systems in which abrupt structural changes may signal anomalous events or important transitions. Detecting such changes requires distinguishing genuine structural signals from fluctuations in network observations and learned representations. We propose a DeepWalk-based framework for detecting and localizing structural changes in dynamic networks. For each snapshot, we learn low-dimensional node embeddings, align them to a fixed reference using orthogonal Procrustes transformations, and aggregate them by mean pooling into comparable graph-level vectors. We then construct a multivariate cumulative sum (CUSUM) scan statistic to identify changes in the embedding mean. Under stated regularity conditions, we derive bounds on stochastic fluctuations under the null hypothesis and sufficient conditions for reliable detection and consistent localization under the alternative. The analysis connects detection performance with network sparsity, spectral separation, embedding dimension, and change magnitude, clarifying the balance between structural signals and representation variability. Simulation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in detecting and localizing structural changes across a range of dynamic network settings. An application to an international trade network further illustrates its practical usefulness in identifying shifts in trade allocation.

</details>


### [272] [Cross Validation for the log Gaussian Cox Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17908)
*Hans Montcho,Håvard Rue,Finn Lindgren,David Bolin,Elias T Krainski,Daniela Castro-Camilo,Sara Martino*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为对数高斯Cox过程（LGCP）提出了一套实用的贝叶斯交叉验证框架，通过"留区域"（leave-region-out）策略、对数评分规则、新的似然近似以及贝叶斯遗忘（unlearning）与拉普拉斯近似，无需反复重新拟合模型即可高效获得留区域预测分布，并在模拟与真实数据上进行了验证与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGCP作为空间点模式分析的主流模型，其贝叶斯拟合方法与软件已较成熟，但模型批评、预测评估与模型比较的实用工具仍相对不足。对点过程定义预测验证面临概念与计算上的挑战，包括留出数据的选择、预测任务、评分规则与计算策略的确定，这促使本文提出系统化的贝叶斯模型评估框架。

Method: 作者提出"留区域交叉验证"（leave-region-out cross-validation）框架，以有界空间区域而非单个事件作为预测的基本单元；采用对数评分规则评估联合概率预测；计算上结合新的似然近似、贝叶斯遗忘策略与拉普拉斯近似，避免反复重新拟合模型即可获取留区域预测分布。该方法在欧氏域与网络上的空间点模式数据中实现，并集成于R-INLA、inlabru和MetricGraph软件包。

Result: 所提出的近似方法通过与基于暴力重拟合得到的蒙特卡洛估计进行对比得到验证，并在模拟数据及欧氏域与网络上的真实空间点模式数据集上展示了方法的效果。

Conclusion: 该工作弥补了LGCP模型批评与预测评估工具的不足，提供了一套理论统一、计算高效且易于通过R-INLA、inlabru和MetricGraph实现的贝叶斯交叉验证框架，推动LGCP从拟合走向更完善的预测性模型评估。

Abstract: The log Gaussian Cox Process (LGCP) is one of the most widely used models for the analysis of spatial point patterns. Although Bayesian methods and software for fitting LGCPs are now well established, practical tools for model criticism, predictive assessment, and model comparison remain comparatively underdeveloped. This paper develops a practical Bayesian cross-validation framework for LGCPs while placing it within a broader framework for Bayesian model assessment. Our approach is motivated by the conceptual and computational challenges that arise when defining predictive validation for point processes, including the choice of holdout data, prediction task, scoring rule, and computational strategy. We propose a leave-region-out cross-validation framework in which the fundamental unit of prediction is a bounded spatial region rather than an individual event. Predictive performance is assessed through the logarithmic scoring rule for joint probabilistic forecasts, while computational efficiency is achieved by combining a new likelihood approximation with a Bayesian unlearning strategy and Laplace approximations to obtain leave-region-out predictive distributions without repeated model refitting. We validate the proposed approximations against Monte Carlo estimates obtained via brute-force refitting, demonstrate the methodology on simulated and real spatial point pattern datasets defined on Euclidean domains and networks, and provide implementations in the R-INLA, inlabru, and MetricGraph packages.

</details>


### [273] [Coherent Hierarchical Forecasting for Proportion and Discrete Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17918)
*Hannah Comiskey*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种事后（post-hoc）分层/分组时间序列预测协调方法，通过卷积与指数倾斜（exponential tilting）构建离散与有界时间序列的一致（coherent）分布预测，并在流行病学与人口学数据上验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层与分组时间序列需满足聚合约束，促使学界发展保证一致预测的协调方法；但许多实际应用涉及离散或有界支撑，基于高斯假设的传统协调方法无法处理这类分布特性，因而需要一种能保留离散/有界支撑与分布性质的协调方法。

Method: 提出事后分层预测方法：在基础（不协调）分布预测之上，利用卷积与指数倾斜构造分层结构中各层级的一致预测分布，以保留各序列的分布性质与底层支撑。

Result: 通过模拟研究与流行病学、人口学数据的实证应用，表明该方法在离散与连续设定下均优于现有最先进协调方法，能提供可靠且一致的分层分布预测。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了离散与有界时间序列的分层预测协调问题，在保持分布性质与支撑的同时实现全层级一致，并在多种挑战性场景下表现出稳健性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical and grouped time series arise when a multivariate time series is forced to satisfy a set of aggregation constraints, motivating forecast reconciliation methods that ensure coherent forecasts of such hierarchical structures. Many real-world applications involve discrete or bounded supports, introducing additional challenges that are not addressed Gaussian-based reconciliation methods. We develop a post-hoc hierarchical forecasting approach to construct coherent forecast hierarchies for discrete and bounded time series. The method constructs coherent forecasts by convolution and exponential tilting, preserving the distributional properties and the underlying support throughout the hierarchy. We evaluate the proposed approach against state-of-the-art reconciliation methods for both discrete and continuous settings, demonstrating strong performance across a range of experiments. Through simulation studies and empirical applications in epidemiological and demographic data, we show that the method provides reliable and coherent distributional forecasts in challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [274] [Taylor Diagram and Wasserstein Distance for Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17950)
*Dongwei Chen,Emily J. King,Hungjui Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型模型评估工具——Wasserstein-Taylor图，它将最优传输中的Wasserstein距离融入泰勒图框架，利用余弦定律在替换统计量（中心化2-Wasserstein距离与分位数相关系数）后依然成立的事实，实现对不同大小数据集乃至仅有观测数据的统计模型的可视化比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统泰勒图依赖中心化均方根误差、标准差与Pearson相关系数三个统计量，受限于数据量须相同且需成对匹配的约束；作者希望借助Wasserstein距离的分布视角，突破这些限制，实现对不同规模数据集及仅含观测数据的统计模型进行比较评估。

Method: 将最优传输中的Wasserstein距离引入泰勒图框架，构建Wasserstein-Taylor图。其核心依据是：在泰勒图中，若将中心化均方根误差替换为中心化2-Wasserstein距离、将Pearson（乘积矩）相关系数替换为分位数相关系数，余弦定律依然成立，从而用分布距离替代逐点误差比较。

Result: 构建了Wasserstein-Taylor图这一新型评估工具，证明其能比较不同大小的观测与预测数据集，并可评估仅有观测数据的统计模型；同时进一步证明经验测度上的分位数相关系数收敛于被抽样测度上的分位数相关系数。

Conclusion: Wasserstein-Taylor图通过Wasserstein距离提供的分布视角，克服了传统泰勒图对数据规模与成对匹配的限制，扩展了模型评估的适用范围，并具有统计收敛性保证，是一种更具通用性的可视化比较工具。

Abstract: The Taylor diagram is used to evaluate and compare predictive models with observed data and has many applications in climate and environmental sciences. Three statistics of interest--the centered root-mean-squared error, standard deviation, and the product-moment correlation coefficient--are related by the law of cosines; Taylor diagrams leverage this fact to allow visualization of all three statistics in a two-dimensional plot without any loss of information. In this work, we present a novel model evaluation tool--the Wasserstein-Taylor diagram--formed from integrating the Wasserstein distance from optimal transport into a Taylor diagram framework. This tool is built upon the fact that the law of cosines still holds in the Taylor diagram if the centered root-mean-squared error and product-moment correlation coefficient are replaced by the centered 2-Wasserstein distance and quantile correlation coefficient, respectively. The advantage of this Wasserstein-Taylor diagram is that one can use the distribution perspective afforded by the Wasserstein distance to compare observed and predicted data sets of different sizes and even to compare statistical models with only observed data. We further show that the quantile correlation coefficient on empirical measures converges to the quantile correlation coefficient on the sampled measures.

</details>


### [275] [Variable-Projection Sparse Functional Principal Component Analysis: Interpretable Functional Dimensionality Reduction with Applications to Raman Spectral Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17963)
*Han Ying Lim,Dharini Pathmanathan,Philipp Otto,Sophie Dabo-Niang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出变投影稀疏函数主成分分析（VP-SFPCA），通过稀疏权函数实现可解释的函数降维，在仿真与SERS光谱数据上相比传统FPCA和SCAD-SFPCA具有计算优势且保持接近的重构精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FPCA生成的成分通常是稠密的，难以识别对主导变异模式有贡献的局部区域；这一局限在Raman光谱等有序域函数数据中尤为突出，因为解释往往聚焦于具有化学意义的谱区。

Method: 将稀疏FPCA形式化为带正则化且嵌入函数内积几何的矩阵分解问题，区分用于生成成分得分的稀疏权函数与用于重构的正交载荷函数；利用变投影对载荷函数进行条件最小化，从而将优化问题归约为仅对稀疏权重的求解。

Result: 仿真中VP-SFPCA能恢复局部化函数结构且计算量显著低于SCAD-SFPCA；实证分析中保持计算优势，留出重构误差与传统FPCA接近，估计权函数的若干显著特征与已知的腺嘌呤SERS谱带吻合。

Conclusion: VP-SFPCA通过局部化提供了一种计算上实用的方法，可提升主导函数模式的可解释性。

Abstract: Functional principal component analysis (FPCA) provides low-rank representations of functional data but generally produces dense components, making it difficult to identify the localised regions contributing to dominant modes of variation. This limitation is particularly relevant in Raman spectroscopy, where spectra are observed over an ordered domain and interpretation often focuses on chemically meaningful spectral regions. This study proposes variable-projection sparse FPCA (VP-SFPCA) for interpretable functional dimensionality reduction through sparse weight functions that promote localisation. The method formulates sparse FPCA as a regularised matrix-factorisation problem that incorporates the functional inner-product geometry and distinguishes sparse weight functions used to generate component scores from orthonormal loading functions used for reconstruction. Variable projection conditionally minimises over the loading functions, reducing the optimisation problem to the sparse weights. Performance was evaluated through simulation studies and empirical analyses of surface-enhanced Raman scattering (SERS) spectra, with conventional FPCA and SCAD-SFPCA serving as dense and sparse functional benchmarks, respectively. In the simulations, VP-SFPCA recovered localised functional structure while requiring substantially less computation than SCAD-SFPCA. In the empirical analysis, VP-SFPCA retained this computational advantage while yielding held-out reconstruction error close to that of conventional FPCA. Several prominent features of the estimated weight functions also coincided with established adenine SERS bands. Overall, VP-SFPCA provides a computationally practical approach to improving the interpretability of dominant functional modes through localisation.

</details>


### [276] [Weighted Least Squares in Integrated Galton--Watson Processes: Intercept Inference and Optimal Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17999)
*Yang Lu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文推广了 Wei–Winnicki 的状态加权最小二乘估计，将权重由 (1+X_{t-1})^{-1} 扩展为 (a+X_{t-1})^{-1}（a 为任意固定正数），解决了递归情形下截距估计的渐近分布问题，并给出严格递归时多项式速率、边界处 log n 速率（极限分布非正态）的结果，同时验证了跨三种区制的估计偏移量程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wei 和 Winnicki (1990) 在带移民的积分 Galton–Watson 过程中使用权重 (1+X_{t-1})^{-1} 的 WLS，但未能解决递归情形下截距估计量的渐近分布；Lu (2026) 改用权重 1/t 的时间加权 WLS 虽得到跨区制统一的高斯程序，但速率仅为 √(log n)。本文旨在保留状态加权方案以提升收敛速度，同时解决其遗留的分布问题。

Method: 将 Wei–Winnicki 的状态加权 WLS 从权重 (1+X_{t-1})^{-1} 推广为 (a+X_{t-1})^{-1}（任意固定正数 a），推导截距估计量的渐近分布，并基于此建立统一的估计偏移量（estimated-offset）推断程序，通过模拟和应用进行评估。

Result: 在严格递归（strict recurrence）下收敛速率为 n 的多项式阶，在边界处速率为 log n 且极限分布非正态；所提估计偏移量程序在三种区制下均成立。模拟表明施加单位根能显著改善覆盖，尤其在边界附近；对加拿大洪灾次数的应用显示状态加权漂移估计对样本起始年份的敏感性明显低于时间加权估计。

Conclusion: 通过推广权重参数 a，作者解决了状态加权 WLS 截距估计的渐近分布难题，获得了比时间加权方案更快的收敛速率，并提供了跨区制有效的统一推断方法，在模拟和加拿大洪灾数据应用中表现良好。

Abstract: In integrated Galton--Watson processes with immigration, Wei and Winnicki (1990) fitted weighted least squares (WLS) with weights $(1+X_{t-1})^{-1}$ and left open the asymptotic distribution of the resulting intercept estimator in the recurrent case. Lu (2026) bypasses this difficulty by proposing time-weighted WLS with weights $1/t$. While this estimator yields one Gaussian procedure valid across all regimes, its rate is only $\sqrt{\log n}$. We extend Wei--Winnicki's state-weighted WLS estimator to weights $(a+X_{t-1})^{-1}$ for any fixed positive $a$. We solve this distributional problem and show that the convergence rate is polynomial in $n$ under strict recurrence and $\log n$ at the boundary, where the limiting distribution is nonnormal. We also justify a common estimated-offset procedure across all three regimes. Simulations illustrate the finite-sample performance of the resulting inference and show that imposing the unit root substantially improves coverage, especially near the boundary. An application to Canadian flood-disaster counts shows that state-weighted drift estimates are substantially less sensitive to the sample's starting year than time-weighted estimates.

</details>


### [277] [Pseudo-Incrementality Testing: Measuring Advertising Lift from Naturally Occurring Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18257)
*Georgios Filippou,Boi Mai Quach,Ashish Kumar Jha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在无法进行随机实验时衡量广告增量效应的方法：将企业因运营产生的广告突变（预算削减、渠道上线、项目暂停）视为准实验，通过两阶段流程（贝叶斯变点检测 + 方差约束的结构时间序列因果影响分析）识别干预日期并估计效应，并在 Meta GeoLift 两个实验基准上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机实验常常不可用或成本高昂，但企业运营中会自然产生广告的突然干预（预算调整、渠道启停、项目暂停），这些事件可充当准实验。现有方法多依赖地理面板或缺乏严谨的时序因果推断与推断有效性保证，难以在无地理面板条件下同时保证区间正确且足够窄以便决策。

Method: 提出两阶段流程：第一阶段对营销活动序列进行精确贝叶斯游程长度推断（Bayesian run-length inference），自动发现并确定干预发生日期；第二阶段以方差约束的结构时间序列反事实做因果影响分析，配合基于模拟的推断、五项合格性条件以及一个闭式功效下界，从而估计广告增量效应并保证推断可靠性。

Result: 在 Meta GeoLift 发布的两个实验基准上，方法两次均精确识别干预日期。在移除广告的实验中，效应恢复结果与实验估计偏差在 6.2% 以内（隐含回报 1.50 对 1.60）；在添加广告的实验中，其 90% 区间覆盖实验估计且不含零。在六个基于相同发布数据的估计器中，本方法是唯一无需地理面板、且在两个实验上区间均正确且足够窄、可供决策使用的。

Conclusion: 该方法能在缺乏随机实验的情况下，利用企业自发的营销中断作为准实验，可靠地识别干预日期并估计广告增量效应；其区间推断既正确又务实，适用于无地理面板数据的场景，为增量测量提供了可操作的替代方案。

Abstract: We develop a method for measuring the incremental effect of advertising when randomized exper- iments are unavailable. Firms generate abrupt interventions in their own marketing as a byproduct of operations: budgets are cut, channels launch, programs pause. We propose a two-stage proce- dure that treats these events as quasi-experiments. The first stage discovers and dates interventions by exact Bayesian run-length inference on marketing activity series. The second stage runs a causal impact analysis against a variance-constrained structural time series counterfactual, with simulation-based inference, five qualification conditions, and a closed-form power bound. We validate the procedure on the two experimental benchmarks that Meta released with its GeoLift framework. The method identifies the day of intervention exactly in both cases. On the experiment where advertising was removed, it recovers the effect within 6.2 percent of the experimental esti- mate (implied return of 1.50 versus 1.60). On the experiment where advertising was added, its 90 percent interval covers the experimental estimate and excludes zero. Of six estimators evaluated on the same released data, ours is the only one that requires no geographic panel and produces intervals that are both correct on both experiments and narrow enough to act on.

</details>


### [278] [What Does a Benford Test Actually Test? Marginal Conformity, Sampling Structure, and Forensic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18424)
*Arthur Charpentier*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文区分了本福德定律的边缘分布陈述与基于独立多项抽样模型校准的首位数字皮尔逊$p$值，通过四种具有相同本福德边缘但不同联合结构的构造，证明本福德$p$值的有效性依赖统计量、抽样律和校准程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清本福德定律作为边缘分布陈述与其传统皮尔逊检验在独立多项抽样模型下校准之间的根本差异，指出现有检验未考虑序列的联合结构，可能导致虚假拒绝率升高。

Method: 构造四种共享同一一次性或合并本福德目标、但联合结构不同的设计，包括包裹高斯圆形马尔可夫构造、随机旋转和随机组合设计；提出序列级二阶矩匹配校准，并用网格近似的连续对数有效数字Cramér-von Mises离差进行验证。

Result: 在包裹高斯圆形马尔可夫构造中，尽管每个一次性边缘分布精确为本福德，名义5%的皮尔逊检验在固定持续水平下仍拒绝21.6%的样本；经二阶矩匹配校准后拒绝率降至4.8%。该效应在不同样本量和序列长度下持续存在，且网格近似Cramér-von Mises检验显示相同的设计依赖性。修正后的程序在光滑边缘偏离增大时拒绝概率上升。

Conclusion: 本福德符合性是一个边缘陈述；本福德$p$值仅在指定统计量、抽样律和校准程序下才有效；完整性声明需要实质性竞争模型。同时识别出信息边界：仅基于有效数字的方法无法检测保持完整有效数字过程不变的改变，但能检测改变该过程的扰动。

Abstract: Benford's law specifies a marginal distribution for significant digits, whereas the usual first-digit Pearson $p$-value is calibrated under an independent multinomial sampling model. We separate these statements with four constructions that share the same one-time or pooled Benford target but have different joint structures. Under a wrapped-Gaussian circular Markov construction, the nominal 5% Pearson test rejects 21.6% of samples at a fixed persistence level even though every one-time marginal is exactly Benford; a sequence-level second-order moment-matching calibration reduces the rejection rate to 4.8%. The same strategy performs well in the randomized-rotation and random-composition designs. The effect persists across changes in sample size and sequence length, and a grid approximation to a continuous log-significand Cramér-von Mises discrepancy shows the same design dependence. In the wrapped-Gaussian design examined here, corrected procedures retain rejection probabilities that rise with the size of a smooth marginal departure. Finally, we distinguish calibration from an information boundary: significand-only methods cannot detect changes that leave the complete significand process unchanged, but they readily detect perturbations that alter it. Benford conformity is a marginal statement; a Benford $p$-value is valid only relative to a specified statistic, sampling law, and calibration procedure; an integrity claim requires substantive competing models.

</details>


### [279] [Doubly valid and doubly sharp sensitivity analysis to unobserved confounding for survival outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18713)
*Jean-Baptiste Baitairian,Bernard Sebastien,Rana Jreich,Sandrine Katsahian,Agathe Guilloux*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为存在未观测混杂的生存结局因果推断提出了边际敏感性模型（MSM）下的敏感性分析框架，针对生存函数差异和限制平均生存时间（RMST）构造了双重有效、双重尖锐（DVDS）的界，并考虑了信息性删失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学和罕见病中时间至事件结局十分关键，常用生存曲线差异或RMST衡量治疗效果。但在真实世界数据中，因果效应估计依赖"无未观测混杂"这一几乎难以满足的假设，因此需要敏感性分析方法来评估结论对未观测混杂的稳健性。

Method: 作者在边际敏感性模型（MSM）框架下，将近期DVDS结果推广到时间至事件场景，并处理信息性删失，提出生存函数差异与RMST的双重有效、双重尖锐（DVDS）界限。为便于处理，假设删失与未观测混杂相互独立。

Result: 与文献中已有方法相比，该方法在模拟数据和真实数据上得到更紧的界限，且计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 提出的DVDS界在MSM下为生存结局提供了更紧、更高效的敏感性分析。但假设删失与未观测混杂独立的限制，留待未来工作解决。

Abstract: Time-to-event outcomes are central in oncology and rare diseases, where treatment effects are often summarized by differences in survival curves or Restricted Mean Survival Time (RMST). In real-world data, estimating these causal effects relies on the absence of unobserved confounding, an assumption that is rarely satisfied. We develop a sensitivity analysis framework for causal treatment effects with survival outcomes under the Marginal Sensitivity Model (MSM). We introduce doubly valid and doubly sharp (DVDS) bounds for differences in survival functions and RMST, extending recent DVDS results to the time-to-event setting while accounting for informative censoring. In practice, our method yields tighter bounds and improved computational efficiency compared to a previous approach from the literature, on simulated and real data. For tractability, we assume independence between censoring and unobserved confounding, a limit that should be addressed in future works.

</details>


### [280] [A new tractable Archimedean copula for full-range tail dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18742)
*Lei Hua*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型阿基米德Copula族（FRA1），能同时刻画上下尾的全范围尾部相依，并具有闭式密度函数，解决了现有全范围尾部相依Copula无闭式密度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能刻画上下尾全范围尾部相依的参数Copula在藤Copula模型中作为pair-copula构建块非常有用，但构造同时具备闭式密度函数的此类Copula极具挑战性；据作者所知，现有所有全范围尾部相依Copula均无闭式密度函数，这限制了它们在复杂建模场景中的实际应用。

Method: 作者提出一个新的阿基米德Copula族——I型全范围阿基米德Copula（FRA1），具有两个参数，分别控制下尾和上尾的相依强度，并拥有闭式密度函数；给出该Copula的相依性质结果，讨论基于极大似然的参数估计方法；构建仅使用FRA1的藤Copula模型，并将其应用于医疗支出面板调查（MEPS）数据，同时实现于R包CopulaOne中。

Result: 仅使用FRA1构建的藤Copula模型能达到普通藤Copula模型的相媲美性能，同时避免了对pair-copula进行模型选择的需要；FRA1藤模型能有效刻画医疗支出中多样的时序与截面相依模式。

Conclusion: FRA1 Copula凭借闭式密度与双参数的全范围尾部相依刻画能力，在藤Copula建模中兼具灵活性与实用性，可规避pair-copula模型选择问题，并已集成到R包CopulaOne中供使用。

Abstract: Parametric copulas capable of capturing full-range tail dependence in both lower and upper tails are useful in many applications, including as pair-copula building blocks in vine copula models. However, it is challenging to create such copulas that have closed-form density functions, which limits their practical applicability in complex modeling scenarios. To the best of our knowledge, all existing full-range tail dependence copulas do not have closed-form density functions. In this paper, we propose a new Archimedean copula family, called the \emph{full-range Archimedean copula of type I} (FRA1), that captures full-range tail dependence in both tails and admits a closed-form density function. The copula has two parameters, one controlling the strength of dependence in the lower tail and the other controlling that in the upper tail. We provide results on dependence properties of the copula, and discuss how to effectively estimate the parameters based on maximum likelihood. We build a vine copula model only using the FRA1 copula, and demonstrate that the model can achieve comparable performance of an ordinary vine copula model while avoiding the need of pair-copula model selection. The new copula is also applied to data from the Medical Expenditure Panel Survey, demonstrating that an FRA1 vine model can effectively capture diverse temporal and cross-sectional dependence patterns in medical expenditures. Finally, the new copula is implemented in \texttt{CopulaOne}, an R package for full-range tail dependence copulas.

</details>


### [281] [Efficient transport and generalization of survival treatment effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18764)
*Axel Martin,Iván Díaz,Michele Santacatterina*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在离散时间设定下，提出非参数、去偏的机器学习估计器，用于将源试验人群的生存处理效应差异传递（transport）与推广（generalize）到目标人群，并推导高效影响函数、证明双重稳健性与半参数效率，同时提出利用效应修饰子集降维的改进估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验虽能提供有内部效度的处理效应估计，但由于基线协变量分布差异、治疗依从性差异或结局机制差异，其结果未必能直接适用于更广泛的目标人群。因此需要发展能将试验证据外推到目标人群的因果推断方法，尤其是在生存结局（time-to-event）场景下。

Method: 在标准传递性与时间到事件可识别性假设下，于离散时间框架中推导传递与推广生存差异估计量的高效影响函数，构建交叉拟合的一步（one-step）去偏机器学习估计器；进一步通过生存函数的可加参数化引入已知效应修饰子集，降低重加权的维度，从而获得更小或相等的渐近方差。所有估计器均建立渐近正态性、双重稳健性与收敛速率。

Result: 所提出的交叉拟合一步估计器在弱正则条件下具备双重稳健性并达到半参数效率界；利用效应修饰子集的估计器有效降维并降低渐近方差。蒙特卡洛模拟在灵活及误设的讨厌参数估计情境下验证了有限样本性质；应用于妇女健康倡议（WHI）数据估计激素治疗对冠心病的影响，覆盖试验与观察性人群。

Conclusion: 本文为生存结局的传递与推广问题提供了具有理论保障（渐近正态、双重稳健、半参数效率）的非参数机器学习估计框架，并通过效应修饰子集实现方差改进，兼具方法创新与实际应用价值。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials provide internally valid estimates of treatment effects, but their results may not directly apply to broader target populations due to differences in baseline covariate distributions, adherence to treatment or variations in outcome mechanisms. Under standard transport and time-to-event identifiability assumptions, we develop nonparametric, debiased machine learning estimators for transporting and generalizing causal survival treatment effect differences from a source population to a target population in discrete time. We derive the efficient influence functions for the transport and generalization survival difference estimands and propose cross-fitted one-step estimators that are doubly robust and achieve semiparametric efficiency bounds under weak regularity conditions. We further introduce estimators that exploit known effect modifier subsets through an additive parameterization of the survival function, reducing the dimensionality of the reweighting and yielding smaller or equal asymptotic variance. We establish asymptotic normality, double robustness, and rates of convergence for all proposed estimators. Finite-sample properties are illustrated through Monte Carlo simulations under flexible and misspecified nuisance estimation scenarios. We apply the methods to data from the Women's Health Initiative to estimate the effect of hormone therapy on coronary heart disease across trial and observational populations.

</details>


### [282] [Earth and space observations meet complex algebras: from complex to octonions for multivariate autoregressive time series analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18794)
*Susana Eyheramendy,Felipe Elorrieta,Wilfredo Palma,Javier Lopatin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超复数自回归过程的离散时间框架，用于分析不规则采样间隔下的多元时间序列。该方法将多元观测嵌入超复数代数结构，并以状态空间形式表示，利用卡尔曼滤波进行参数估计与预测，在遥感和天文数据上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数据集（如地球观测与天文测量）以不规则时间间隔的多元时间序列形式出现。经典时间序列模型假设时间离散且观测等间隔，难以刻画观测间隔变化时的动态。现有处理不规则采样的方法依赖连续时间公式，且假设观测间隔足够小，这一假设在实际中常常不成立。因此需要一种离散时间框架，既能容纳不规则的观测间隔，又能保留多元依赖结构。

Method: 提出基于超复数自回归（hypercomplex autoregressive）过程的框架：将多元观测嵌入超复数代数结构，使多个变量及其交互在单一数学实体中表示，同时纳入不规则观测间隔。所得过程具有结构化矩阵表示，可表达为状态空间系统，从而利用卡尔曼滤波技术进行递归的参数估计与预测。

Result: 该方法利用状态空间表示支持在不规则采样间隔下进行递归估计和预测。在遥感与天文数据集上的应用表明，该框架能够捕捉传统时间序列技术难以建模的动态特性与跨变量交互。

Conclusion: 所提框架为不规则采样多元时间序列提供了一种分析方法，并有望推广到跨学科观测数据的建模。

Abstract: Many datasets arise as multivariate time series observed at irregular time intervals, particularly in Earth observation and astronomical measurements. Classical time-series models assume that time is discrete and observations occur at equally spaced intervals, limiting their ability to capture dynamics when observation gaps vary. Existing approaches to irregular sampling rely on continuous-time formulations, which assume that observation intervals are sufficiently small. We address this limitation by introducing a discrete-time framework that accommodates irregular observation gaps while preserving multivariate dependence structures. We introduce a framework for the analysis of irregularly observed multivariate time series based on hypercomplex autoregressive processes. The framework embeds multivariate observations into a hypercomplex algebraic structure, allowing multiple variables and their interactions to be represented within a single mathematical entity while incorporating irregular observation gaps. The resulting process admits a structured matrix representation, which enables the model to be expressed as a state-space system. This formulation provides an estimation methodology in which model parameters can be inferred using Kalman filtering techniques. The state-space representation allows recursive estimation and prediction in the presence of irregular sampling intervals. The performance of the methodology is illustrated through data and applications to remote sensing and astronomical datasets, where observations occur at nonuniform time intervals. The results demonstrate that the approach captures dynamics and cross-variable interactions difficult to model using traditional time-series techniques. The proposed framework provides a methodology for analyzing irregularly sampled multivariate time series and opens possibilities for modeling observational data across disciplines.

</details>


### [283] [Regularized Estimation of Spatial Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18951)
*Wen-Ting Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出结合平滑性与稀疏性正则化的空间模式估计方法（SpatPCA 和 SpatMCA），用于从低信噪比大气数据中提取更具物理意义的空间结构，并在印度洋海温和东非降水的实例中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖与厄尔尼诺现象导致极端天气频发。传统统计方法（如主成分分析 PCA、最大协方差分析 MCA）在信噪比较低时，得到的空间模式噪声大、缺乏物理可解释性，难以有效揭示大气变量的真实空间结构。

Method: 提出正则化方法 SpatPCA 和 SpatMCA，同时引入平滑性（smoothness）与稀疏性（sparseness）惩罚，使估计的空间模式更具可解释性；方法适用于规则与不规则间距数据；并基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）设计高效求解算法。

Result: 通过对印度洋海表温度数据与东非降水数据的分析，证明了所提方法能够有效揭示空间结构，并研究印度洋温度变化如何影响东非降水，相比传统方法得到更清晰、更有物理意义的模式。

Conclusion: 结合平滑与稀疏正则化的方法能够在低信噪比下稳健地估计可解释的空间模式，优于传统 PCA/MCA，为大气与气候数据分析提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Global warming and the El Niño phenomenon cause various climate changes and anomalies worldwide. Recently, extreme weather events such as heatwaves, droughts, and floods have become increasingly frequent. Researchers have studied atmospheric dynamics to reduce potential damage and increase safety. Statistical methods such as principal component analysis and maximum covariance analysis have been widely used to analyze spatial patterns of atmospheric variables. However, when the signal-to-noise ratio is low, the patterns obtained from these methods are often too noisy to be physically meaningful.
  This dissertation proposes regularization methods that simultaneously incorporate smoothness and sparseness penalties to estimate spatial patterns more interpretably. The proposed methods, called SpatPCA and SpatMCA, can be applied to both regularly and irregularly spaced data. An efficient algorithm based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) is developed for computation. Through analysis of sea surface temperature data in the Indian Ocean and precipitation data in East Africa, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods in revealing the spatial structure and studying how temperature variations in the Indian Ocean influence precipitation in East Africa.

</details>


### [284] [Forecast reconciliation meets compositional data analysis: A study of Italian cause-specific age-at-death](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18987)
*Andrea Nigri,Giacomo Lanfiuti Baldi,Raffaele Mattera,Han Lin Shang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出将成分数据分析（CoDA）与分层/分组时间序列预测相结合的新方法，用于改进死亡率预测，并在意大利分死因死亡率数据上验证了其相对于传统CoDA的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 死亡率数据可按死因和性别自然分解为层级结构，传统CoDA虽能处理成分数据的约束，但难以保证不同汇总层级之间预测结果的内在一致性，且预测精度有待提高。因此需要一种兼顾层级一致性与预测精度的框架。

Method: 提出一种将成分数据分析（CoDA）与分层及分组时间序列预测相结合的新框架，利用死亡率数据按死因和性别的固有分解结构，在各汇总层级间保证内部一致性，并维持高预测精度。

Result: 在意大利分死因死亡率数据上的实证结果表明，相较于传统CoDA，该方法显著提升了预测的可靠性，尤其是总体死亡率的预测；总死亡人数按死因和按性别划分的预测精度也获得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了死亡率预测中层级一致性与预测精度的权衡，为层级/分组死亡率预测提供了更可靠的成分数据分析工具，具有较好的应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce a novel approach that integrates compositional data analysis (CoDA) in the forecasting of hierarchical and grouped time series and apply it to improve mortality forecasts. By leveraging the inherent decomposition of mortality data by cause of death and sex, our framework addresses key challenges in mortality forecasting, ensuring internal coherence across different levels of aggregation while maintaining high predictive accuracy. Our empirical results on Italian cause-specific mortality data demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves the reliability of forecasts compared to the traditional CoDA, especially for total mortality forecasts. Consistent improvements in forecasting accuracy are also found for total deaths by cause and by sex.

</details>


### [285] [Do stable performance metrics guarantee stable model predictions?: An empirical investigation using the GUSTO-1 dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19019)
*Natthanaphop Isaradech,Wachiranun Sirikul,Wuttipat Kiratipaisarl,Pakpoom Wongyikul,Noraworn Jirattikanwong,Phichayut Phinyo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了临床预测模型在不同训练样本下的稳定性，发现判别能力(AUC)的稳定早于个体预测的稳定，且人工神经网络(ANN)比逻辑回归(LR)更不稳定、需要更大的样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规的模型验证仅量化和校正性能估计中的过拟合，但未充分评估基于略有不同的训练样本所开发模型的稳定性。作者希望探究"稳定的判别能力是否意味着稳定的个体预测"，并比较逻辑回归与人工神经网络的差异。

Method: 使用GUSTO-I试验中40,830名参与者的数据，以30天死亡率为结局，设置六种不同样本量场景(对应不同的每个变量事件数EPV)。每个场景中分别用8个预测因子开发LR和ANN模型，并通过200次bootstrap重采样，评估判别能力稳定性、平均绝对预测误差(MAPE)和分类不稳定指数(CII)。

Result: LR的判别能力在EPV为44.50时趋于稳定，而MAPE和CII直到EPV为89.12才稳定。ANN的判别能力波动更大，其乐观度(optimism)仅在EPV为178.25时才稳定，且在EPV为4.50时ANN预测存在严重校准不良(miscalibration)。总体上两种方法的判别能力均比个体预测更早稳定，ANN的不稳定性更大且所需样本量更多。

Conclusion: 模型开发者应评估并报告预测层面的稳定性，因为仅凭判别指标可能无法反映个体预测的可靠性。

Abstract: Model validation is routinely used to quantify and correct for overfitting in performance estimates, but it does not fully address the stability of models developed from slightly different training samples. We investigated whether stable discrimination implies stable individual predictions and compared logistic regression (LR) with artificial neural networks (ANNs). Using data from 40,830 participants in the GUSTO-I trial, with 30-day mortality as the outcome, we created six sample-size scenarios with varying events per variable (EPV). In each scenario, LR and ANN models were developed using eight predictors, and 200 bootstrap resamples were used to assess stability in discrimination, mean absolute prediction error (MAPE), and the classification instability index (CII). For LR, discrimination stabilized at an EPV of 44.50, whereas MAPE and CII did not stabilize until an EPV of 89.12. ANN discrimination was more volatile, with optimism stabilizing only at an EPV of 178.25, and ANN predictions were severely miscalibrated at an EPV of 4.50. Overall, discrimination stabilized earlier than individual predictions for both approaches, with greater instability and larger sample-size requirements for ANN. Model developers should therefore assess and report prediction-level stability, because discrimination metrics alone may not reflect the reliability of individual predictions.

</details>


### [286] [Calibrated Predictive Distributions from Sample-Based Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19035)
*Wen-Ting Wang,ShengLi Tzeng,Yu-Ting Fan,Hsin-Cheng Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种偏差校正的保形概率积分变换（PIT）校准方法，可在无似然表示的条件下，将基于样本的预测分布（如扩散模型、集合预报）后处理为全局校准的概率预测，而非仅输出固定水平预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型（扩散模型、集合预报器）常产生无易处理似然表示的预测样本，这类基于样本的预测分布可能存在系统性偏差且校准不良；而标准保形预测主要提供固定水平预测集或阈值决策，难以直接估计预测概率、高分位数及高密度区域，无法满足对完整校准预测分布的需求。

Method: 提出一个分割样本后处理框架：先在留出的偏差分割上估计仿射位置-尺度校正，再使用保形校准器对随机化PIT值进行校准；最终预测律表示为生成器顺序统计量上的加权经验分布，据此可直接计算阈值一致的超限概率、任意分位数、最高密度区间、期望尾部损失及校准重采样；并引入基于PIT中心性得分的可选分割保形包装器，以有限样本边际覆盖率给出嵌套预测区间。

Result: 在可交换性假设下建立了有限样本概率意义下的校准性；通过受控误设的模拟研究和WeatherBench-2降水预报应用表明，相较于未校准的样本预测和仅区间保形基线，该方法在概率校准及下游分布汇总量上均有显著改进。

Conclusion: 该方法将保形校准与仿射位置-尺度校正相结合，能够从无似然的样本预测中构建全局校准的预测分布，并提供覆盖率保证及丰富的分布统计量，在模拟与真实业务预报场景中均显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Conditional generative models, including diffusion models and ensemble forecasters, often produce predictive samples without a tractable likelihood representation. Such sample-based predictive distributions can be systematically biased and poorly calibrated. We propose bias-corrected conformal probability integral transform (PIT) calibration, a split-sample post-processing framework that outputs a calibrated predictive distribution rather than a single fixed-level prediction interval. The method first estimates an affine location-scale correction on a held-out bias split, then calibrates randomized PIT values using a conformal calibrator. The resulting predictive law is represented as a weighted empirical distribution on the generator order statistics, enabling the direct computation of threshold-coherent exceedance probabilities, arbitrary quantiles, highest-density intervals, expected tail losses, and calibrated resamples. In contrast, standard conformal prediction primarily provides fixed-level prediction sets or threshold decisions and does not directly estimate predictive probabilities or high-density regions. We establish finite-sample calibration in probability under exchangeability and show how an optional split-conformal wrapper based on a PIT-centrality score gives nested prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage at user-specified levels. Simulation studies with controlled misspecification and a WeatherBench-2 precipitation-forecasting application demonstrate substantial improvements in probabilistic calibration and downstream distributional summaries relative to uncalibrated sample-based forecasts and interval-only conformal baselines.

</details>


### [287] [Double Descent in High-dimensional Linear Discriminant Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19061)
*Yonghe Lu,Han Lin Shang,Yanrong Yang,Kehan Zhao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文利用随机矩阵理论推导了高维设置下线性判别分析(LDA)的渐近误分类风险，完整刻画了其在欠参数化与过参数化两种情形下的双下降风险曲线，并通过模拟和ARCENE癌症分类数据验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双下降现象挑战了经典的偏差-方差权衡，但该现象对经典统计方法的启示尚不清晰。研究旨在探究LDA这一基础二分类方法是否及如何呈现双下降行为。

Method: 采用随机矩阵理论，在高维情形(p/n→γ)下推导LDA的渐近误分类风险：对γ∈(0,1)情形，给出一般总体协方差矩阵下LDA风险的显式渐近表达式；对γ∈(1,∞)情形，在各向同性协方差模型下推导Moore-Penrose伪逆LDA分类器的风险。

Result: 得到LDA在欠参数化(γ<1)与过参数化(γ>1)两个区域的完整双下降风险曲线，并在模拟研究与ARCENE癌症分类数据集上验证了理论与实验高度吻合。

Conclusion: LDA作为经典统计方法同样呈现双下降现象，且其风险曲线可通过随机矩阵理论精确刻画，表明双下降并非仅限深度学习高容量模型的特性。

Abstract: The double-descent phenomenon observed in modern machine learning models has challenged the classical bias-variance trade-off by showing that prediction error can decrease again as model complexity exceeds the interpolation threshold. Although this phenomenon has been extensively studied for deep neural networks and other high-capacity models, its implications for classical statistical methods remain less well understood. In this paper, we investigate double descent in linear discriminant analysis (LDA), a fundamental classification method for binary classification. Using random matrix theory, we derive the asymptotic misclassification risk of LDA in the high-dimensional regime where the dimension (p) and sample size (n) satisfy (p/n \to γ). Specifically, we obtain an explicit asymptotic expression for the LDA risk with a general population covariance matrix when (γ\in (0,1)), and for the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse LDA classifier under an isotropic covariance model when (γ\in (1,\infty)). Together, these results provide a complete characterization of the double-descent risk curve of LDA across both the under- and over-parameterized regimes. Simulation studies and experiments on the ARCENE cancer classification dataset demonstrate close agreement with the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [288] [Beyond Pretrends: A Discordance-Based Sensitivity Analysis for Difference-in-Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19081)
*Thomas Leavitt*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在双重差分（DiD）框架下提出基于"不协调性（discordance）"的敏感性分析模型，说明平行趋势假设下的推断在替代性反事实插补方法给出不同结论时尤为脆弱，并以南非种族隔离时期劳动力供给冲击对选举支持的影响为例，展示了该方法与传统基于前趋势的敏感性分析的不同结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DiD用对照组在政策后的变化来插补处理组的反事实变化，依赖平行趋势假设；但组间构成差异会导致组间时间演化的组间混杂（between-group confounding）。改用处理组自身前趋势插补虽能避免组间混杂，却引入组内时间冲击的组内混杂（within-group confounding）。两种插补各有脆弱性；若二者结论不一致，则平行趋势下的因果结论高度依赖该假设。然而现有基于前趋势的敏感性分析忽略了对照组后处理期对前趋势的偏离，可能错误地显示低敏感性。

Method: 作者提出基于不协调性的敏感性模型，其核心是：平行前趋势仅是低敏感性的必要条件而非充分条件。该模型被形式化为"平行趋势下的ATT与替代假设下ATT之间期望距离"的加权度量，权重为各假设的联合合理性（joint plausibility）。随后，作者给出决策论依据，用"平行趋势插补与替代插补之间的最坏情形不协调（worst-case discordance）"作为平行趋势违背的基准化（benchmarking）标准。最后将前趋势型与不协调型两种敏感性模型应用于南非的实证案例。

Result: 不协调型敏感性模型识别出：在前趋势型方法显示低敏感性（即看似稳健）的场景中，处理组与对照组的后处理期行为仍可能发生显著背离，使得平行趋势推断实际上高度脆弱。南非案例表明，两种敏感性模型对劳动力供给冲击影响种族隔离时期政策选举支持这一问题的评估结论存在差异。

Conclusion: 在DiD分析中，仅依赖前趋势进行敏感性检验并不充分，因为它无法捕捉对照组后处理期偏离前趋势的情形。基于不协调性的敏感性模型通过联合考量替代插补方法的合理性并利用最坏情形不协调进行基准化，能够更全面地揭示平行趋势假设的脆弱性，从而为因果推断的稳健性提供更可靠的评估。

Abstract: In the canonical Difference-in-Differences design, the control group's post-treatment change serves as an imputation of the treated group's counterfactual change in the same period, an imputation justified by parallel trends. However, differences in group composition can produce between-group differences in how outcomes would evolve over time, rendering this imputation vulnerable to confounding. An alternative imputation -- such as one based on the treated group's pre-treatment change -- avoids such between-group confounding but introduces the risk of confounding from within-group temporal shifts. Ideally, both imputations, each vulnerable to different sources of confounding, would have concordant values, thereby yielding the same causal conclusions. When the imputations are discordant, conclusions under parallel trends hinge more critically on that assumption since alternative imputations would point to different results. Yet in these scenarios, existing pretrends-based sensitivity analyses can show low sensitivity because they ignore post-treatment deviations from pretrends in the control group. This paper therefore proposes a discordance-based sensitivity model in which parallel pretrends are necessary but not sufficient for low sensitivity. I formally justify this model in terms of the expected distance between the ATT under parallel trends and under alternative assumptions, weighted by the joint plausibility of those assumptions. I then provide a decision-theoretic rationale for benchmarking violations of parallel trends using the worst-case discordance between the parallel trends imputation and alternative imputations. Finally, I apply both pretrends- and discordance-based sensitivity models to assess how a labor supply shock influenced electoral support for apartheid-era policies in South Africa, showing how the two approaches yield different results.

</details>


### [289] [RECaST-Surv: A Calibrated Borrowing Method for Survival Endpoints in Unequal Randomized Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19109)
*Dehua Bi,Arlina Shen,Ruben P. A. van Eijk,Lu Tian,Jiapeng Xu,Guillemette de la Borderie,Nate Bennet,Sarno Maria,Ying Lu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出 RECaST-Surv，一种用于生存结局的贝叶斯迁移学习框架，通过从外部对照数据学习结构生存模型并用柯西随机效应校准到当前试验，结合自助法校准检验规则以控制 I 类错误，在模拟和 ALS 试验仿真中显著提升检验效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机试验（尤其是不等随机化、儿科和罕见病场景）中同期对照信息有限，直接比较的对照患者少导致检验效能下降；借用外部对照信息可提高效率，但当外部与试验人群不充分可比时会推高 I 类错误率。因此需要一个既能借用外部信息提升效能、又能控制 I 类错误的生存分析框架。

Method: 提出 RECaST-Surv：将 RECaST 方法扩展到生存结局的贝叶斯迁移学习框架。先从外部对照数据学习一个结构生存模型，再通过柯西（Cauchy）随机效应该模型校准到当前试验的同期对照臂；为改善频率学派操作特性，用基于自助法（bootstrap）的程序校准检验规则以控制 I 类错误。方法支持多个外部数据集，且仅需外部来源的汇总级信息。

Result: 模拟研究显示，该方法在多种具有挑战性的场景下维持接近标称的 I 类错误率，同时相较标准分析将检验效能提高约 10%–12%。在肌萎缩侧索硬化（ALS）试验仿真中，RECaST-Surv 相对不借用的 RCT 分析将效能从 82.8% 提升至 95.7%，并保持可接受的错误控制。

Conclusion: RECaST-Surv 能够在仅获得外部汇总级信息的条件下，有效借用外部对照以提升生存分析的检验效能，同时在人群不匹配等困难场景下仍能通过自助法校准保持接近标称的 I 类错误控制，为儿科、罕见病等对照信息有限的试验提供了实用的统计工具。

Abstract: Randomized trials with limited concurrent control information---including but not limited to unequal-randomization settings---can offer ethical and practical advantages, especially in pediatric and rare diseases, but they often lose power because fewer control patients are available for direct comparison. Borrowing information from external controls may improve efficiency, but can also inflate the Type I error rate when the external and trial populations are not sufficiently comparable. We propose RECaST-Surv, a Bayesian transfer-learning framework for time-to-event outcomes that extends the RECaST method to survival settings. The method learns a structural survival model from external control data and calibrates it to the concurrent control arm of the current trial through a Cauchy random effect. To improve frequentist operating characteristics, we further develop a bootstrap-based procedure to calibrate the testing rule for Type I error control. RECaST-Surv can accommodate multiple external datasets and requires only summary-level information from external sources. Simulation studies show that the method maintains near-nominal Type I error across challenging settings while improving power by roughly 10%--12% over standard analyses. In an Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis trial emulation, RECaST-Surv increased power from 82.8% to 95.7% relative to the no-borrowing RCT analysis, while maintaining acceptable error control.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [290] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DISCERN，一个序列化的两阶段更新推广审计协议：零标签阶段仅凭未标注流量中的分歧率即可认证良性更新；有标签阶段通过对采样分歧点使用随时有效的置信序列进行标签审计。该协议提供有限样本有效性与近似 1/ρ 倍的标签复杂度节省，并支持从单一误差预算出发对无限次模型推广进行复合保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的模型会不断通过重训练、微调、量化或供应商替换等方式更新，而每次更新都面临比被替换模型更差的风险。作者希望将"更新推广"形式化为可认证的成对风险差审计，从而在不标注大量数据的前提下，可靠地判断新模型是否不劣于旧模型。

Method: 以"风险差集中在两模型分歧输入上"这一支持恒等式为出发点，构建 DISCERN 两阶段协议：（1）零标签阶段，仅利用未标注流量，当分歧率低于容忍阈值时即认证良性更新；（2）有标签阶段，仅对采样的分歧输入打标签，并使用在任何停止时刻、任何标签路由规则（甚至对抗性裁判）下均有效的随时有效置信序列。作者证明了有限样本有效性以及量级为 ρ²/ε² 的匹配标签复杂度界，使得利用免费分歧信息相较任何"配对盲"审计器节省 1/ρ 倍标签，且保证可在单一误差预算下跨无限推广序列组合。

Result: 在 785 个更新对（包括多达 14 亿参数的 LoRA 微调语言模型）上回放超过 14,000 条审计流：误覆盖率 0.0002（名义 5%），功效 0.986 且零误报，56% 的良性更新以零标签完成认证。每次审计还输出可机器核验的证据记录，用于上市后监控。

Conclusion: DISCERN 提供了一种标签高效、统计有效且可组合的模型更新推广审计方案，能显著降低审计成本并支撑持续的上市后监控。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [291] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在NVIDIA T4等消费级GPU上部署RAG时出现的"压缩悖论"（Compression Paradox）问题，提出了一种免训练、确定性的Tri-Metric Router，依据三个CPU侧信号（空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key、型符比TTR）在原始、神经压缩和词汇压缩三条管线间进行调度，实现0% OOM、88.5% oracle对齐率与49.3% Combined F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在16GB VRAM的T4等消费级GPU上部署RAG时，神经提示压缩会引入额外KV缓存竞争和预处理延迟，可能抵消生成阶段节省的时间；但不压缩又会在长上下文下导致显存不足（OOM）。作者识别出vLLM服务的LLM与PyTorch压缩器在紧内存预算下共同部署时的两种失效机制，需要一种能兼顾内存与延迟的调度策略。

Method: 提出Tri-Metric Router，一种确定性、免训练的策略，在Raw（原始）、Neural（LLMLingua-2神经压缩）、Lexical（BM25词汇压缩）三条管线间做选择。路由器使用三个CPU侧信号：空间复杂度（L）、句法密度（ρ_key）与型符比（TTR）。与以往仅做语义层面的自适应不同，其调度信号是"硬件物理"层面的，基于VRAM余量和延迟交叉点。阈值通过在LongBench qasper上剖析校准得到，在T4上的运行交叉点约在4332词处；作者强调贡献在于校准方法论而非特定硬件常数。

Result: 在分布外留出集上，方法实现0%的OOM失败率、88.5 ± 4.4%的oracle对齐率、49.3%的Combined F1，相比始终开启词汇压缩的方案提升5.2个百分点，且无需额外VRAM或训练成本。

Conclusion: Tri-Metric Router通过硬件物理层面的调度信号与校准方法论，有效解决了消费级GPU上RAG的压缩悖论，在零OOM的前提下取得了接近oracle的对齐效果，并以零额外成本优于固定压缩基线。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [292] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入 Transition Games 来定位 Transformer 中从记忆到泛化转变的功能表达位置，发现该转变是一种已有分布式电路的光谱重编码，而非模块切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图明确 Transformer 中从记忆到泛化的转变在功能上究竟发生在何处，并以可检验的方式区分不同模块（尤其是 MLP 与注意力）在这一过程中的作用。

Method: 作者提出 Transition Games——一种行为对齐的精确激活博弈，并配以成对的非泛化对照；通过替代博弈分析效用增益，并结合精确路径研究来追踪块-1 MLP 与其他下游路径的作用。

Result: 研究发现存在分布式效用增益，并带有前瞻性的块-0 注意力偏置；选定的二阶模式在替代博弈中贡献了 67–92% 的加法对比度。精确路径研究显示块-1 MLP 在 12/12 对中比所有其他测试下游路径更多地中介了这些模式的效果。而"MLP 记忆、注意力泛化"的预测被反转（记忆锚点处为 -.331 bits/example，12 对中 0 对符合预测方向）；路由起始、全局秩坍塌和素数不变架构脊线也均不成立。

Conclusion: grokking 在此处表现为对已有分布式电路的光谱重编码，而非模块间的切换。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [293] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对 CLIP 视觉语言模型进行公平性审计，发现基于 Open Access 艺术品元数据的零样本提示打分在性别维度上无显著差异，但指出该"等效"结论源于指标粗糙与噪声下限，而非模型真正公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文化遗产领域审计 VLM 的社会偏见时，需要区分模型直接产生的算法估值差异与档案元数据中嵌入的混淆因素；现有研究缺乏对零样本评分作为测量工具的灵敏度与等效性的严格量化评估。

Method: 利用大都会艺术博物馆 Open Access 藏品的历史艺术品元数据（N=1500，其中 743 件有作者归属：男性 534、女性 209；匿名 618），构建零样本 CLIP logit 差分分数的定量审计框架，覆盖 masterpiece、quality、influence 三组语义提示对；采用未调整的均值比较、双单侧检验（TOST，Cohen's d ≥ 0.25）以及控制媒介、创作年代、纵横比的多元 OLS 回归（R² < 0.02）进行多变量混淆控制分析。

Result: OpenAI CLIP（μ_F=-0.0067 vs μ_M=-0.0035，p=0.1829）与 OpenCLIP（μ_F=0.0171 vs μ_M=0.0237，p=0.1224）均无显著主效应；TOST 证实等效性（pTOST<0.005）；多元回归显示艺术家性别无显著条件效应（p>0.20）；但残差嵌入方差极高（R²<2%），表明全局零样本估值指标接近噪声下限，属于粗糙测量工具。

Conclusion: 宏观层面的分数等效反映了指标对细粒度视觉-语义特征的不敏感，不能排除局部微尺度视觉偏见；同时排除 41.2% 无归属藏品反映了制度性幸存者偏差。研究强调在 AI 公平性审计中必须进行多变量混淆控制、等效性检验和档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [294] [Temperon: Full-Time SAM Quality at a Third Less Wall-Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17575)
*Stamatis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Temperon 提出一种成本分配策略：前 43% 的 epoch 预算使用普通 SGD 探索，之后一次性切换到 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精调器完成整个余弦退火阶段，以远低于全时 SAM 的成本达到同等甚至更好的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 使每个训练步骤成本翻倍，但其收益集中在训练后期。作者研究昂贵训练模式应花在何处，以设计一种分阶段、成本高效且不牺牲精度的训练方案。

Method: 在前 43% 的 epoch 预算内使用普通 SGD 探索器，随后一次性切换到 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精调器，并把整个最终余弦退火阶段交给后者。通过消融实验精确归因各组件贡献，并在 CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet（五次种子）、GPT-2 预训练与 GLUE 微调上验证。成本统一以"达到目标的 epoch 数 × 经空闲 GPU 校准的单 epoch 成本"衡量。

Result: Temperon 在所有数据集上达到与最佳全时 SAM 配方相当的精度，在四分之三的数据集上达到最难公共目标的速度分别快 35%、34%、32%，且在同等成本下高于已发表的 SAM+SGD 配方一个档次。消融显示 Muon 精调器在其余条件固定时贡献 +0.85pp，而探索器形状与重启无贡献（已被撤回）。与最接近的对手 late-phase SAM 对比，后者在中等目标上最快，但 Muon 精调器带来的档次提升（CIFAR-100 上 0.83、CIFAR-10 上 0.97）是任何纯 SGD 精调方法都无法达到的；在 Muon 无档次增益的 Tiny ImageNet 上对手则胜出。该方法适用于 GPT-2 预训练（全 SAM 质量、墙钟时间 -29%）和 GLUE 微调（从不差于全时 SAM，SAM 成本仅为其三分之一）。经济学上：早期跳过 SAM 累计固定信用，Muon 单 epoch 成本为 SAM+SGD 的 1.50 倍（四个数据集一致）。

Conclusion: Temperon 表明把昂贵的训练模式（SAM+Muon）集中在训练后期比全程使用更经济高效，且该分配规律可迁移到语言模型预训练与微调。由于余弦调度下精度曲线呈"平台—突升"形态，切换时机无法从轨迹推断，只能由调度决定，因此定时切换是原则性的而非权宜之计。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) doubles the cost of every training step, yet its benefit concentrates where training ends. We study where an expensive training mode should be spent and propose Temperon: a plain-SGD explorer for the first 43% of the epoch budget, then one scheduled hand-off that gives the entire final cosine anneal to a SAM-wrapped Muon refiner. On CIFAR-10/100, SVHN and Tiny ImageNet (five seeds, times reported as epochs-to-target times an idle-GPU-calibrated epoch cost), Temperon matches the best full-time-SAM recipe on accuracy everywhere while reaching the hardest common target 35%, 34% and 32% sooner on three of the four, and sits a tier above the published SAM+SGD recipe at level cost. Ablations make the attribution exact: the Muon refiner is worth +0.85pp with everything else fixed; the explorer's shape and its restarts are worth nothing, and we withdraw them as contributions. Re-running the closest rival, late-phase SAM, at matched budget shows the frontier: it is fastest to every mid-level target, but the tier the Muon refiner buys (0.83 on CIFAR-100, 0.97 on CIFAR-10) is reached by no SGD-refined method in any seed, and on Tiny ImageNet, where Muon buys no tier, the rival simply wins -- the measured boundary of the method. The allocation law transfers to GPT-2 pretraining (full-SAM quality at -29% wall-clock) and GLUE fine-tuning (never worse than full-time SAM at a third of its SAM cost). Two constants organize the economics: skipping SAM early buys a fixed credit, and a Muon epoch costs 1.50x a SAM+SGD epoch on all four datasets. Finally, the hand-off cannot be timed from the trajectory: under cosine schedules the accuracy curve is plateau-then-surge, so the information lives in the schedule, making the scheduled switch principled rather than convenient. Code and a pip-installable implementation are released.

</details>


### [295] [When the Gradient Sees Rank: Provable Necessity, Causal Recruitment, and Composition in Trained Matrix Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17594)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究梯度训练能否学习存储和组合关联所需的秩，实验表明学习到的有效秩随键值对数量 K 增长，并在秩上限接近 K 时出现恢复相变，训练后的算子可反复自应用且近似理想循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究使用矩阵增强推理器完成的任务允许秩-1 解，无法回答“梯度训练能否学习到存储和组合关联所需的秩”这一开放问题。本文通过在需要精确线性恢复的 K 个新键值绑定上训练矩阵记忆，探究学习秩与所需最小秩的关系。

Method: 在 K 个新的键值绑定上训练矩阵记忆，其精确线性恢复要求秩(Z)≥K；使用固定线性读出查询单一矩阵状态，不访问原始绑定。以余弦相似度大于 0.9 作为恢复标准（区别于数学相等）。引入训练期秩上限（秩帽）以观察恢复行为，并通过 21 次算子自应用及实体子空间分析检验组合能力。实验覆盖 d=8、16 等维度，考察多种种子。

Result: 学习到的有效秩随 K 增加（d=16 时 Spearman ρ=1.0）；秩帽产生恢复相变，d=8、K=4 时秩 3 最多恢复 0.0004，秩 4 恢复 0.97。5 个种子中 4 个在 21 次自应用后保持至少 0.9996 恢复率。实体子空间上学习算子有效秩接近 K，并近似理想循环。对单个收敛于低于 K 的种子，用实体子空间算子与理想循环计算可预测 7 次应用内余弦误差在 0.008 以内。扩展训练可解决若干初始失败，但在编码器宽度固定、矩阵维度增大时恢复率仍下降。

Conclusion: 梯度训练确实能学习到存储和组合键值关联所需的秩：学习秩随 K 增长，并在秩帽等于 K 附近呈现清晰的恢复相变，说明最小秩是学习的约束瓶颈而非单纯的近似优解。训练后的算子支持高保真多次自应用，其行为可用实体子空间算子与理想循环精确建模，但大维度下仍存在扩展性挑战。

Abstract: Can gradient-based training learn the rank needed to store and compose associations in a matrix memory? In our earlier study, we used a matrix-augmented reasoner on a task that admits a rank-1 solution, leaving this question open. We train matrix memories on $K$ fresh key-value bindings whose exact linear recovery requires $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$. A fixed linear readout queries a single matrix state without access to the original bindings. Experiments measure recovery by cosine similarity greater than 0.9, a threshold distinct from mathematical equality. Learned effective rank increases with $K$ across the tested grid (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ at $d = 16$). Training-time rank caps produce a recovery transition near $k = K$: at $d = 8$, $K = 4$, rank 3 gives at most 0.0004 recovery and rank 4 gives 0.97. Four of five seeds retain at least 0.9996 recovery through 21-fold self-application of the trained operator. On the entity subspace, the learned operator has effective rank close to $K$ and approximates the ideal cycle. For the single converged seed capped below $K$, a calculation using the entity-subspace operator and ideal cycle predicts the measured cosine within 0.008 through seven applications. Extending training resolves several initial failures, but recovery still declines at larger matrix dimensions with encoder width fixed.

</details>


### [296] [Prior-Free Competitive Ratios for Improving Bandits: Scale, Curvature and Horizon Are Free, but Not Jointly Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17595)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究改进型多臂老虎机问题，证明无需先验知识即可实现近似最优的竞争比：通过探针-承诺算法移除对数因子，并在无噪声及乘性噪声模型中确定了各置信度条件下的最优价格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在改进型多臂老虎机中，各臂的未知非减、离散凹性奖励曲线使算法面临在探索与利用间权衡的挑战。此前 Blum 和 Ravichandran 证明已知尺度时随机化算法达到 O(√k) 近似、未知尺度时为 O(√k log k)，但未解决对数因子是否必要、最优竞争比的确切形式，以及无先验（不知尺度、凹包络指数、时域、噪声水平）时能否保持最优性能等问题。

Method: 提出一页篇幅的"探针-承诺"（probe-and-commit）算法，在 T≥2⌊√k⌋ 时无需任何尺度知识即达到 4√3·√k 的竞争比；确定每个时域的最优比 Θ(√k+k/T)（含未知时域）。在无噪声情形下，设计随机边缘探测算法，不读尺度 m、凹包络指数 β 或时域 T，对所有 β 与时域同时达到最优 Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)。在乘性噪声模型下，探针-承诺无需已知噪声水平即保持全时域阶 Θ(√k+k/T)；并引入价格函数 φ_ε(k)（在 T≥16k 上相对 k^{β/(1+β)} 的最坏损失）分析先验代价，证明其为 Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，匹配算法为嵌套随机排列探测算法；而仅知 m 或 β 之一即可恢复常数价格。

Result: 探针-承诺算法获得竞争比 4√3·√k（T≥2⌊√k⌋），移除对数因子；最优竞争比为 Θ(√k+k/T)，对未知时域同样成立。无噪声且无先验时达到最优 Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T) 对所有 β 与 T 同时成立。乘性噪声下保持 Θ(√k+k/T) 的全时域阶，且 φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，已知 m 或 β 之一即可降为常数。

Conclusion: 改进型多臂老虎机的对数因子是本不必要的，简单的探针-承诺算法即可达到 √k 阶的无先验近似最优。无噪声时完全无需先验知识即可同时对所有 β 与所有时域达到最优；在乘性噪声下，探针-承诺保持相同全时域阶而无须噪声水平，但"同为未知 m 与 β"的代价为 √(log k/log log k) 阶，单独知晓其一即恢复常数代价。这些结果精确刻画了各先验信息在改进型多臂老虎机中的价值。

Abstract: In the improving multi-armed bandits problem, each of $k$ arms has an unknown nondecreasing, discretely concave reward curve $f_i$, and pulling arm $i$ for the $t$-th time yields $f_i(t)$. For sufficiently long horizons, Blum and Ravichandran (ALT 2025) proved that randomized algorithms achieve an $O(\sqrt k)$ approximation to the best single arm when the scale $m=f^*(T)$ of the optimal arm is known ($T\ge2k$), and $O(\sqrt k\log k)$ when it is not ($T>4k$), against an $Ω(\sqrt k)$ lower bound. The logarithmic factor is unnecessary: a one-page \emph{probe-and-commit} algorithm achieves competitive ratio $4\sqrt3\,\sqrt k$ for $T\ge2\lfloor\sqrt k\rfloor$, without any knowledge of the scale, and we determine the optimal ratio for every horizon, $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$, also for unknown horizons. Without noise, \emph{no prior is needed at all}: a random-marginal probing algorithm reading neither the scale $m$, nor the concavity-envelope exponent $β$ of Blum, Garicano, Ravichandran and Sharma (UAI 2026), nor the horizon $T$, achieves the optimal $Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)$ simultaneously for every $β$ and every horizon. Under the multiplicative noise model of Blum and Ravichandran, probe-and-commit keeps the same all-horizon order $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$ without knowing the noise level (and $Θ(\sqrt k)$ on the same range), but the price of priors jumps: for any fixed noise level $\varepsilon\in(0,1/2]$, the uniform price of adaptation $φ_\varepsilon(k)$ --- the worst case over horizons $T\ge16k$ of the loss relative to $k^{β/(1+β)}$ for algorithms knowing neither $m$ nor $β$ --- is $Θ_\varepsilon(\sqrt{\log k/\log\log k})$, the lower bound asymptotic in $k$ at fixed positive $\varepsilon$ and matched by a nested random-permutation probing algorithm, whereas knowing either $m$ or $β$ alone restores a constant price.

</details>


### [297] [Lecture notes on Physics Informed Neural Networks, Neural Operators, and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17638)
*Alessandro Bombini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一份博士课程讲义，介绍物理信息神经网络（PINN）与神经算子（NOs），涵盖从零实现、开源库应用及前沿主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为博士研究生系统引入物理信息深度神经网络和神经算子的概念，并教授如何将其应用于工程、物理和石油储层等真实世界问题。

Method: 通过课程讲授，从零在PyTorch中实现PINN与神经算子，并使用NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等高级开源库解决实际问题；同时讨论混合模型（Mixture-of-Models）、傅里叶神经算子、物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PIKANs）等近期主题。

Result: 形成一套完整的课程讲义，内容覆盖PINN与神经算子的理论与实现，并结合实际领域案例（工程、物理、石油储层）展示其应用方法。

Conclusion: 课程成功建立了从基础实现到前沿算法的知识体系，使博士生能够掌握并运用PINN与神经算子技术解决多领域实际问题。

Abstract: This is the set of lecture notes for the PhD course \href{https://www.unibz.it/en/faculties/engineering/phd-computer-science/study-course-offering/2025/36967}{\textit{Physics Informed Neural Network}, held at the University of Bozen/Bolzano} in the academic year 2025/2026.
  The goal of the course was to introduce the concept of Physics Informed Deep Neural Networks (PINN) and Neural Operators (NOs), discuss their implementation from scratch in PyTorch and using advanced ad-hoc developed open-source libraries such as NVIDia PhysicsNeMo to address real-world problems in various fields (engineering, physics, petroleum reservoir). We discuss recent topics such as Mixture-of-Models, Fourier Neural Operators, Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs) and Fourier Neural Operators.

</details>


### [298] [Fathom: Per-Query Read Depth for Sparse Decoding over Offloaded KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17652)
*Vivek Kalyanarangan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fathom 提出一种 KV cache 的 key 扫描方法：查询按通道重要性自适应决定每个 key 通道读取多少比特（以位平面形式存储），用带位预算的逆向注水分配，在百万 token 场景下以更少字节读取实现更低注意力误差，并达到与精确 top-k 一致的解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当 agentic 会话长达百万 token 且多会话并存时，KV cache 及其排序索引驻留主机内存，每个 top-k 解码步都要扫描并排序全部 n 个 key，这次扫描的访存流量成为解码的瓶颈，需要减少每次 key 扫描读取的字节数。

Method: 将 4-bit K cache 以通道为主（channel-major）存储为位平面，使前 t 个位平面恰为该通道的 t-bit 量化值；每个查询拥有一个位预算，按各通道方差加权的通道重要性做逆向注水（reverse water-filling）来分配每通道读取的比特数。Fathom 读取量化 serving 栈已有的 4-bit K 副本，不额外建索引。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 的百万 token 场景下，Fathom 单解码步 GPU 时间比 Double Sparsity、Loki 和 SparQ(r=32) 的 136-bit 扫描快 1.67 倍；在与 SparQ 68-bit（r=16）相同 GPU 时间内，Fathom 读取字节少 18%，且在七个模型与上下文设置中的六个上注意力误差更低。RULER 类任务上每次 per-token 扫描都与精确 top-k 解码一致；真实 coding-agent 会话上以 92 位达到最精确 136-bit 扫描的步一致率。

Conclusion: Fathom 利用查询自适应的通道级位预算，在主机内存驻留的大规模 KV cache 上显著降低了 top-k key 扫描的访存与延迟，以更少比特达到与精确/高精度扫描相当的注意力精度；但当索引驻留 GPU 内存时该方法不再更快。

Abstract: When agentic sessions run to a million tokens with many sessions resident at once, the KV cache and the index that ranks it live in host memory, and the scan that ranks all n keys for a top-k step becomes the traffic that bounds decoding. We present Fathom, a key scan in which each query decides how many bits of each key channel to read. The 4-bit K cache is stored channel-major as bit planes, so a prefix of t planes is exactly the channel's t-bit quantizer, and the query spends its bit budget by reverse water-filling over the variance-weighted importance of its channels. At one million tokens on Qwen3-8B a decode step is 1.67x faster in GPU time than with the 136-bit scans of Double Sparsity, Loki and SparQ r=32, and in the same GPU time as SparQ's 68-bit read (r=16) Fathom reads 18% fewer bytes with lower attention error on six of seven model and context settings. On RULER-style tasks every per-token scan matches exact top-k decoding, and on real coding-agent sessions Fathom reaches the step agreement of the most accurate 136-bit scan at 92 bits. The store is the 4-bit K copy a quantized serving stack already holds, and the method is not faster when the index is resident in GPU memory.

</details>


### [299] [Reflect, Revise, Reuse: Training-Free Skill Evolution for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17653)
*Bofan Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Fei Tang,Zhengxi Lu,Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoSkill-GUI 是一个免训练的 GUI 智能体技能框架，将技能设计为可随执行反馈不断"反思-修订-复用"演化的多文件包，在 MobileWorld、AndroidWorld、OSWorld 上分别带来最高 +16.2%、+6.0%、+10.5% 的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 智能体技能框架大多将技能视为部署前生成的静态工件，未针对 GUI 执行的动态特性（弹窗、延迟加载、控件位移等导致预先固定的计划失效）设计，也无法从部署后的执行反馈中在线改进；作者认为 GUI 智能体需要的不是更好的静态技能，而是能在部署时无需额外训练即可依据执行反馈修订的"活的"程序性知识。

Method: 提出 EvoSkill-GUI，一个免训练框架。每个技能是结构化多文件包，包含检索元数据、可执行计划、备用定位、故障恢复规则、无障碍工具和失败案例。框架通过"反思-修订-复用"循环运行：执行器在 rollout 中进行即时修订；一个隔离的 critic 在严格信息隔离下诊断失败轨迹；执行器通过受限工具接口编辑特定技能文件，使技能库随部署持续演化。

Result: 在 MobileWorld、AndroidWorld、OSWorld 三个覆盖移动端和桌面端的主流 GUI 基准上，EvoSkill-GUI 在无任何训练的情况下持续提升多个基础模型，最大收益分别为 +16.2%、+6.0%、+10.5%；且演化后的技能库能继续惠及相关任务，而无需从零重建。

Conclusion: 相比追求更好的静态技能，让技能能够在部署时从执行反馈中自行修订是提升 GUI 智能体鲁棒性的关键。EvoSkill-GUI 以免训练方式实现了技能的持续演化，并在多个主流基准上验证了其有效性与可迁移性，代码已开源。

Abstract: GUI agents execute long-horizon tasks on dynamic graphical user interfaces, where pop-ups, delayed loads, and relocated widgets routinely invalidate plans fixed before execution. Recent agent-skill frameworks encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge to mitigate this, yet existing skill designs are largely developed without targeting GUI execution dynamics and treat skills as static artifacts produced before deployment rather than living procedural knowledge that improves through it. We argue that what GUI agents need is not better static skills, but skills that can be revised from execution feedback at deployment time, without additional training. We propose \textbf{EvoSkill-GUI}, a training-free framework in which each skill is a structured multi-file package containing retrieval metadata, executable plans, backup localization, failure-recovery rules, accessibility utilities, and failure cases. EvoSkill-GUI operates through a \textbf{\emph{reflect-revise-reuse}} loop: the executor performs instant in-rollout revisions, an isolated critic diagnoses failed trajectories under strict information isolation, and the executor edits specific skill files through a restricted tool interface. Across MobileWorld, AndroidWorld, and OSWorld, three mainstream GUI benchmarks spanning mobile and desktop platforms, EvoSkill-GUI consistently improves multiple base models without any training, with maximum gains of $+16.2\%$, $+6.0\%$, and $+10.5\%$ respectively, and evolved skill libraries continue to benefit related tasks rather than being rebuilt from scratch. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/EvoSkill-GUI.

</details>


### [300] [Regularized Least Squares Training of Quadratic Neural Networks with Applications to System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17654)
*Luis Rodrigues,Zachary Yetman Van Egmond,Mohammad R. Amiri Fard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带正则化的最小二乘法来训练二次神经网络，可给出训练优化问题解的下界，并在正则化系数为零时得到闭式最优解及其灵敏度表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统训练方法（如反向传播）属于迭代数值算法，计算耗时长且易陷入局部极小值。因此需要一种能给出解析（闭式）表达、降低计算时间并避免局部最优的二次神经网络训练方法。

Method: 提出一种带正则化的最小二乘训练方法：利用最小二乘框架推导二次神经网络权重的闭式近似解及其对数据误差的灵敏度解析表达式，并将求解下界的优化问题与核范数最小化建立联系；当正则化系数为零时，下界紧且近似解即为最优解。

Result: 该方法在正则化系数为正时给出训练优化问题解的下界；正则化系数为零时下界紧且闭式解为最优解，权重与灵敏度均有解析表达式，显著缩短计算时间并避免局部极小值。在非线性系统辨识示例中成功应用，所得下界与最优值进行了比较验证。

Conclusion: 所提最小二乘训练方法能够为二次神经网络提供解析权重、解析灵敏度，并建立与核范数最小化的联系，在非线性系统辨识任务中表现出有效性，相比迭代法计算更快且可避免局部极小值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a least squares approach for the training of quadratic neural networks with regularization. The proposed methodology yields a lower bound on the solution of the training optimization problem for the case where the regularization coefficient is positive. Moreover, it yields closed-form expressions for the approximate solution and its sensitivity The lower bound is tight and the approximate solution is the optimal solution when the regularization coefficient is zero. Having a closed-form expression for the weights reduces considerably the computational time when compared with iterative numerical methods such as backpropagation that can get stuck in local minima. The proposed approach has three main contributions, namely, (i) it yields an analytical expression for the weights, (ii) an analytical expression for the sensitivity of the weights to errors in the data is also provided, (iii) it establishes a connection between the optimization to compute a lower bound and nuclear norm minimization. The proposed least squares training is successfully applied to a nonlinear system identification example where the proposed lower bound is compared with the optimal value.

</details>


### [301] [TabPFN-3.5: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17895)
*Benjamin Jäger,Nick Erickson,Léo Grinsztajn,Felix Birkel,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Kürşat Kaya,Jonas Kübler,Adèle Frankel,Tobias Schröder,Anurag Garg,Jan Hendrik Metzen,David Salinas,Simon Bing,Kristina Collins,Tuana Çelik,Vahid Balazadeh,Lydia Sidhoum,Tomás Pereda,Brendan Roof,Andrej Tschalzev,Siyuan Guo,Philipp Singer,Lennart Purucker,Jake Robertson,Marie Salmon,Philipp Jund,Jerry Chen,Diana Kriuchkova,Arthur Cahu,Eliott Kalfon,Adrian Hayler,Georg Grab,Vitor Monteiro,Lilly Wehrhahn,Dominik Safaric,Clara Cornu,Alan Arazi,Rylee Grace,Simone Alessi,Mihir Manium,Bernhard Schölkopf,Yann LeCun,Madelon Hulsebos,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3.5 是新的旗舰级表格基础模型，在广泛的表格任务上显著超越前代 TabPFN-3 及所有基线，并在多种实际数据场景与任务专用框架中达到最新最优，同时推出 Fast、Plus、Thinking 等加速与增强变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格模型主要针对标准 i.i.d. 预测场景优化，难以覆盖实际数据实践中遇到的非独立同分布（时间/分组切分）、含字符串/文本/图像的表格、高基数类别特征、宽表等复杂情形，且推理速度、多模态能力与推理时计算扩展仍有不足。

Method: 推出新旗舰模型 TabPFN-3.5，在基础模型之上扩展对非 i.i.d. 数据、多模态（字符串、文本、图像）、高基数类别与宽表的支持；并提供三个变体：TabPFN-3.5-Fast（推理提速至 3x）、TabPFN-3.5-Plus（增强文本与日期处理及专有推理优化）、TabPFN-3.5-Thinking（扩展推理时计算，速度提升高达 12x）。

Result: TabPFN-3.5 在 TabArena 标准表格预测上创下新最优，并推广到非 i.i.d.、多模态、高基数类别、宽表等实际场景；在关系数据与时间序列预测的任务专用框架上同样达到最优；Fast 变体在保留大部分准确率的同时推理提速约 3x，Thinking 变体相较前代提速高达 12x。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3.5 及其 Fast、Plus、Thinking 变体共同刷新了表格基础模型的性能上限，并将能力从标准基准拓展到真实世界表格数据与专用任务，兼顾准确率与推理效率。

Abstract: We introduce TabPFN-3.5, our new flagship Tabular Foundation Model. It significantly outperforms its predecessor, TabPFN-3, and all existing baselines across a broad range of tabular problems. TabPFN-3.5 sets a new state of the art on standard tabular prediction in TabArena, and extends it to the data practitioners encounter in practice: non-i.i.d. data with temporal or grouped splits, tables with strings, text and images, high-cardinality categorical features, and wide tables with many features. These gains carry over to our task-specific harnesses: state of the art on relational data and stronger time-series forecasting. For faster inference, our variant TabPFN-3.5-Fast runs up to 3x faster than TabPFN-3 while keeping most of the accuracy gains. In addition, we upgrade TabPFN-3.5-Plus, expanding our multimodal capabilities with advanced text and date handling alongside proprietary inference optimizations. Finally, we release a new version of our Thinking mode, TabPFN-3.5-Thinking, which scales inference-time computation to push the state of the art further. It benefits from our stronger base model and from inference-time improvements that make it up to 12x faster than TabPFN-3-Thinking.

</details>


### [302] [DSD: Learning Diverse and Reusable Motor Skills via Diffusion Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17682)
*Sun Woo Kim,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD)，利用扩散模型通过分数匹配近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，以准确估计边际状态熵，从而发现更多样、可复用的运动技能，并在层级控制与零样本下游任务中取得优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能学习方法通过最大化技能潜变量与状态间的互信息来促进多样性，但边际状态熵在高维控制问题中难以直接估计，之前的近似方法（潜空间近似或粗粒度状态分布估计）不能有效促进状态空间的广泛覆盖，导致所学技能的多样性受限、对下游任务价值降低。

Method: 提出 Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD)：使用扩散模型通过 score matching（分数匹配）近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，从而构造目标函数以更准确地估计和最大化边际状态熵，鼓励技能发现覆盖更广的行为范围。

Result: 实验表明，DSD 在高维人形控制中发现了比先前技能发现方法更广泛的、可复用的运动技能库，涌现出复杂且敏捷的行为；所学技能在层级控制（任务特定高层策略）和零样本控制（从离线轨迹进行潜变量选择）两种下游设置中均可复用，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过扩散模型精确估计熵梯度，DSD 克服了传统方法在边际状态熵估计上的困难，能够发现更广泛多样的可复用技能，并有效支撑下游任务，优于现有的技能发现方法。

Abstract: Humans efficiently learn new tasks by reusing a rich repertoire of motor skills across different goals and contexts. A similar strategy can also be used to enable simulated characters to efficiently perform new tasks by leveraging reusable motor skills. To support a wide range of downstream tasks, the learned repertoire should be diverse, consisting of distinct behaviors as well as spatial and temporal variation within each behavior. A commonly used method for learning diverse skills is by maximizing the mutual information between skill latents and the states produced by a policy. The marginal state entropy promotes broad behavioral coverage, while the conditional entropy encourages consistent behaviors from each latent. However, directly estimating the marginal state entropy is intractable in high-dimensional control problems. Prior methods therefore rely on indirect latent-space approximations or coarse estimators of the state distribution. These approximations may not effectively promote broad coverage of the state space, resulting in skills with limited behavioral diversity and reduced utility for downstream tasks. In this work, we propose Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD), a skill discovery method that uses a diffusion model to approximate the entropy gradient of the policy-induced state distribution through score matching. The resulting objective encourages the discovery of skills that produce a broader range of behaviors for high-dimensional humanoid control. The learned skills are reused in two downstream control settings: hierarchical control with a task-specific high-level policy and zero-shot control through latent selection from offline trajectories. Our experiments show that DSD discovers a broader repertoire of reusable motor skills than prior skill discovery methods, leading to the emergence of complex and agile behaviors that can be reused across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [303] [Symmetry without a manifold: intrinsic dimension on orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17926)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Miloud Bessafi,Frédéric Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准神经缩放指数推导所依赖的数据流形内在维度，在模加法这类对称性问题上根本不成立；对称性破坏后得到的"维度"只是探测分辨率而非真维度，真实的缩放规律是指数形式而非幂律，且其速率由正则化器而非群阶决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准做法将神经缩放指数理解为数据流形内在维度的函数，而模加法这一精确代数可解问题恰好是验证该推导的困难案例：其解集是等距群作用的轨道，标准维度估计量在此退化，需要揭示对称性破坏下缩放规律的真正形式与来源。

Method: 以 Z_p 上的模加法为模型，利用群作用的等距性与传递性分析标准内在维度估计量（基于最近邻距离比值统计量）的退化行为；在尺度 ε 打破对称性后考察估计量随分辨率的变化；进一步在任意等距群作用的有限轨道上证明其一般性，并用隐藏宽度 h 的指数函数 L(h)=L∞+A exp(-c h^α) 拟合损失曲线，与同协议下允许相同下限的幂律作 R² 对比；通过改变权重衰减与群阶，检验指数速率 c 的归属。

Result: 标准维度估计量在传递性对称下为点质量、无定义，两最近邻距离精确相等；打破对称后估计量随 1/ε 变化、无尺度自由平台，报告的是探测分辨率而非维度。损失随隐藏宽度呈指数衰减，R² 在 0.982–0.995，优于同协议幂律的 0.857–0.906；数据充足时指数速率 c 由正则化器主导——权重衰减使 c 变化 47 倍，而群阶仅使其变化 1.10（低于种子间分辨率），且对所有 α∈[0.75,2] 成立。临界宽度随群阶下降而非上升，与按不可约表示分配固定神经元数的容量计数相悖。

Conclusion: 基于内在维度的几何缩放推导在对称性问题上失效，幂律被指数律取代；指数衰减速率由正则化器而非数据群的代数结构决定，从而对"容量—表示"式的缩放理论构成挑战。

Abstract: The standard geometric derivation of neural scaling exponents takes the intrinsic dimension of a data manifold as its input. On modular addition in $\mathbb{Z}_p$ that derivation has no input. The exact algebraic solution is an orbit of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ acting by isometries. Transitivity alone makes the ratio statistic underlying the standard dimension estimator a point mass, so the estimator is undefined, and here the two nearest neighbour distances coincide exactly. Breaking the symmetry at scale $ε$ returns a number, but one that tracks $1/ε$ with no scale free plateau. We show that the failure is general, since on any finite orbit of a group acting by isometries the estimator reports the resolution at which the set is probed rather than a dimension. What replaces the power law is exponential in hidden width, $L(h)=L_\infty+A\exp(-c\,h^α)$, with $R^2$ between 0.982 and 0.995 against 0.857 to 0.906 for a power law admitting the same floor and fitted under the same protocol.
